 
Option Explicit
'AW, KN
Const cstrModuleName As String = "ModSections"

Public Function GetPassDoorWgt() As Single
    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".GetPassDoorWgt()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""

    Dim lstrSQL As String
    Dim lobjRS As ADODB.Recordset
    
    lstrSQL = "select SpringingWeight from Main where PartNum=" & SQLString(varPassDoor.strPN)
    Set lobjRS = CnnBTMS.Execute(lstrSQL, 0, adCmdText)
    If Not lobjRS Is Nothing Then
        If Not (lobjRS.EOF And lobjRS.BOF) Then
            GetPassDoorWgt = DBLValue(lobjRS.Fields("SpringingWeight").Value)
        End If
    End If
    
    Exit Function

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, lstrErrDescr & vbCrLf & Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Function

Public Sub SectionsNumCombinationOversized()

    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".SectionsNumCombinationOversized()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""

    'used to determine the number of 21" and 24" sections
    'based on the Door Height (Public)
    
    Dim N3 As Single, NX As Single, NY As Single
    'Find the number of 3" increments for door height
    If ProdType = "Alumatite" Then
        NX = DrHgt
        NY = NX \ 24
        If (NX Mod 24) > 1 Then
            NY = NY + 1
        End If
        N24 = NY
        N21 = 0
    Else
        N3 = DrHgt / 3
        N24 = 0
        N21 = 0
        NX = N3 / 8
        N24 = N3 \ 8
        NY = NX - N24
        If (NY > 0) Then
            N21 = (1 - NY) / 0.125          'Find the remaining (3")s needed
            If ((N21 * 7) <= N3) Then       'and choose that number for 21" sections
                N24 = (N3 - (N21 * 7)) \ 8  'This will make the combination accurate
            Else                            'Since every (24" section) = ((21" section) + (3"))
                If (ProdType = "RollTite") Then
                    N24 = 0     'If the number of 21" section is higher than door height
                    N21 = 0     'then it's an invalid height
                Else
                    N21 = 1     'Add one extra section and cap the top
                                'or/and bottom section(s)
                End If
            End If
        End If
        If (DrHgt - 24 * N24) < 10 And (DrHgt - 24 * N24) > 0 Then
            N21 = N21 + N24
            N24 = 0
        End If
    End If
    Exit Sub

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, lstrErrDescr & vbCrLf & Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Sub

Public Sub SectionsNumCombination()

    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".SectionsNumCombination()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""
    Dim lstrSQL As String
    Dim lobjSecConfRS As ADODB.Recordset
    Dim llngAffected As Long
    Dim TempHgt As Long
    
    N21 = 0
    N24 = 0
    If DrHgt > 0 Then
        TempHgt = RoundUp(DrHgt)
        lstrSQL = "SELECT * from SectionsConfig where drht=" & Replace(TempHgt, ",", ".")
        Set lobjSecConfRS = CnnBTMS.Execute(lstrSQL, llngAffected, adCmdText)
        If Not lobjSecConfRS Is Nothing Then
            lobjSecConfRS.ActiveConnection = Nothing
            If Not (lobjSecConfRS.BOF And lobjSecConfRS.EOF) Then
                lobjSecConfRS.MoveFirst
                
                N21 = IIf(IsNull(lobjSecConfRS.Fields("21").Value), 0, lobjSecConfRS.Fields("21").Value)
                N24 = IIf(IsNull(lobjSecConfRS.Fields("24").Value), 0, lobjSecConfRS.Fields("24").Value)
            End If
            Set lobjSecConfRS = Nothing
        End If
    End If
    Exit Sub

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Sub

Public Function GetCut(ByVal Btm As Boolean) As Single

    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".GetCut()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""

    Dim lstrSQL As String
    Dim lobjSecConfRS As ADODB.Recordset
    Dim llngAffected As Long
    Dim lsngTempDrHgt As Single
    Dim lsngUpperLimit As Single
    Dim lsngLowerlimit As Single
      
    If MeasU = 0 Then
        lsngUpperLimit = DrHgt + 0.1875
        lsngLowerlimit = DrHgt - 0.1875
        lstrSQL = "SELECT * from SectionsConfig where drht between " & Replace(lsngLowerlimit, ",", ".") & " and " & Replace(lsngUpperLimit, ",", ".")
        Set lobjSecConfRS = CnnBTMS.Execute(lstrSQL, llngAffected, adCmdText)
        If Not lobjSecConfRS Is Nothing Then
            lobjSecConfRS.ActiveConnection = Nothing
            If Not (lobjSecConfRS.BOF And lobjSecConfRS.EOF) Then
                lobjSecConfRS.MoveFirst
                If Btm = True Then
                    GetCut = DBLValue(lobjSecConfRS.Fields("CutBtm").Value)
                Else
                    GetCut = DBLValue(lobjSecConfRS.Fields("CutTop").Value)
                    lsngTempDrHgt = DBLValue(lobjSecConfRS.Fields("DrHt").Value)
'                    If GetCut = 0 Then
'                        GetCut = Abs(lsngTempDrHgt - DrHgt)
'                    Else
'                        GetCut = GetCut + (lsngTempDrHgt - DrHgt)
'                    End If
                End If
            Else    'could not find drht in a specific upper and lower limit
                lstrSQL = "SELECT * from SectionsConfig where drht= " & Replace(RoundUp(DrHgt), ",", ".")
                Set lobjSecConfRS = CnnBTMS.Execute(lstrSQL, llngAffected, adCmdText)
                If Not lobjSecConfRS Is Nothing Then
                    lobjSecConfRS.ActiveConnection = Nothing
                    If Not (lobjSecConfRS.BOF And lobjSecConfRS.EOF) Then
                        lobjSecConfRS.MoveFirst
                        If Btm = True Then
                            GetCut = DBLValue(lobjSecConfRS.Fields("CutBtm").Value)
                        Else
                            GetCut = DBLValue(lobjSecConfRS.Fields("CutTop").Value)
                            lsngTempDrHgt = DBLValue(lobjSecConfRS.Fields("DrHt").Value)
                            If GetCut = 0 Then
                                GetCut = Abs(lsngTempDrHgt - DrHgt)
                            Else
                                GetCut = GetCut + (lsngTempDrHgt - DrHgt)
                            End If
                        End If
                    End If
                End If
            End If
        End If
    Else    'imperial
        lstrSQL = "SELECT * from SectionsConfig where drht= " & Replace(RoundUp(DrHgt), ",", ".")
        Set lobjSecConfRS = CnnBTMS.Execute(lstrSQL, llngAffected, adCmdText)
        If Not lobjSecConfRS Is Nothing Then
            lobjSecConfRS.ActiveConnection = Nothing
            If Not (lobjSecConfRS.BOF And lobjSecConfRS.EOF) Then
                lobjSecConfRS.MoveFirst
                If Btm = True Then
                    GetCut = DBLValue(lobjSecConfRS.Fields("CutBtm").Value)
                Else
                    GetCut = DBLValue(lobjSecConfRS.Fields("CutTop").Value)
                    lsngTempDrHgt = DBLValue(lobjSecConfRS.Fields("DrHt").Value)
                    If GetCut = 0 Then
                        GetCut = Abs(lsngTempDrHgt - DrHgt)
                    Else
                        GetCut = GetCut + (lsngTempDrHgt - DrHgt)
                    End If
                End If
            End If
        End If
    End If
    Set lobjSecConfRS = Nothing
    
'    lstrSQL = "SELECT * from SectionsConfig where drht=" & Replace(RoundUp(DrHgt), ",", ".")
'    Set lobjSecConfRS = CnnBTMS.Execute(lstrSQL, llngAffected, adCmdText)
'    If Not lobjSecConfRS Is Nothing Then
'        lobjSecConfRS.ActiveConnection = Nothing
'        If Not (lobjSecConfRS.BOF And lobjSecConfRS.EOF) Then
'            lobjSecConfRS.MoveFirst
'            If Btm = True Then
'                GetCut = DBLValue(lobjSecConfRS.Fields("CutBtm").Value)
'            Else
'                GetCut = DBLValue(lobjSecConfRS.Fields("CutTop").Value)
'            End If
'        End If
'        Set lobjSecConfRS = Nothing
'    End If

    Exit Function

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Function

Public Function GetCutInstructions() As String

    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".GetCutInstructions()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""

    Dim lstrSQL As String
    Dim lobjSecConfRS As ADODB.Recordset
    Dim llngAffected As Long
    
    lstrSQL = "SELECT * from SectionsConfig where drht=" & Replace(RoundUp(DrHgt), ",", ".")
    Set lobjSecConfRS = CnnBTMS.Execute(lstrSQL, llngAffected, adCmdText)
    If Not lobjSecConfRS Is Nothing Then
        lobjSecConfRS.ActiveConnection = Nothing
        If Not (lobjSecConfRS.BOF And lobjSecConfRS.EOF) Then
            lobjSecConfRS.MoveFirst
            GetCutInstructions = lobjSecConfRS.Fields("CutInstructions").Value
        End If
        Set lobjSecConfRS = Nothing
    End If

    Exit Function

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Function

Public Function GetSectionsWeight(ByVal LiteArea As Single) As Single
    
    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".GetSectionsWeight()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""

    'used to calculate the weight of the Sections per door
    
    Dim lsngSecArea As Single, lsngTempSectHgt  As Single
   
    Dim lsngSecWgt As Single      'weight of section
    Dim SectionWidth As Single    'cut section to fit endcaps.
    Dim i As Integer              'counter
    Dim DrInsulator As Integer
    Dim lsngCutFactor As Single
    Dim lsngLiteFactor As Single
    Dim lsngCutAmount As Single
    DrInsulator = Insulator
    
    If ProdType = "Alumatite" And DrType <> "A175T" Then        'door weight is not calculated for Alumatite
        GetSectionsWeight = 0
        Exit Function
    End If
    
    SectionWidth = GetSectionWidth(DrWidth)
    
    For i = 0 To UBound(varDoorData.arrSections())
        If Not (varDoorData.arrSections(i).intNLites > 0 And (varDoorData.LiteType > 7000 Or varDoorData.LiteType = 7)) Then 'full view weight is calc. seperately.
            If i = 0 Then 'for top section
                If DrType = "T45" Then
                    lsngCutFactor = 2.75    'T45: the amount required to be cut to delete all of the reinforcing on top has increased.
                Else
                    lsngCutFactor = 2.375   'at 2.375", reinforcement strip ends, we need to know
                                            'if they cut beyond the reinforcement
                                            'and use different weight constants.
                End If
                lsngCutAmount = varDoorData.CutTop
            End If
            
            If i = UBound(varDoorData.arrSections()) Then 'for bottom section
                If DrType = "T45" Then
                     lsngCutFactor = 2      'T45: the amount required to be cut to delete all of the reinforcing on bottom has increased.
                Else
                    lsngCutFactor = 1.625
                End If
                lsngCutAmount = varDoorData.CutBtm
            End If
            
            If varDoorData.arrSections(i).intHeight = 21 Then
                lsngSecArea = (SectionWidth * 21) / 144 'get section area
            Else
                lsngSecArea = (SectionWidth * 24) / 144
            End If
            
            Select Case DrType
            Case "SR24", "SR16", "SR1626"   'Rolltite doors
                If varDoorData.arrSections(i).intNLites > 0 Then 'take away lite area
                    lsngSecArea = lsngSecArea - LiteArea
                End If
                If (DrType = "SR16") Or (DrType = "SR1626") Then '16 gauge
                    If (DrInsulator = 0) Then         'No Insulation
                        lsngSecWgt = lsngSecArea * 3.5
                    ElseIf (DrInsulator = 1) Then     'AF-530
                        lsngSecWgt = lsngSecArea * 4.65
                    ElseIf (DrInsulator = 2) Then     'Styrofoam
                        lsngSecWgt = lsngSecArea * 4.5
                    ElseIf (DrInsulator = 3) Then     'Styroplast
                        lsngSecWgt = lsngSecArea * 3.9
                    End If
                ElseIf (DrType = "SR20") Then
                    If (DrInsulator = 0) Then         'No Insulation
                        lsngSecWgt = lsngSecArea * 2.6
                    ElseIf (DrInsulator = 1) Then     'AF-530
                        lsngSecWgt = lsngSecArea * 3.32
                    ElseIf (DrInsulator = 2) Then     'Styrofoam
                        lsngSecWgt = lsngSecArea * 3.169
                    ElseIf (DrInsulator = 3) Then     'Styroplast
                        lsngSecWgt = lsngSecArea * 3
                    End If
                ElseIf (DrType = "SR24") Then
                    If (DrInsulator = 0) Then         'No Insulation
                        lsngSecWgt = lsngSecArea * 1.8
                    ElseIf (DrInsulator = 1) Then     'AF-530
                        lsngSecWgt = lsngSecArea * 2.65
                    ElseIf (DrInsulator = 2) Then     'Styrofoam
                        lsngSecWgt = lsngSecArea * 2.499
                    ElseIf (DrInsulator = 3) Then     'Styroplast
                        lsngSecWgt = lsngSecArea * 2.2
                    End If
                End If
                If BSheet = 4 Then 'ga16
                    lsngSecWgt = lsngSecWgt + (lsngSecArea * 1.744)
                ElseIf BSheet = 3 Then 'ga20
                    lsngSecWgt = lsngSecWgt + (lsngSecArea * 0.744)
                ElseIf BSheet = 2 Then 'ga24
                    lsngSecWgt = lsngSecWgt + (lsngSecArea * 0.124)
                End If
            Case "T150", "T150 Multi-Rib"
            
                lsngSecWgt = (lsngSecArea * IIf(varDoorData.arrSections(i).intHeight = 21, 2.01, 1.94))
                
                If varDoorData.CutTop + varDoorData.CutBtm > 0 And (i = 0 Or i = UBound(varDoorData.arrSections())) Then
                    lsngSecWgt = lsngSecWgt - (SectionWidth * 0.01109) 'we cut off the metal strip on the very tip of the section
                    lsngSecWgt = lsngSecWgt - (SectionWidth * lsngCutFactor * 0.022221) 'we cut off some of the reinforcement
                    If lsngCutAmount > lsngCutFactor Then 'condition if the cut is greater than the height of the reinforcement
                        lsngSecWgt = lsngSecWgt - (SectionWidth * (lsngCutAmount - lsngCutFactor) * 0.010815) 'we cut beyond the reinforcement
                    End If
                End If
                
                If varDoorData.arrSections(i).intNLites > 0 Then 'reduce the weight further by lite factor
    '                Select Case varDoorData.LiteType
    '                    Case 1:   lsngLiteFactor = 3.115
    '                    Case 2:   lsngLiteFactor = 1.782
    '                    Case 3:   lsngLiteFactor = 5.883
    '                    Case 5:   lsngLiteFactor = 3.266
    '                End Select
                    lsngLiteFactor = 0.01081
                    lsngSecWgt = lsngSecWgt - (LiteArea * 144 * lsngLiteFactor * varDoorData.arrSections(i).intNLites)
                End If
                
            Case "T175", "A175T", "T175 Multi-Rib", "T45"
                If DrType = "T45" Then
                    lsngSecWgt = (lsngSecArea * IIf(varDoorData.arrSections(i).intHeight = 21, 2.28, 2.23))
                    'Cut top and bottom is almost the same as T175
                Else
                    lsngSecWgt = (lsngSecArea * IIf(varDoorData.arrSections(i).intHeight = 21, 2.25, 2.2))
                End If
                
                If varDoorData.CutTop + varDoorData.CutBtm > 0 And (i = 0 Or i = UBound(varDoorData.arrSections())) Then
                    lsngSecWgt = lsngSecWgt - (SectionWidth * 0.013299)
                    lsngSecWgt = lsngSecWgt - (SectionWidth * lsngCutFactor * 0.024302)
                    If lsngCutAmount > lsngCutFactor Then
                        lsngSecWgt = lsngSecWgt - (SectionWidth * (lsngCutAmount - lsngCutFactor) * 0.012896)
                    End If
                End If
                        
                If varDoorData.arrSections(i).intNLites > 0 Then 'reduce the weight further by lite factor
    '                Select Case varDoorData.LiteType
    '                    Case 1:   lsngLiteFactor = 3.714
    '                    Case 2:   lsngLiteFactor = 2.125
    '                    Case 3:   lsngLiteFactor = 7.016
    '                    Case 5:   lsngLiteFactor = 3.895
                    lsngLiteFactor = 0.0129
    
    '                End Select
                    lsngSecWgt = lsngSecWgt - (LiteArea * 144 * lsngLiteFactor * varDoorData.arrSections(i).intNLites)
                End If
            
            Case "T175A"
                
                lsngSecWgt = (lsngSecArea * IIf(varDoorData.arrSections(i).intHeight = 21, 1.33, 1.29))
            
                If varDoorData.CutTop + varDoorData.CutBtm > 0 And (i = 0 Or i = UBound(varDoorData.arrSections())) Then
                    lsngSecWgt = lsngSecWgt - (SectionWidth * 0.005278)
                    lsngSecWgt = lsngSecWgt - (SectionWidth * lsngCutFactor * 0.020999)
                    If lsngCutAmount > lsngCutFactor Then
                        lsngSecWgt = lsngSecWgt - (SectionWidth * (lsngCutAmount - lsngCutFactor) * 0.009593)
                    End If
                End If
                        
                If varDoorData.arrSections(i).intNLites > 0 Then 'reduce the weight further by lite factor
    '                Select Case varDoorData.LiteType
    '                    Case 1:   lsngLiteFactor = 2.763
    '                    Case 2:   lsngLiteFactor = 1.581
    '                    Case 3:   lsngLiteFactor = 5.219
    '                    Case 5:   lsngLiteFactor = 2.897
    '                End Select
                    lsngLiteFactor = 0.00959
    
                    lsngSecWgt = lsngSecWgt - (LiteArea * 144 * lsngLiteFactor * varDoorData.arrSections(i).intNLites)
                End If
                
            Case "T175-20"
                
                lsngSecWgt = (lsngSecArea * IIf(varDoorData.arrSections(i).intHeight = 21, 2.96, 2.88)) 'old value for 21" (2.73 lb/sq ft) old value for 24" (2.65 lb/sq ft)
            
                If varDoorData.CutTop + varDoorData.CutBtm > 0 And (i = 0 Or i = UBound(varDoorData.arrSections())) Then
                    lsngSecWgt = lsngSecWgt - (SectionWidth * 0.016032)
                    lsngSecWgt = lsngSecWgt - (SectionWidth * lsngCutFactor * 0.028278)
                    If lsngCutAmount > lsngCutFactor Then
                        lsngSecWgt = lsngSecWgt - (SectionWidth * (lsngCutAmount - lsngCutFactor) * 0.016872)
                    End If
                End If
                                
                If varDoorData.arrSections(i).intNLites > 0 Then 'reduce the weight further by lite factor
    '                Select Case varDoorData.LiteType
    '                    Case 1:   lsngLiteFactor = 4.859
    '                    Case 2:   lsngLiteFactor = 2.78
    '                    Case 3:   lsngLiteFactor = 9.179
    '                    Case 5:   lsngLiteFactor = 5.095
    '                End Select
                    lsngLiteFactor = 0.01687
    
                    lsngSecWgt = lsngSecWgt - (LiteArea * 144 * lsngLiteFactor * varDoorData.arrSections(i).intNLites)
                End If
    
            Case "ADV200", "ADV200 Multi-Rib"
                
                lsngSecWgt = (lsngSecArea * IIf(varDoorData.arrSections(i).intHeight = 21, 2.07, 2.04))
            
                If varDoorData.CutTop + varDoorData.CutBtm > 0 And (i = 0 Or i = UBound(varDoorData.arrSections())) Then
                    lsngSecWgt = lsngSecWgt - (SectionWidth * 0.013036)
                    lsngSecWgt = lsngSecWgt - (SectionWidth * lsngCutFactor * 0.02285)
                    If lsngCutAmount > lsngCutFactor Then
                        lsngSecWgt = lsngSecWgt - (SectionWidth * (lsngCutAmount - lsngCutFactor) * 0.011444)
                    End If
                End If
                                
                If varDoorData.arrSections(i).intNLites > 0 Then 'reduce the weight further by lite factor
    '                Select Case varDoorData.LiteType
    '                    Case 1:   lsngLiteFactor = 3.296
    '                    Case 2:   lsngLiteFactor = 1.886
    '                    Case 3:   lsngLiteFactor = 6.225
    '                    Case 5:   lsngLiteFactor = 3.456
    '                End Select
                    lsngLiteFactor = 0.01144
    
                    lsngSecWgt = lsngSecWgt - (LiteArea * 144 * lsngLiteFactor * varDoorData.arrSections(i).intNLites)
                End If
    
    
    
                Case "T300", "T300 Multi-Rib"   'T300 - 2.0.22 fake.
                
                lsngSecWgt = (lsngSecArea * IIf(varDoorData.arrSections(i).intHeight = 21, 2.07, 2.04))
            
                If varDoorData.CutTop + varDoorData.CutBtm > 0 And (i = 0 Or i = UBound(varDoorData.arrSections())) Then
                    lsngSecWgt = lsngSecWgt - (SectionWidth * 0.013036)
                    lsngSecWgt = lsngSecWgt - (SectionWidth * lsngCutFactor * 0.02285)
                    If lsngCutAmount > lsngCutFactor Then
                        lsngSecWgt = lsngSecWgt - (SectionWidth * (lsngCutAmount - lsngCutFactor) * 0.011444)
                    End If
                End If
                                
                If varDoorData.arrSections(i).intNLites > 0 Then 'reduce the weight further by lite factor
    '                Select Case varDoorData.LiteType
    '                    Case 1:   lsngLiteFactor = 3.296
    '                    Case 2:   lsngLiteFactor = 1.886
    '                    Case 3:   lsngLiteFactor = 6.225
    '                    Case 5:   lsngLiteFactor = 3.456
    '                End Select
                    lsngLiteFactor = 0.01144
    
                    lsngSecWgt = lsngSecWgt - (LiteArea * 144 * lsngLiteFactor * varDoorData.arrSections(i).intNLites)
                End If

    
            Case "T200", "T200 Multi-Rib"
                
                lsngSecWgt = (lsngSecArea * IIf(varDoorData.arrSections(i).intHeight = 21, 2.45, 2.42))
            
                If varDoorData.CutTop + varDoorData.CutBtm > 0 And (i = 0 Or i = UBound(varDoorData.arrSections())) Then
                    lsngSecWgt = lsngSecWgt - (SectionWidth * 0.015436)
                    lsngSecWgt = lsngSecWgt - (SectionWidth * lsngCutFactor * 0.02541)
                    If lsngCutAmount > lsngCutFactor Then
                        lsngSecWgt = lsngSecWgt - (SectionWidth * (lsngCutAmount - lsngCutFactor) * 0.014004)
                    End If
                End If
                                
                If varDoorData.arrSections(i).intNLites > 0 Then 'reduce the weight further by lite factor
    '                Select Case varDoorData.LiteType
    '                    Case 1:   lsngLiteFactor = 4.033
    '                    Case 2:   lsngLiteFactor = 2.307
    '                    Case 3:   lsngLiteFactor = 7.618
    '                    Case 5:   lsngLiteFactor = 4.229
    '                End Select
                    lsngLiteFactor = 0.014
    
                    lsngSecWgt = lsngSecWgt - (LiteArea * 144 * lsngLiteFactor * varDoorData.arrSections(i).intNLites)
                End If
    
            Case "T200A", "T200A Multi-Rib"
            
                lsngSecWgt = (lsngSecArea * IIf(varDoorData.arrSections(i).intHeight = 21, 1.467, 1.387))
            
                If varDoorData.CutTop + varDoorData.CutBtm > 0 And (i = 0 Or i = UBound(varDoorData.arrSections())) Then
                    lsngSecWgt = lsngSecWgt - (SectionWidth * 0.005966)
                    lsngSecWgt = lsngSecWgt - (SectionWidth * lsngCutFactor * 0.021045)
                    If lsngCutAmount > lsngCutFactor Then
                        lsngSecWgt = lsngSecWgt - (SectionWidth * (lsngCutAmount - lsngCutFactor) * 0.009639)
                    End If
                End If
                                    
                If varDoorData.arrSections(i).intNLites > 0 Then 'reduce the weight further by lite factor
    '                Select Case varDoorData.LiteType
    '                    Case 1:   lsngLiteFactor = 2.776
    '                    Case 2:   lsngLiteFactor = 1.588
    '                    Case 3:   lsngLiteFactor = 5.244
    '                    Case 5:   lsngLiteFactor = 2.911
    '                End Select
                    lsngLiteFactor = 0.00964
    
                    lsngSecWgt = lsngSecWgt - (LiteArea * 144 * lsngLiteFactor * varDoorData.arrSections(i).intNLites)
                End If
    
            Case "T140", "T138", "T35"
                If DrType = "T140" Or DrType = "T138" Then
                    lsngSecWgt = (lsngSecArea * IIf(varDoorData.arrSections(i).intHeight = 21, 2.003, 1.952))
                Else
                    lsngSecWgt = (lsngSecArea * IIf(varDoorData.arrSections(i).intHeight = 21, 1.6623, 1.8483))
                End If
                
                If varDoorData.CutTop + varDoorData.CutBtm > 0 And (i = 0 Or i = UBound(varDoorData.arrSections())) Then
                    lsngSecWgt = lsngSecWgt - (SectionWidth * 0.0112)
                    lsngSecWgt = lsngSecWgt - (SectionWidth * lsngCutFactor * 0.02205)
                    If lsngCutAmount > lsngCutFactor Then
                        lsngSecWgt = lsngSecWgt - (SectionWidth * (lsngCutAmount - lsngCutFactor) * 0.01064)
                    End If
                End If
                                    
                If varDoorData.arrSections(i).intNLites > 0 Then 'reduce the weight further by lite factor
    '                Select Case varDoorData.LiteType
    '                    Case 1:   lsngLiteFactor = 3.065
    '                    Case 2:   lsngLiteFactor = 1.754
    '                    Case 3:   lsngLiteFactor = 5.79
    '                    Case 5:   lsngLiteFactor = 3.214
    '                End Select
                    lsngLiteFactor = 0.01064
    
                    lsngSecWgt = lsngSecWgt - (LiteArea * 144 * lsngLiteFactor * varDoorData.arrSections(i).intNLites)
                End If
                
            Case "T165"
            
                lsngSecWgt = (lsngSecArea * IIf(varDoorData.arrSections(i).intHeight = 21, 2.09, 1.989))
                
                If varDoorData.CutTop + varDoorData.CutBtm > 0 And (i = 0 Or i = UBound(varDoorData.arrSections())) Then
                    lsngSecWgt = lsngSecWgt - (SectionWidth * 0.01225)
                    lsngSecWgt = lsngSecWgt - (SectionWidth * lsngCutFactor * 0.02245)
                    If lsngCutAmount > lsngCutFactor Then
                        lsngSecWgt = lsngSecWgt - (SectionWidth * (lsngCutAmount - lsngCutFactor) * 0.01104)
                    End If
                End If
                
                If varDoorData.arrSections(i).intNLites > 0 Then 'reduce the weight further by lite factor
    '                Select Case varDoorData.LiteType
    '                    Case 1:   lsngLiteFactor = 3.18
    '                    Case 2:   lsngLiteFactor = 1.819
    '                    Case 3:   lsngLiteFactor = 6.006
    '                    Case 5:   lsngLiteFactor = 3.334
    '                End Select
                    lsngLiteFactor = 0.01104
    
                    lsngSecWgt = lsngSecWgt - (LiteArea * 144 * lsngLiteFactor * varDoorData.arrSections(i).intNLites)
                End If
            
            Case Else
                
                lsngSecWgt = 0
            
            End Select
            
            varDoorData.arrSections(i).sngWgt = lsngSecWgt
            GetSectionsWeight = GetSectionsWeight + lsngSecWgt
        End If
    Next
    
    Exit Function

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Function

Public Function ConfigureSectionPN(ByVal Sect24 As Boolean) As String
    
    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".ConfigureSectionPN()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""

    Dim PNum As String, T As Integer
    
    Select Case DrType   'Product code
    Case "T35"
        PNum = "135"
    Case "T138"
        PNum = "140"
    Case "T140"
        PNum = "375"
    Case "T165"
        PNum = "625"
    Case "T150", "T150 Multi-Rib"
        If DrType = "T150" Then
            PNum = "150"
        ElseIf DrType = "T150 Multi-Rib" Then
            PNum = "190"
        End If
    Case "T175", "A175T", "T175 Multi-Rib"
        If DrType = "T175" Or DrType = "A175T" Then
            PNum = "175"
        ElseIf DrType = "T175 Multi-Rib" Then
            PNum = "195"
        End If
    Case "T45"
        PNum = "145"
    Case "T175A"
        PNum = "175"
    Case "T175-20"
        PNum = "172"
    Case "T200"
        PNum = "200"
        
    Case "T200 Multi-Rib"
        PNum = "290"
        
    Case "ADV200"
        PNum = "20A"
        
    Case "T300"
         PNum = "330"
         
    Case "T200A"
        PNum = "320"
    Case "T200A Multi-Rib"
        PNum = "390"
    
    Case "ADV200 Multi-Rib"
        PNum = "29A"
    Case Else
        Exit Function
    End Select
    
    If (Len(PNum) > 0) Then     'Add Colour code
        If (DrType = "T200") Or (DrType = "T200 Multi-Rib") Or (DrType = "ADV200") Or (DrType = "ADV200 Multi-Rib") _
            Or (DrType = "T200A") Or (DrType = "T200A Multi-Rib") Or (DrType = "T175-20") Or (DrType = "T175A") Or (DrType = "T300") Or (DrType = "T300 Multi-Rib") Then     'T300
            PNum = PNum & "1-"  'Only WHITE is available
        Else
            If (varDoorData.intFaceColour = 2) Then
                PNum = PNum & "2-"  'Brown
            ElseIf (varDoorData.intFaceColour = 3) Then
                PNum = PNum & "4-"  'Silver
            Else    'Default colour is WHITE
                PNum = PNum & "1-"
            End If
        End If
    End If
    
    If (Len(PNum) > 0) Then     'Add Section code
        
        If Sect24 Then
            PNum = PNum & "4"
        Else
            PNum = PNum & "1"
        End If
    End If
    
    PNum = PNum & "000"
    
    ConfigureSectionPN = PNum   'return Part Number as string

    Exit Function

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Function

Public Function GetMRSBWeight(ByVal NumSwitches As Integer) As Single
    
    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".GetMRSBWeight()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""

    'function meant to calculate the Mechanical Reverse Safety Bar weight
    'number of switches: 1 per first 14' of width

    GetMRSBWeight = ((DrWidth / 12) * (2.85)) + ((NumSwitches * 0.67)) + ((DrWidth / 12) * (0.62))

    Exit Function

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Function

Public Function GetMaxLitesPerSect() As Integer

    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".GetMaxLitesPerSect()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""

    Dim M As Integer
    Select Case DrWidth
    Case Is <= 0
        M = 0
    Case Is <= 110
        M = 2
    Case Is <= 146
        M = 3
    Case Is <= 194
        M = 4
    Case Is <= 230
        M = 5
    Case Is <= 290
        M = 6
    Case Is <= 338
        M = 7
    Case Is <= 386
        M = 8
    Case Is <= 434
        M = 9
    Case Else
        M = 10
    End Select
    GetMaxLitesPerSect = M

    Exit Function

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Function

Public Function GetInsideHinges() As Single

    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".GetInsideHinges()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""

    'separately get hinges for sections with trusses and without.

''''''''''Change in .NET''''''''''''''''
'    Dim PN(2) As String, W As Single
    Dim PN(1) As String, W(1) As Single
    Dim TotalW As Single
''''''''''END Change in .NET
    Dim QTruss As Integer
    Dim HTruss As Integer       'number of truss hinges
    Dim HNoTruss As Integer     'number of no truss hinges
    Dim NoHPerSec As Integer    'number of hinges per section
    Dim NHPerDoor As Integer    'number of inside hinges per door
    Dim i As Integer
    Dim HLoc() As Single         'location of first roller bracket
    Dim HCtr As Single          'distance btwn inside hinge centers
    Dim lstrLocations As String 'position of inside hinges saved in the part notes, will display below the part in pick list.
    HTruss = 0
    HNoTruss = 0
    
    For i = 0 To UBound(varDoorData.arrSections) 'get number of sections with truss
        If varDoorData.arrSections(i).intNTruss > 0 Then QTruss = QTruss + 1
    Next
    
    NoHPerSec = GetNumInsideHingesPerWidth 'number of rows
    ReDim HLoc(NoHPerSec)
    
    If QTruss > 0 Then
        HTruss = QTruss - 1 'QTruss includes top section and inside hinges don't go on top
    End If
    
    HNoTruss = (NoHPerSec * (N21 + N24 - 1 - HTruss))      'get number of hinges for all no truss sections
    If HNoTruss < 0 Then HNoTruss = 0
    HTruss = NoHPerSec * HTruss                          'number of hinges for all truss sections
    
    'for pass door add 12
    If varPassDoor.strPN <> "" Then
        HNoTruss = NoTruss + 12
    End If
    
    If (ProdType = "Thermatite") Or (ProdType = "Thermalume") Or (ProdType = "Replacement") Then
        If HTruss = 0 Then             'if no truss
            PN(0) = "9-2317"

''''''''''Change in .NET''''''''''''''''
'            W = 0.23
            W(0) = 0.23
''''''''''END Change in .NET
        Else        'with truss
            If HNoTruss = 0 Then
                PN(1) = "9-2318"

''''''''''Change in .NET''''''''''''''''
'                W = 0.32
                W(1) = 0.32

''''''''''END Change in .NET
            Else    'combination
                PN(0) = "9-2317"
                PN(1) = "9-2318"

''''''''''Change in .NET''''''''''''''''
'                W = 0.55
                W(0) = 0.23
                W(1) = 0.32
''''''''''END Change in .NET
            End If
        End If
    ElseIf (ProdType = "Alumatite") Then
        PN(0) = "9-2317"

''''''''''Change in .NET''''''''''''''''
'        W = 0.23
        W(0) = 0.23

''''''''''END Change in .NET
    ElseIf (ProdType = "Rolltite") Then
        If HTruss = 0 Then              'if no truss
            PN(0) = "9-2211"

''''''''''Change in .NET''''''''''''''''
'            W = 0.44
            W(0) = 0.44
''''''''''END Change in .NET
        Else                        'with truss
            If HNoTruss = 0 Then
                PN(1) = "425-0218"         'for Steel doors, standard HW is used.

''''''''''Change in .NET''''''''''''''''
'                W = 0.44
                W(1) = 0.44

''''''''''END Change in .NET
            Else
                PN(0) = "9-2211"
                PN(1) = "425-0218"

''''''''''Change in .NET''''''''''''''''
'                W = 0.44 * 2
                W(0) = 0.44
                W(1) = 0.44
''''''''''END Change in .NET
            End If
        End If
    End If
    
    If varDoorData.DblEndHinges = False Then
        HCtr = (DrWidth - 3.125) / (NoHPerSec + 1)
        HLoc(0) = 1.5625
    Else
        HCtr = (DrWidth - 9.375) / (NoHPerSec + 1)
        HLoc(0) = 4.6875
    End If
    
    If MeasU = 0 Then
        ClearConObj
        aCon.Inch = HCtr
        aCon.mmcalc
        HCtr = aCon.mm
        ClearConObj
        aCon.Inch = HLoc(0)
        aCon.mmcalc
        HLoc(0) = aCon.mm
    End If
    
    If ProdType = "Thermatite" Or ProdType = "Thermalume" Then
        For i = 1 To NoHPerSec
            HLoc(i) = HLoc(i - 1) + HCtr
            If i > 1 Then lstrLocations = lstrLocations & ", "
            If MeasU = 1 Then
                If Fix(HLoc(i)) = HLoc(i) Then 'a whole number.
                    lstrLocations = lstrLocations & HLoc(i) & Chr(34)
                Else
                    Dim Min As Single
                    Dim Max As Single
                    Dim Dec As Single
                    'need to get the lower and higher fraction, compare which is closer to the decimal and round up or down accordingly.
                    Max = RoundFractionUp(HLoc(i) - Fix(HLoc(i)))
                    Min = RoundFractionDown(HLoc(i) - Fix(HLoc(i)))
                    Dec = IIf(Max - (HLoc(i) - Fix(HLoc(i))) > (HLoc(i) - Fix(HLoc(i))) - Min, Min, Max)
                    If Fix(Dec) = Dec Then
                        lstrLocations = lstrLocations & Fix(HLoc(i)) & Chr(34)
                    Else
                        lstrLocations = lstrLocations & Fix(HLoc(i)) & "-" & GetFraction(Dec) & Chr(34)
                    End If
                End If
            Else
                lstrLocations = lstrLocations & Round(HLoc(i), 0)
            End If
        Next
    End If
    
    If varDoorData.TrussSchedule > 0 And NewLinearHW = True Then
        lstrLocations = "ICB (425-0357) and center hinge centers located at: " & lstrLocations & " from section edge"
    Else
''''''''''Change in .NET''''''''''''''''
'        lstrLocations = "Hinge centers located at: " & lstrLocations & " from section edge"
        lstrLocations = IIf(lstrLocations <> "", "Hinge centers located at: " & lstrLocations & " from section edge", "")

''''''''''END Change in .NET
    End If
    
    If HNoTruss > 0 Then
        Call FillSummaryGrid(PN(0), HNoTruss, "Sections", GetSubCategory(PN(0)), 0, lstrLocations)
    End If
    If HTruss > 0 Then
        Call FillSummaryGrid(PN(1), HTruss, "Sections", GetSubCategory(PN(1)), 0, IIf(HNoTruss = 0, lstrLocations, ""))
    End If
    
    NHPerDoor = HNoTruss + HTruss

''''''''''Change in .NET''''''''''''''''
'    W = (HNoTruss + HTruss) * W
    TotalW = (HNoTruss * W(0) + HTruss * W(1))

''''''''''END Change in .NET
    
    If varDoorData.TrussSchedule > 0 And NewLinearHW = True Then 'Intermediate Cross Brace
        Call FillSummaryGrid("425-0357", NoHPerSec, "Sections", GetSubCategory("425-0357"), 0)
        TotalW = TotalW + (0.15 * NoHPerSec)
    End If
    
    GetInsideHinges = TotalW

    Exit Function

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Function

Public Function GetLiteWeight_andPN() As Single

    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".GetLiteWeight_andPN()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""

    '   This function calculate weight of normal lites saves in global, returns Part Number.
    
    Dim PN As String, P As Single, i As Integer, lintNLites As Integer
    Dim W As Single
    P = 0
    
    If (varDoorData.LiteType >= 1) Then
        For i = 0 To UBound(varDoorData.arrSections)
            lintNLites = varDoorData.arrSections(i).intNLites + lintNLites
        Next i
    End If
    
    Select Case varDoorData.LiteType
    Case 1  'Type E thermal glass
        If (CECompliant = 1 And DrType = "T35") Then
            If varDoorData.LiteColour = 1 Then  'white
                Select Case varDoorData.LiteMaterial
                    Case 1                              'acrylic
                        P = 5.308
                        PN = "326-093A"
                    Case 2                              'tempered
                        P = 7.543
                        PN = "326-093T"
                End Select
                
            ElseIf varDoorData.LiteColour = 2 Then 'brown
                Select Case varDoorData.LiteMaterial
                    Case 1                              'acrylic
                        P = 5.308
                        PN = "326-091A"
                    Case 2                              'tempered
                        P = 7.543
                        PN = "326-091T"
                End Select
            Else 'black
                Select Case varDoorData.LiteMaterial
                    Case 1                              'acrylic
                        P = 5.308
                        PN = "326-089A"
                    Case 2                              'tempered
                        P = 7.543
                        PN = "326-089T"
                End Select
            End If

        ElseIf DrType = "T140" Or DrType = "T138" Or (CECompliant = 0 And DrType = "T35") Then
            P = 7.543
            If varDoorData.LiteColour = 1 Then 'white
                PN = "326-093"
            ElseIf varDoorData.LiteColour = 2 Then 'brown
                PN = "326-091"
            Else
                PN = "326-089"                      'black
            End If
        
        ElseIf DrType = "T165" Then
            P = 7.949
            If varDoorData.LiteColour = 1 Then
                PN = "326-094"
            ElseIf varDoorData.LiteColour = 2 Then
                PN = "326-092"
            Else
                PN = "326-090"
            End If
                
        ElseIf (DrType = "T150") Or (DrType = "T150 Multi-Rib") Then
            P = 7.746
            If varDoorData.LiteColour = 1 Then
                PN = "326-038"
            ElseIf varDoorData.LiteColour = 2 Then
                PN = "326-045"
            Else
                PN = "326-036"
            End If
        ElseIf (DrType = "T175") Or _
                (DrType = "T175-20") Or _
                (DrType = "T175A") Or _
                (DrType = "T175 Multi-Rib") Or _
                (CECompliant = 0 And DrType = "T45") Then
                
'            P = 8.152
'            If varDoorData.LiteColour = 1 Then
'                PN = "326-037"
'            ElseIf varDoorData.LiteColour = 2 Then
'                PN = "326-046"
'            Else
'                PN = "326-035"
'            End If
            
            If varDoorData.LiteColour = 1 Then  'white
                Select Case varDoorData.LiteMaterial
                    Case 1                              'acrylic
                        P = 5.917
                        PN = "326-037A"
                    Case 2                              'tempered
                        P = 8.152
                        PN = "326-037T"
                    Case 3                              'glass
                        P = 8.152
                        PN = "326-037"
                End Select
            ElseIf varDoorData.LiteColour = 2 Then 'brown
                Select Case varDoorData.LiteMaterial
                    Case 1                              'acrylic
                        P = 5.917
                        PN = "326-046A"
                    Case 2
                        P = 8.152
                        PN = "326-046T"                 'tempered
                    Case 3
                        P = 8.152
                        PN = "326-046"                  'glass
                End Select
            ElseIf varDoorData.LiteColour = 3 Then 'black
                Select Case varDoorData.LiteMaterial
                    Case 1                              'acrylic
                        P = 5.917
                        PN = "326-035A"
                    Case 2
                        P = 8.152
                        PN = "326-035T"                 'tempered
                    Case 3
                        P = 8.152
                        PN = "326-035"                  'glass
                End Select
            End If
                
        ElseIf (CECompliant = 1 And DrType = "T45") Then    '(DrType = "T45")
            If varDoorData.LiteColour = 1 Then  'white
                Select Case varDoorData.LiteMaterial
                    Case 1                              'acrylic
                        P = 5.917
                        PN = "326-037A"
                    Case 2
                        P = 8.152
                        PN = "326-037T" 'tempered
                End Select
                
            ElseIf varDoorData.LiteColour = 2 Then 'brown
                Select Case varDoorData.LiteMaterial
                    Case 1                              'acrylic
                        P = 5.917
                        PN = "326-046A"
                    Case 2
                        P = 8.152
                        PN = "326-046T"                 'tempered
                End Select
            
            ElseIf varDoorData.LiteColour = 3 Then 'black
                Select Case varDoorData.LiteMaterial
                    Case 1                              'acrylic
                        P = 5.917
                        PN = "326-035A"
                    Case 2
                        P = 8.152
                        PN = "326-035T"                 'tempered
                End Select
            End If
        
        
        ElseIf (DrType = "T200") Or (DrType = "T200 Multi-Rib") Or (DrType = "ADV200") Or (DrType = "T200A") Or (DrType = "T200A Multi-Rib") Or (DrType = "T200A Multi-Rib") Or (DrType = "ADV200 Multi-Rib") Then
            P = 7.938
            If varDoorData.LiteColour = 1 Then
                PN = "326-039"
            Else
                PN = "326-040"
            End If
        End If
        If lintNLites <> 0 Or PN <> "" Then
            Call FillSummaryGrid(PN, lintNLites, "Sections", GetSubCategory(PN), 0)
        End If
    Case 2 'Type CPL thermal glass
        If (CECompliant = 1 And DrType = "T35") Then
            If varDoorData.LiteColour = 1 Then  'white
                Select Case varDoorData.LiteMaterial
                    Case 1                              'acrylic
                        P = 3.209
                        PN = "326-083A"
                    Case 2                              'tempered
                        P = 4.558
                        PN = "326-083T"
                End Select
                
            ElseIf varDoorData.LiteColour = 2 Then 'brown
                Select Case varDoorData.LiteMaterial
                    Case 1                              'acrylic
                        P = 3.209
                        PN = "326-081A"
                    Case 2                              'tempered
                        P = 4.558
                        PN = "326-081T"
                End Select
            Else 'black
                Select Case varDoorData.LiteMaterial
                    Case 1                              'acrylic
                        P = 3.209
                        PN = "326-079A"
                    Case 2                              'tempered
                        P = 4.558
                        PN = "326-079T"
                End Select
            End If
        
        ElseIf DrType = "T140" Or DrType = "T138" Or (CECompliant = 0 And DrType = "T35") Then
            P = 4.558
            If varDoorData.LiteColour = 1 Then
                PN = "326-083"
            ElseIf varDoorData.LiteColour = 2 Then
                PN = "326-081"
            Else
                PN = "326-079"
            End If
        ElseIf DrType = "T165" Then
            P = 4.802
            If varDoorData.LiteColour = 1 Then
                PN = "326-084"
            ElseIf varDoorData.LiteColour = 2 Then
                PN = "326-082"
            Else
                PN = "326-080"
            End If
        ElseIf (DrType = "T150") Or (DrType = "T150 Multi-Rib") Then
            P = 4.68
            If varDoorData.LiteColour = 1 Then
                PN = "326-043"
            ElseIf varDoorData.LiteColour = 2 Then
                PN = "326-047"
            Else
                PN = "326-044"
            End If
        ElseIf (DrType = "T175") Or (DrType = "T175-20") Or (DrType = "T175A") Or _
            (DrType = "T175 Multi-Rib") Or (CECompliant = 0 And DrType = "T45") Then
'            P = 4.89
'            If varDoorData.LiteColour = 1 Then
'                PN = "326-041"
'            ElseIf varDoorData.LiteColour = 2 Then
'                PN = "326-048"
'            Else
'                PN = "326-042"
'            End If
            
            If varDoorData.LiteColour = 1 Then  'white
                Select Case varDoorData.LiteMaterial
                    Case 1                              'acrylic
                        P = 3.541
                        PN = "326-041A"
                    Case 2                              'tempered
                        P = 4.89
                        PN = "326-041T"
                    Case 3                              'glass
                        P = 4.89
                        PN = "326-041"
                End Select
            ElseIf varDoorData.LiteColour = 2 Then 'brown
                Select Case varDoorData.LiteMaterial
                    Case 1                              'acrylic
                        P = 3.541
                        PN = "326-048A"
                    Case 2                              'tempered
                        P = 4.89
                        PN = "326-048T"
                    Case 3                              'glass
                        P = 4.89
                        PN = "326-048"
                End Select
            ElseIf varDoorData.LiteColour = 3 Then  'black
                Select Case varDoorData.LiteMaterial
                    Case 1                              'acrylic
                        P = 3.541
                        PN = "326-042A"
                    Case 2                              'tempered
                        P = 4.89
                        PN = "326-042T"
                    Case 3                              'glass
                        P = 4.89
                        PN = "326-042"
                End Select
            End If
        ElseIf (CECompliant = 1 And DrType = "T45") Then '(DrType = "T45")
            If varDoorData.LiteColour = 1 Then  'white
                Select Case varDoorData.LiteMaterial
                    Case 1                              'acrylic
                        P = 3.541
                        PN = "326-041A"
                    Case 2                              'tempered
                        P = 4.89
                        PN = "326-041T"
                End Select
                
            ElseIf varDoorData.LiteColour = 2 Then 'brown
                Select Case varDoorData.LiteMaterial
                    Case 1                              'acrylic
                        P = 3.541
                        PN = "326-048A"
                    Case 2                              'tempered
                        P = 4.89
                        PN = "326-048T"
                End Select
            
            ElseIf varDoorData.LiteColour = 3 Then 'black
                Select Case varDoorData.LiteMaterial
                    Case 1                              'acrylic
                        P = 3.541
                        PN = "326-042A"
                    Case 2                              'tempered
                        P = 4.89
                        PN = "326-042T"
                End Select
            End If
        
        ElseIf (DrType = "T200") Or (DrType = "ADV200") Or (DrType = "T200A") Or (DrType = "T200A Multi-Rib") Or (DrType = "ADV200 Multi-Rib") Then
            P = 4.924
            If varDoorData.LiteColour = 1 Then
                PN = "326-067"
            ElseIf varDoorData.LiteColour = 2 Then
                PN = "326-069"
            Else
                PN = "326-068"
            End If
        End If
        If lintNLites <> 0 Or PN <> "" Then
            Call FillSummaryGrid(PN, lintNLites, "Sections", GetSubCategory(PN), 0)
        End If
    Case 3  'Type D thermal glass
        If (CECompliant = 1 And DrType = "T35") Then
            If varDoorData.LiteColour = 1 Then  'white
                Select Case varDoorData.LiteMaterial
                    Case 1                              'acrylic
                        P = 8.904
                        PN = "326-087A"
                    Case 2                              'tempered
                        P = 13.362
                        PN = "326-087T"
                End Select
                
            ElseIf varDoorData.LiteColour = 3 Then 'black
                Select Case varDoorData.LiteMaterial
                    Case 1                              'acrylic
                        P = 8.904
                        PN = "326-085A"
                    Case 2                              'tempered
                        P = 13.362
                        PN = "326-085T"
                End Select
            End If
        ElseIf DrType = "T140" Or DrType = "T138" Or (CECompliant = 0 And DrType = "T35") Then
            P = 13.362
            If varDoorData.LiteColour = 1 Then
                PN = "328-087"
            ElseIf varDoorData.LiteColour = 3 Then
                PN = "328-085"
            End If
            
        ElseIf DrType = "T165" Then
            P = 14.006
            If varDoorData.LiteColour = 1 Then
                PN = "328-088"
            ElseIf varDoorData.LiteColour = 3 Then
                PN = "328-086"
            End If
            
        ElseIf (DrType = "T150") Or (DrType = "T150 Multi-Rib") Then
            P = 13.684
            If varDoorData.LiteColour = 1 Then
                PN = "326-053"
            Else
                PN = "326-054"
            End If
        ElseIf (DrType = "T175") Or (DrType = "T175-20") Or (DrType = "T175A") Or _
            (DrType = "T175 Multi-Rib") Or (CECompliant = 0 And DrType = "T45") Then
'            P = 14.328
'            If varDoorData.LiteColour = 1 Then
'                PN = "326-055"
'            Else
'                PN = "326-056"
'            End If
            If varDoorData.LiteColour = 1 Then  'white
                Select Case varDoorData.LiteMaterial
                    Case 1                              'acrylic
                        P = 9.87
                        PN = "326-055A"
                    Case 2                              'tempered
                        P = 14.328
                        PN = "326-055T"
                    Case 3
                        P = 14.328
                        PN = "326-055"
                End Select
            Else                                'black
                Select Case varDoorData.LiteMaterial
                    Case 1                              'acrylic
                        P = 9.87
                        PN = "326-056A"
                    Case 2                              'tempered
                        P = 14.328
                        PN = "326-056T"
                    Case 3                              'glass
                        P = 14.328
                        PN = "326-056"
                End Select
            End If
        ElseIf (CECompliant = 1 And DrType = "T45") Then '(DrType = "T45") Then
            If varDoorData.LiteColour = 1 Then  'white
                Select Case varDoorData.LiteMaterial
                    Case 1                              'acrylic
                        P = 9.87
                        PN = "326-055A"
                    Case 2                              'tempered
                        P = 14.328
                        PN = "326-055T"
                End Select
                
            Else                                'black
                Select Case varDoorData.LiteMaterial
                    Case 1                              'acrylic
                        P = 9.87
                        PN = "326-056A"
                    Case 2                              'tempered
                        P = 14.328
                        PN = "326-056T"
                End Select
            End If
            
        ElseIf (DrType = "T200") Or (DrType = "T200 Multi-Rib") Or (DrType = "ADV200") Or (DrType = "T200A") Or (DrType = "T200A Multi-Rib") Or (DrType = "ADV200 Multi-Rib") Then
            P = 14.872
            If varDoorData.LiteColour = 1 Then
                PN = "326-057"
            Else
                PN = "326-058"
            End If
        End If
        If lintNLites <> 0 Or PN <> "" Then
            Call FillSummaryGrid(PN, lintNLites, "Sections", GetSubCategory(PN), 0)
        End If
    Case 4  'Type A
        P = 3.694
        PN = "326-030"
        If lintNLites <> 0 Or PN <> "" Then
            Call FillSummaryGrid(PN, lintNLites, "Sections", GetSubCategory(PN), 0)
        End If
    Case 5 'Type AA
        P = 4.48
        If varDoorData.LiteColour = 1 Then
            PN = "326-072"
            If DrType = "T200" Or DrType = "T200 Multi-Rib" Or DrType = "ADV200" Or DrType = "T200A" Or DrType = "T200A Multi-Rib" Or (DrType = "ADV200 Multi-Rib") Then
                Call FillSummaryGrid("326-099", lintNLites, "Sections", GetSubCategory("326-099"), 0)
                P = P + 0.25 'lite plus spacer
            End If
        Else
            If DrType = "T138" Or DrType = "T140" Or DrType = "T35" Then
                PN = "326-095"  '"AA" (BLACK) lites for 1-3/8" thick sections
            Else
                PN = "326-070"
            End If
            If DrType = "T200" Or DrType = "T200 Multi-Rib" Or DrType = "ADV200" Or DrType = "T200A" Or DrType = "T200A Multi-Rib" Or (DrType = "ADV200 Multi-Rib") Then
                Call FillSummaryGrid("326-098", lintNLites, "Sections", GetSubCategory("326-098"), 0)
                P = P + 0.25 'lite plus spacer
            End If
        End If
        If lintNLites <> 0 Or PN <> "" Then
            Call FillSummaryGrid(PN, lintNLites, "Sections", GetSubCategory(PN), 0)
        End If
    Case 6 'Type B thermal glass
        If (CECompliant = 1 And DrType = "T35") Then
            If varDoorData.LiteColour = 1 Then  'white
                Select Case varDoorData.LiteMaterial
                    Case 1                              'acrylic
                        P = 3.209
                        PN = "326-078A"
                    Case 2                              'tempered
                        P = 4.558
                        PN = "326-078T"
                End Select
            ElseIf varDoorData.LiteColour = 3 Then 'black
                Select Case varDoorData.LiteMaterial
                    Case 1                              'acrylic
                        P = 2.491
                        PN = "326-076A"
                    Case 2                              'tempered
                        P = 3.359
                        PN = "326-076T"
                End Select
            End If
        ElseIf DrType = "T140" Or DrType = "T138" Or (CECompliant = 0 And DrType = "T35") Then
            P = 3.359
            If varDoorData.LiteColour = 1 Then
                PN = "326-078"
            ElseIf varDoorData.LiteColour = 3 Then
                PN = "326-076"
            End If
        ElseIf DrType = "T165" Then
            P = 3.501
            If varDoorData.LiteColour = 1 Then
                PN = "326-075"
            ElseIf varDoorData.LiteColour = 3 Then
                PN = "326-077"
            End If
        ElseIf (DrType = "T150") Or (DrType = "T150 Multi-Rib") Then
            P = 3.43
            If varDoorData.LiteColour = 1 Then
                PN = "326-063"
            Else
                PN = "326-064"
            End If
        ElseIf (DrType = "T175") Or (DrType = "T175-20") Or (DrType = "T175A") Or _
                (DrType = "T175 Multi-Rib") Or (CECompliant = 0 And DrType = "T45") Then
'            P = 3.572
'            If varDoorData.LiteColour = 1 Then
'                PN = "326-065"
'            Else
'                PN = "326-066"
'            End If
            If varDoorData.LiteColour = 1 Then  'white
                Select Case varDoorData.LiteMaterial
                    Case 1                              'acrylic
                        P = 2.704
                        PN = "326-065A"
                    Case 2                              'tampered
                        P = 3.572
                        PN = "326-065T"
                    Case 3                              'glass
                        P = 3.572
                        PN = "326-065"
                End Select
            Else                                'black
                Select Case varDoorData.LiteMaterial
                    Case 1                              'acrylic
                        P = 2.704
                        PN = "326-066A"
                    Case 2                              'tampered
                        P = 3.572
                        PN = "326-066T"
                    Case 3                              'glass
                        P = 3.572
                        PN = "326-066"
                End Select
           End If
''''''''''Change in .NET''''''''''''''''

'            Call FillSummaryGrid(PN, lintNLites, "Sections", GetSubCategory(PN), 0)
''''''''''END Change in .NET
        ElseIf (CECompliant = 1 And DrType = "T45") Then
            If varDoorData.LiteColour = 1 Then  'white
                Select Case varDoorData.LiteMaterial
                    Case 1                              'acrylic
                        P = 2.704
                        PN = "326-065A"
                    Case 2                              'tampered
                        P = 3.572
                        PN = "326-065T"
                End Select
                
            Else                                'black
                Select Case varDoorData.LiteMaterial
                    Case 1                              'acrylic
                        P = 2.704
                        PN = "326-066A"
                    Case 2                              'tampered
                        P = 3.572
                        PN = "326-066T"
                End Select
            End If
        
        ElseIf (DrType = "T200") Or (DrType = "T200 Multi-Rib") Or (DrType = "ADV200") Or (DrType = "ADV200 Multi-Rib") Then
'             ' B lite with 2" panel is a non stock item.
            Exit Function
             
        End If

''''''''''Change in .NET''''''''''''''''
        Call FillSummaryGrid(PN, lintNLites, "Sections", GetSubCategory(PN), 0)

''''''''''END Change in .NET
    Case Is >= 7          'Full view or special
        Exit Function
    End Select
    
    If (varDoorData.LiteType >= 1) Then
        For i = 0 To UBound(varDoorData.arrSections)
            W = (varDoorData.arrSections(i).intNLites * P) + W
        Next i
    End If
    
    GetLiteWeight_andPN = W                     'return LiteWgt

    Exit Function

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Function
Public Function GetLiteArea() As Single
    
    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".GetLiteArea()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""

    'Calculates area of a lite
    'It does not calculate area of fullview sections

    Dim T As Single
    T = 0
    
    If varDoorData.NLiteSec <> 0 Then
        Select Case varDoorData.LiteType
        Case 1   'Type E thermal glass
            T = ((24 * 12) / 144) '* lintNLites
        Case 2 'CPL
            T = ((24 * 8) / 144) '* lintNLites
        Case 3 'D
           T = ((34 * 16) / 144) '* lintNLites
        Case 4 'A not used
            T = ((24 * 12) / 144) '* lintNLites
        Case 5 'AA
            T = ((24 * 6) / 144) '* lintNLites
        Case 6 'B
            T = ((26 * 13) / 144) '* lintNLites
        End Select
    End If
    GetLiteArea = T

    Exit Function

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Function

Public Function CheckMaxDrWidth() As Single 'not called
    
    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".CheckMaxDrWidth()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""

    'returns the maximum door width based on the door type
    Dim W As Single
    Select Case DrType
    Case "T150", "T150 Multi-Rib"
        W = 240
    Case "T175", "T175 Multi-Rib"
        W = 480
    Case "T175-20"
        W = 432
    Case "T200", "T200 Multi-Rib"
        W = 408
    Case "ADV200", "ADV200 Multi-Rib", "T300", "T300 Multi-Rib"    'T300
        W = 360
    Case "T200A"
        W = 276
    End Select
    
    CheckMaxDrWidth = W

    Exit Function

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Function

Public Function GetEndCaps(ByVal Ind As Integer) As Single
    
    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".GetEndCaps()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""

    'based on door type choice of end caps will be selected.
    'declare 4 part number variables for 21 Left and Right, and 24 Left and Right
    
    Dim PN21L As String, PN24L As String, PN21R As String, PN24R As String
    Dim W21 As Single, W24 As Single, W As Single
    If ProdType = "Rolltite" Or ProdType = "Alumatite" Then
        Exit Function    'end caps go only on thermatite.
    End If
    Select Case Ind ' itemdata value of the endcaps combo box
    Case 1, 2
        If DrType = "T150" Or DrType = "T150 Multi-Rib" Then
            If N21 <> 0 Then
                PN21L = "426-0316"   'L/H
                PN21R = "426-0317"   'R/H
                W21 = 1.816
            End If
            If N24 <> 0 Then
                PN24L = "426-0314"
                PN24R = "426-0315"
                W24 = 2.094
            End If
        ElseIf DrType = "T200" Or DrType = "T200 Multi-Rib" Or DrType = "ADV200" Or DrType = "T200A" Or DrType = "T200A Multi-Rib" Or DrType = "ADV200 Multi-Rib" Then
            If N21 <> 0 Then
                PN21L = "426-0340"
                PN21R = "426-0341"
                W21 = 1.898
            End If
            If N24 <> 0 Then
                PN24L = "426-0338"
                PN24R = "426-0339"
                W24 = 2.192
            End If
        
        
        ElseIf DrType = "T300" Or DrType = "T300 Multi-Rib" Then     ''  Bill - Weight need to be revised
            If N21 <> 0 Then
                PN21L = "426-0254"
                PN21R = "426-0255"
                W21 = 1.898
            End If
            If N24 <> 0 Then
                PN24L = "426-0256 "
                PN24R = "426-0257"
                W24 = 2.192
            End If
       
        
        End If
        
        
        
        
        
        
    Case 3, 4
        If DrType = "T150" Or DrType = "T150 Multi-Rib" Then
            If N21 <> 0 Then
                PN21L = "426-0334"
                PN21R = "426-0335"
                W21 = 3.094
            End If
            If N24 <> 0 Then
                PN24L = "426-0332"
                PN24R = "426-0333"
                W24 = 3.744
            End If
        ElseIf DrType = "T200" Or DrType = "T200 Multi-Rib" Or DrType = "ADV200" Or DrType = "T200A" Or DrType = "T200A Multi-Rib" Or DrType = "ADV200 Multi-Rib" Then
            If N21 <> 0 Then
                PN21L = "426-0344"
                PN21R = "426-0345"
                W21 = 2.938
            End If
            If N24 <> 0 Then
                PN24L = "426-0342"
                PN24R = "426-0343"
                W24 = 3.382
            End If
        
        ElseIf DrType = "T300" Or DrType = "T300 Multi-Rib" Then  'Weight need to be revised
            If N21 <> 0 Then
                PN21L = "426-0258"
                PN21R = "426-0259"
                W21 = 2.938
            End If
            If N24 <> 0 Then
                PN24L = "426-0260"
                PN24R = "426-0261"
                W24 = 3.382
            End If
        
       End If
  
  
  
  Case 5
        If N21 <> 0 Then
            PN21L = "7-2474"
            PN21R = "7-2475"
            W21 = 0.744
        End If
        If N24 <> 0 Then
            PN24L = "7-2472"
            PN24R = "7-2473"
            W24 = 0.826
        End If
   Case 6
        If N21 <> 0 Then
            PN21L = "426-0307"
            PN21R = "426-0307"
            W21 = 0.396
        End If
        If N24 <> 0 Then
            PN24L = "426-0306"
            PN24R = "426-0306"
            W24 = 0.396
        End If
    Case 7, 8
        If DrType = "T45" Then
            If N21 <> 0 Then
                PN21L = "426-0580"
                PN21R = "426-0581"
                W21 = 1.912
            End If
            If N24 <> 0 Then
                PN24L = "426-0582"
                PN24R = "426-0583"
                W24 = 2.218
            End If
        Else
            If N21 <> 0 Then
                PN21L = "426-0320"
                PN21R = "426-0321"
                W21 = 1.912
            End If
            If N24 <> 0 Then
                PN24L = "426-0318"
                PN24R = "426-0319"
                W24 = 2.218
            End If
        End If
        
    Case 9, 10
        If DrType = "T45" Then
            If N21 <> 0 Then
                PN21L = "426-0584"
                PN21R = "426-0585"
                W21 = 2.964
            End If
            If N24 <> 0 Then
                PN24L = "426-0586"
                PN24R = "426-0587"
                W24 = 3.734
            End If
        Else
            If N21 <> 0 Then
                PN21L = "426-0324"
                PN21R = "426-0325"
                W21 = 2.964
            End If
            If N24 <> 0 Then
                PN24L = "426-0322"
                PN24R = "426-0323"
                W24 = 3.734
            End If
        End If
    Case 13 'T35 single encaps
        If N21 <> 0 Then
            PN21L = "426-0562" 'L/H
            PN21R = "426-0563" 'R/H
            W21 = 1.46
        End If
        If N24 <> 0 Then
            PN24L = "426-0564"
            PN24R = "426-0565"
            W24 = 1.669
        End If
    Case 14 'T138 single endcaps
        If N21 <> 0 Then
            PN21L = "426-0503" 'L/H
            PN21R = "426-0504" 'R/H
            W21 = 1.46
        End If
        If N24 <> 0 Then
            PN24L = "426-0505"
            PN24R = "426-0506"
            W24 = 1.669
        End If
       
    Case 15 'T35 double endcaps
        If N21 <> 0 Then
            PN21L = "426-0568"
            PN21R = "426-0569"
            W21 = 2.27
        End If
        If N24 <> 0 Then
            PN24L = "426-0570"
            PN24R = "426-0571"
            W24 = 2.6
        End If
    
    Case 16 'T138 double endcaps same as T140 double endcaps
        If N21 <> 0 Then
            PN21L = "426-0537"
            PN21R = "426-0538"
            W21 = 2.8854
        End If
        If N24 <> 0 Then
            PN24L = "426-0539"
            PN24R = "426-0540"
            W24 = 3.2975
        End If
    End Select
    
    If Ind = 3 Or Ind = 4 Or Ind = 9 Or Ind = 10 Or Ind = 15 Or Ind = 16 Then
        varDoorData.DblEndCaps = True
    Else
        varDoorData.DblEndCaps = False
    End If
    
    Dim Num21 As Integer
    Dim Num24 As Integer
    
    Num21 = N21
    Num24 = N24
    
    If varDoorData.LiteType > 10 And varDoorData.EndCapsOnFullView = False Then
        Dim L21 As Integer
        Dim L24 As Integer
        L21 = ModSections.GetNumberOfLiteSectionsPerHeight(21)
        L24 = ModSections.GetNumberOfLiteSectionsPerHeight(24)
        
        Num21 = N21 - L21
        Num24 = N24 - L24
    End If
    
    If varDoorData.CutBtm + varDoorData.CutTop > 0 Then
        Dim lsngWCut1 As Single, lsngWCut2 As Single, lsngCutAmount As Single
        Dim lintCutTimes21 As Integer, lintCutTimes24 As Integer
        If varDoorData.CutTop > 0 Then
            lsngCutAmount = varDoorData.CutTop
            If varDoorData.arrSections(0).intHeight = 24 Then
                lsngWCut1 = W24 - ((W24 / 24) * lsngCutAmount)
                lintCutTimes24 = lintCutTimes24 + 1
            Else
                lsngWCut1 = W21 - ((W21 / 21) * lsngCutAmount)
                lintCutTimes21 = lintCutTimes21 + 1
            End If
            
        End If
        
        If varDoorData.CutBtm > 0 Then
            lsngCutAmount = varDoorData.CutBtm
            If varDoorData.arrSections(UBound(varDoorData.arrSections)).intHeight = 24 Then
                lsngWCut2 = W24 - ((W24 / 24) * lsngCutAmount)
                lintCutTimes24 = lintCutTimes24 + 1
            Else
                lsngWCut2 = W21 - ((W21 / 21) * lsngCutAmount)
                lintCutTimes21 = lintCutTimes21 + 1
            End If
        End If
        
        W = (((Num21 - lintCutTimes21) * W21) + ((Num24 - lintCutTimes24) * W24) + lsngWCut1 + lsngWCut2) * 2
    Else
