        W = (Num21 * W21 + Num24 * W24) * 2
    End If
    
    If Num21 <> 0 Then
        If PN21L = PN21R Then
            Call FillSummaryGrid(PN21L, 2 * Num21, "Sections", GetSubCategory(PN21L), 0)
        Else
            Call FillSummaryGrid(PN21L, Num21, "Sections", GetSubCategory(PN21L), 0)
            Call FillSummaryGrid(PN21R, Num21, "Sections", GetSubCategory(PN21R), 0)
        End If
    End If
    If Num24 <> 0 Then
        If PN24L = PN24R Then
            Call FillSummaryGrid(PN24L, 2 * Num21, "Sections", GetSubCategory(PN24L), 0)
        Else
            Call FillSummaryGrid(PN24L, Num24, "Sections", GetSubCategory(PN24L), 0)
            Call FillSummaryGrid(PN24R, Num24, "Sections", GetSubCategory(PN24R), 0)
        End If
    End If
    If Ind = 2 Or Ind = 4 Or Ind = 8 Or Ind = 10 Then
        Call FillSummaryGrid("152-328", 2, "Sections", GetSubCategory("152-328"), 0)
        W = W + ((DrWidth / 12) * 0.05)
    End If
    GetEndCaps = W
    
    Exit Function

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Function

Public Function GetResidentialEndCaps(ByVal Ind As Integer) As Single
    
    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".GetResidentialEndCaps()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""

    'based on door type choice of end caps will be selected.
    'declare 4 part number variables for 21 Left and Right, and 24 Left and Right
    
    Dim PN21L As String, PN24L As String, PN21R As String, PN24R As String
    Dim W As Single
    If ProdType = "Rolltite" Or ProdType = "Alumatite" Then
        Exit Function    'end caps go only on thermatite.
    End If
    Select Case Ind ' itemdata value of the endcaps combo box
    Case 11 'single endcaps
        If DrType = "T140" Then
            If N21 <> 0 Then
                PN21L = "426-0521"   'L/H
                PN21R = "426-0522"   'R/H
                W = 1.8518
            End If
            If N24 <> 0 Then
                PN24L = "426-0523"
                PN24R = "426-0524"
                W = 2.1163
            End If
        ElseIf DrType = "T165" Then
            If N21 <> 0 Then
                PN21L = "426-0525"
                PN21R = "426-0526"
                W = 1.9379
            End If
            If N24 <> 0 Then
                PN24L = "426-0527"
                PN24R = "426-0528"
                W = 2.2147
            End If
        End If
    Case 12 'double endcaps
        
        If DrType = "T140" Then
            If N21 <> 0 Then
                PN21L = "426-0537"
                PN21R = "426-0538"
                W = 2.8854
            End If
            If N24 <> 0 Then
                PN24L = "426-0539"
                PN24R = "426-0540"
                W = 3.2975
            End If
        ElseIf DrType = "T165" Then
            If N21 <> 0 Then
                PN21L = "426-0529"
                PN21R = "426-0530"
                W = 2.9715
            End If
            If N24 <> 0 Then
                PN24L = "426-0531"
                PN24R = "426-0532"
                W = 3.3959
            End If
        End If
    End Select
    If Ind = 12 Then varDoorData.DblEndCaps = True

''''''''''Change in .NET''''''''''''''''
'    W = (N21 + N24) * 2
    W = W * ((N21 + N24) * 2)
''''''''''END Change in .NET
    If N21 <> 0 Then
        If PN21L = PN21R Then
            Call FillSummaryGrid(PN21L, 2 * N21, "Sections", GetSubCategory(PN21L), 0)
        Else
            Call FillSummaryGrid(PN21L, N21, "Sections", GetSubCategory(PN21L), 0)
            Call FillSummaryGrid(PN21R, N21, "Sections", GetSubCategory(PN21R), 0)
        End If
    End If
    If N24 <> 0 Then
        If PN24L = PN24R Then
            Call FillSummaryGrid(PN24L, 2 * N21, "Sections", GetSubCategory(PN24L), 0)
        Else
            Call FillSummaryGrid(PN24L, N24, "Sections", GetSubCategory(PN24L), 0)
            Call FillSummaryGrid(PN24R, N24, "Sections", GetSubCategory(PN24R), 0)
        End If
    End If
    GetResidentialEndCaps = W
    
    Exit Function

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Function

Public Function GetRollerWgt(ByVal pblnNylon As Boolean, ByVal pbtQCloseFix As Byte, ByVal pblnDbl As Boolean, ByVal pblnUpgradeRollersToLongStem As Boolean) As Single

    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".GetRollerWgt()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""

    'function to return the total weight of rollers.
    'pbtQCloseFix (Quick close fixture)  (0 for none, 2 or 4)
    
    Dim NS As Integer
    Dim S As Integer, M As Integer, L As Integer    'for short, medium and long stem
    Dim SPN As String, MPN As String, LPN As String ' for part numbers of short, med...
    Dim W As Single
    
    NS = N21 + N24 'total number of sections.
    'first establish the number of rollers needed.
    
    Select Case HWType
    Case 2
        If pblnNylon = True Then
            SPN = "322-101"
            LPN = "322-110"
        Else
            SPN = "322-102"
            LPN = "322-104"
        End If
        If varDoorData.DblEndHinges = False Then
            If ProdType = "Rolltite" Then
                If pbtQCloseFix = 0 Then

''''''''''Change in .NET''''''''''''''''
                    S = (NS + 1) * 2
'                    S = NS + 1 * 2
''''''''''END Change in .NET
                    
                ElseIf pbtQCloseFix = 2 Then

''''''''''Change in .NET''''''''''''''''
                    S = (NS * 2) + 4
'                    S = NS * 2 + 4
''''''''''END Change in .NET
                    
                ElseIf pbtQCloseFix = 4 Then
                    S = NS * 2
                    L = 4
                End If
            Else
                If pbtQCloseFix = 0 Then
                    S = NS * 2
                    L = 2
                ElseIf pbtQCloseFix = 2 Then
                    S = (NS + 1) * 2
                    L = 2
                ElseIf pbtQCloseFix = 4 Then
                    S = (NS - 1) * 2
                    L = 6
                End If
            End If
        Else
            If pbtQCloseFix = 0 Then
                L = (NS * 2) + 2
            ElseIf pbtQCloseFix = 4 Then
                L = (NS * 2) + 4
            End If
        End If
''''''''''Change in .NET''''''''''''''''
'        If pblnNylon = True Then
        If pblnNylon = False Then
''''''''''END Change in .NET
            W = (S * 0.328) + (L * 0.434)   'calculate weight
        Else
            W = (S * 0.325) + (L * 0.42)   'calculate weight
        End If
    Case 3
        SPN = "950-094"
        'MPN = "950-095"
        LPN = "950-096"
        
        'MsgBox LPN
        'june'2004 medium rollers are substituted with long rollers
        If pblnNylon = True Then
            'MPN = "950-097"
            LPN = "950-097"
        End If
        If varDoorData.DblEndHinges = False And pblnNylon = False Then
            If pbtQCloseFix = 0 Then
'                S = (NS * 2) + 2
                S = (NS * 2)
                L = 2
            ElseIf pbtQCloseFix = 2 Then
                S = (NS * 2) + 2            'S = (NS * 2) + 4 'always have long stem roller with thermatite
                L = 2
            ElseIf pbtQCloseFix = 4 Then
                If ProdType = "Rolltite" Then
                    S = (NS * 2)
                    L = 4
                Else
                    S = (NS * 2)
                    'M = 4
                    L = 4
                    
                End If
            End If
        Else
            If pbtQCloseFix = 0 Then
'                If ProdType = "Rolltite" Then
                    L = (NS * 2) + 2
'                Else
'                    M = (NS * 2) + 2
'                End If
            ElseIf pbtQCloseFix = 4 Then
'                If ProdType = "Rolltite" Then
                    L = (NS * 2) + 4
'                Else
'                    M = (NS * 2) + 4
'                End If
            End If
        End If
        If pblnNylon = True Then
            'W = (M * 0.922)
            W = (L * 0.922)
        Else
            'W = (S * 0.616) + (M * 0.826) + (L * 0.842) 'calculate weight
            W = (S * 0.616) + (L * 0.842)  'calculate weight
        End If
    Case 3.25
        SPN = "7-2239"
        MPN = "7-2240"
        LPN = MPN
        If varDoorData.DblEndHinges = False Then
            If pbtQCloseFix = 0 Then
'                S = (NS * 2) + 2
                S = NS * 2
                M = 2
            ElseIf pbtQCloseFix = 2 Then
                S = (NS * 2) + 2    'S = (NS * 2) + 4 'always have long stem roller with thermatite
                M = 2
            ElseIf pbtQCloseFix = 4 Then
                If ProdType = "Rolltite" Then
                    S = (NS * 2)
                    L = 4
                Else
                    S = (NS * 2)
                    M = 4
                End If
            End If
        Else
            If pbtQCloseFix = 0 Then
                If ProdType = "Rolltite" Then
                    L = (NS * 2) + 2
                Else
                    M = (NS * 2) + 2
                End If
            ElseIf pbtQCloseFix = 4 Then
                If ProdType = "Rolltite" Then
                    L = (NS * 2) + 4
                Else
                    M = (NS * 2) + 4
                End If
            End If
        End If
        W = (S * 0.92) + (M * 1.104)    'calculate weight
    End Select
    
    If S > 0 Then
        Call FillSummaryGrid(SPN, S, "Sections", GetSubCategory(SPN), 0)
    End If
    If M > 0 Then
        Call FillSummaryGrid(MPN, M, "Sections", GetSubCategory(MPN), 0)
    End If
    If L > 0 Then
        Call FillSummaryGrid(LPN, L, "Sections", GetSubCategory(LPN), 0)
    End If
    GetRollerWgt = W

    Exit Function

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Function

Public Sub GetStandardTrussSchedule()

    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".GetStandardTrussSchedule()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""

    'determines standard truss schedule, should be called if NewLinearHW = False
    
    Dim TS As Integer 'truss schedule
    Dim DW As Long
    Dim DH As Long
    
    DW = RoundUp(DrWidth)
    DH = RoundUp(DrHgt)
    
    TS = 0
    If (DrType = "T175") Or (DrType = "T175-20") Or (DrType = "T200") Or (DrType = "T175 Multi-Rib") Then
        If (DH <= 96) Then
            If (DW > 198) And (DW <= 234) Then
                TS = 1
            ElseIf (DW > 234) And (DW <= 242) Then
                TS = 2
            ElseIf (DW > 242) And (DW <= 266) Then
                TS = 3
            ElseIf (DW > 266) And (DW <= 300) Then
                TS = 4
            End If
        ElseIf (DH > 96) And (DH <= 120) Then
            If (DW > 198) And (DW <= 230) Then
                TS = 1
            ElseIf (DW > 230) And (DW <= 242) Then
                TS = 2
            ElseIf (DW > 242) And (DW <= 266) Then
                TS = 3
            ElseIf (DW > 266) And (DW <= 300) Then
                TS = 4
            End If
        ElseIf (DH > 120) And (DH <= 144) Then
            If (DW > 198) And (DW <= 222) Then
                TS = 1
            ElseIf (DW > 222) And (DW <= 234) Then
                TS = 2
            ElseIf (DW > 234) And (DW <= 266) Then
                TS = 3
            ElseIf (DW > 266) And (DW <= 300) Then
                TS = 4
            End If
        ElseIf (DH > 144) And (DH <= 168) Then
            If (DW > 198) And (DW <= 222) Then 'changed from 219 to 222 (Apr29/2004 AW)
                TS = 1
            ElseIf (DW > 219) And (DW <= 234) Then
                TS = 2
            ElseIf (DW > 234) And (DW <= 266) Then
                TS = 3
            ElseIf (DW > 266) And (DW <= 300) Then
                TS = 4
            End If
        ElseIf (DH > 168) And (DH <= 192) Then
            If (DW > 198) And (DW <= 218) Then
                TS = 1
            ElseIf (DW > 218) And (DW <= 234) Then
                TS = 2
            ElseIf (DW > 234) And (DW <= 266) Then
                TS = 3
            ElseIf (DW > 266) And (DW <= 300) Then
                TS = 4
            End If
        ElseIf (DH > 192) And (DH <= 264) Then
            If (DW > 198) And (DW <= 234) Then
                TS = 2
            ElseIf (DW > 234) And (DW <= 266) Then
                TS = 3
            ElseIf (DW > 266) And (DW <= 300) Then
                TS = 4
            End If
        End If
    ElseIf (DrType = "ADV200") Or (DrType = "ADV200 Multi-Rib") Then
        If (DH <= 96) Then     '<= 8'
            If (DW > 182) And (DW <= 218) Then
                TS = 1
            ElseIf (DW > 218) And (DW <= 222) Then
                TS = 2
            ElseIf (DW > 222) And (DW <= 242) Then
                TS = 3
            ElseIf (DW > 242) And (DW <= 266) Then
                TS = 4
            ElseIf (DW > 266) And (DW <= 300) Then
                TS = 5
            End If
        ElseIf (DH > 96) And (DH <= 120) Then '<= 10'
            If (DW > 182) And (DW <= 210) Then
                TS = 1
            ElseIf (DW > 210) And (DW <= 222) Then
                TS = 2
            ElseIf (DW > 222) And (DW <= 242) Then
                TS = 3
            ElseIf (DW > 242) And (DW <= 266) Then
                TS = 4
            ElseIf (DW > 266) And (DW <= 300) Then
                TS = 5
            End If
        ElseIf (DH > 120) And (DH <= 144) Then    ' <= 12'
            If (DW > 182) And (DW <= 206) Then
                TS = 1
            ElseIf (DW > 206) And (DW <= 218) Then
                TS = 2
            ElseIf (DW > 218) And (DW <= 242) Then
                TS = 3
            ElseIf (DW > 242) And (DW <= 266) Then
                TS = 4
            ElseIf (DW > 266) And (DW <= 300) Then
                TS = 5
            End If
        ElseIf (DH > 144) And (DH <= 168) Then    '<= 14'
            If (DW > 182) And (DW <= 206) Then 'changed from 201 to 206 (Apr29/2004 AW)
                TS = 1
            ElseIf (DW > 201) And (DW <= 218) Then
                TS = 2
            ElseIf (DW > 218) And (DW <= 242) Then
                TS = 3
            ElseIf (DW > 242) And (DW <= 266) Then
                TS = 4
            ElseIf (DW > 266) And (DW <= 300) Then
                TS = 5
            End If
        ElseIf (DH > 168) And (DH <= 192) Then    '<= 16'
            If (DW > 182) And (DW <= 198) Then
                TS = 1
            ElseIf (DW > 198) And (DW <= 218) Then
                TS = 2
            ElseIf (DW > 218) And (DW <= 242) Then
                TS = 3
            ElseIf (DW > 242) And (DW <= 266) Then
                TS = 4
            ElseIf (DW > 266) And (DW <= 300) Then
                TS = 5
            End If
        ElseIf (DH > 192) And (DH <= 264) Then    ' <= 18' 'changed from 266 to 264 (Apr29/2004 AW)
            If (DW > 182) And (DW <= 218) Then
                TS = 2
            ElseIf (DW > 218) And (DW <= 242) Then
                TS = 3
            ElseIf (DW > 242) And (DW <= 266) Then
                TS = 4
            ElseIf (DW > 266) And (DW <= 300) Then
                TS = 5
            End If
        End If
    ElseIf (DrType = "T150") Or (DrType = "T200A") Or (DrType = "T175A") Or (DrType = "T150 Multi-Rib") Or (DrType = "ADV200 Multi-Rib") Then
        If (DH <= 96) Then
            If (DW > 170) And (DW <= 194) Then
                TS = 1
            ElseIf (DW > 194) And (DW <= 198) Then
                TS = 2
            ElseIf (DW > 198) And (DW <= 219) Then
                TS = 3
            ElseIf (DW > 219) And (DW <= 242) Then
                TS = 4
            End If
        ElseIf (DH > 96) And (DH <= 120) Then
            If (DW > 170) And (DW <= 189) Then
                TS = 1
            ElseIf (DW > 189) And (DW <= 198) Then
                TS = 2
            ElseIf (DW > 198) And (DW <= 219) Then
                TS = 3
            ElseIf (DW > 219) And (DW <= 242) Then
                TS = 4
            End If
        ElseIf (DH > 120) And (DH <= 144) Then
            If (DW > 170) And (DW <= 186) Then
                TS = 1
            ElseIf (DW > 186) And (DW <= 195) Then
                TS = 2
            ElseIf (DW > 195) And (DW <= 219) Then
                TS = 3
            ElseIf (DW > 219) And (DW <= 242) Then
                TS = 4
            End If
        ElseIf (DH > 144) And (DH <= 168) Then
            If (DW > 170) And (DW <= 183) Then
                TS = 1
            ElseIf (DW > 183) And (DW <= 195) Then
                TS = 2
            ElseIf (DW > 195) And (DW <= 219) Then
                TS = 3
            ElseIf (DW > 219) And (DW <= 242) Then
                TS = 4
            End If
        ElseIf (DH > 168) And (DH <= 216) Then
            If (DW > 170) And (DW <= 195) Then
                TS = 2
            ElseIf (DW > 195) And (DW <= 219) Then
                TS = 3
            ElseIf (DW > 219) And (DW <= 242) Then
                TS = 4
            End If
        ElseIf (DH > 216) And (DH <= 264) Then
            If (DW > 170) And (DW <= 194) Then
                TS = 2
            ElseIf (DW > 194) And (DW <= 218) Then
                TS = 3
            ElseIf (DW > 218) And (DW <= 242) Then
                TS = 4
            End If
        End If
    End If

    varDoorData.TrussSchedule = TS
    
    Exit Sub

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Sub

Public Function GetTrussWeight(ByVal TrussType As Integer, ByVal UpgradeTop As Boolean) As Single

    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".GetTrussWeight()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""

    'by the time this function is called, Truss Type should be determined.
    'based on the truss type, truss schedule and door width, the weight will be returned.
    Dim PN(3) As String 'Part Number
    Dim L As Single 'length of Truss
    Dim L1 As Single
    Dim lsngInchWgt(3) As Single 'unit weight of truss per inch.
    Dim W As Single 'weight
    Dim i As Integer
    Dim QTruss(3) As Integer
    Dim TempTrussType As Integer
    
    TempTrussType = TrussType
    'first get the correct number of trusses needed.
    ' truss schedule 0 : No Truss
    '                1 : 1Top + 1Bottom
    '                2 : 1Top + 1EveryOtherSection + 1Bottom
    '                3 : 1EverySection
    '                4 : 1EverySection + 1EveryOtherSection
    '                5 : 2EverySection
    '                6 : 1Top Only (recomended to customer if trolley operator is used on Thermatite door over 12'2" wide.(Bill Leather March22/02)
    'Z truss is available only in 1,2,3and 6 schedules and Standard is not available in 6
    'truss length is door width less 2 inches.
    
    'QTruss = GetTrussQtySelected
    
    
    'now get the weight
    For i = 0 To UBound(varDoorData.arrSections()) 'IIf(UpgradeTop = True, 1, 0)
        L = IIf(i = UBound(varDoorData.arrSections()) And (varPassDoor.btBottom = 2 Or varPassDoor.btBottom = 3), DrWidth - 6, DrWidth - 2)   'CalcTrussLen
        TempTrussType = varDoorData.arrSections(i).intTrussStyle
        'If i = 0 And UpgradeTop = True Then TempTrussType = TempTrussType + 1
        
        Select Case TempTrussType       '#1 = 2.5x18
        Case 1
            Select Case L
                Case Is <= 168           ' 14'truss
                    lsngInchWgt(0) = (13.78 / 168)    'get weight per inch
                    PN(0) = "431-0131"
                Case Is <= 192
                    lsngInchWgt(0) = (15.75 / 192)
                    PN(0) = "431-0132"
                Case Is <= 216
                    lsngInchWgt(0) = (17.72 / 216)
                    PN(0) = "431-0133"
                Case Is <= 240
                    lsngInchWgt(0) = (19.69 / 240)
                    PN(0) = "431-0134"
                Case Is <= 264
                    lsngInchWgt(0) = (21.66 / 264)
                    PN(0) = "431-0135"
                Case Is <= 288
                    lsngInchWgt(0) = (23.63 / 288)
                    PN(0) = "431-0136"
                Case Is <= 312
                    lsngInchWgt(0) = (25.6 / 312)
                    PN(0) = "431-0137"
            End Select
            QTruss(0) = QTruss(0) + 1
            'lsngInchWgt(0) = lsngInchWgt(0) * QTruss(0)
        Case 2              '#2 = 3 5/8x18
            Select Case L
                Case Is <= 240
                    lsngInchWgt(1) = (23.44 / 240)
                    PN(1) = "431-0151"
                Case Is <= 264
                    lsngInchWgt(1) = (25.78 / 264)
                    PN(1) = "431-0152"
                Case Is <= 288
                   lsngInchWgt(1) = (28.13 / 288)
                    PN(1) = "431-0153"
                Case Is <= 312
                    lsngInchWgt(1) = (30.47 / 312)
                    PN(1) = "431-0154"
                Case Is <= 336
                    lsngInchWgt(1) = (32.82 / 336)
                    PN(1) = "431-0155"
                Case Is <= 360
                    lsngInchWgt(1) = (35.16 / 360)
                    PN(1) = "431-0156"
            End Select
            QTruss(1) = QTruss(1) + 1
            'lsngInchWgt(1) = lsngInchWgt(1) * QTruss(1)
        Case 3              '#3 = 4x16
            Select Case L
                Case Is <= 312
                    lsngInchWgt(2) = (40.12 / 312)
                    PN(2) = "431-0171"
                Case Is <= 336
                    lsngInchWgt(2) = (43.21 / 336)
                    PN(2) = "431-0172"
                Case Is <= 360
                    lsngInchWgt(2) = (46.29 / 360)
                    PN(2) = "431-0173"
                Case Is <= 384
                    lsngInchWgt(2) = (49.38 / 384)
                    PN(2) = "431-0174"
                Case Is <= 480
                    lsngInchWgt(2) = (52.47 / 480)
                    PN(2) = "431-0175"
            End Select
            QTruss(2) = QTruss(2) + 1
            'lsngInchWgt(2) = lsngInchWgt(2) * QTruss(2)
        Case 4
            Select Case L
                Case Is <= 144
                    lsngInchWgt(3) = (15.75 / 144)
                    PN(3) = "431-0112"
                Case Is <= 168
                    lsngInchWgt(3) = (18.37 / 168)
                    PN(3) = "431-0114"
                Case Is <= 192
                    lsngInchWgt(3) = (21 / 192)
                    PN(3) = "431-0116"
                Case Is <= 216
                    lsngInchWgt(3) = (23.62 / 216)
                    PN(3) = "431-0118"
                Case Is <= 240
                    lsngInchWgt(3) = (26.25 / 240)
                    PN(3) = "431-0120"
                Case Is <= 264
                    lsngInchWgt(3) = (28.87 / 264)
                    PN(3) = "431-0122"
                Case Is <= 288
                    lsngInchWgt(3) = (31.5 / 288)
                    PN(3) = "431-0124"
                Case Is <= 312
                    lsngInchWgt(3) = (34.12 / 312)
                    PN(3) = "431-0126"
                Case Is <= 360
                    lsngInchWgt(3) = (39.37 / 360)
                    PN(3) = "431-0130"
                End Select
                QTruss(3) = QTruss(3) + varDoorData.arrSections(i).intNTruss
                'lsngInchWgt(3) = lsngInchWgt(3) * QTruss(3)
        End Select
    Next

''''''''''Change in .NET''''''''''''''''
    
'    For i = 0 To UBound(PN())
'        If PN(i) <> "" And QTruss(i) > 0 Then
'            If i = 3 And (varPassDoor.btBottom = 2 Or varPassDoor.btBottom = 3 Or varPassDoor.btTop = 3) Then
'                'pass door with truss on the bottom and on the top
'                If i = 3 And (varPassDoor.btBottom = 2 Or varPassDoor.btBottom = 3) And (varPassDoor.btTop = 3) Then
'                    L = DrWidth - 6
'                    If MeasU = 0 Then
'                        aCon.Inch = L
'                        aCon.mmcalc
'                        L = aCon.mm
'                    End If
'                    L1 = DrWidth - 2
'                    If MeasU = 0 Then
'                        aCon.Inch = L1
'                        aCon.mmcalc
'                        L1 = aCon.mm
'                    End If
'                    MRSBWgt = MRSBWgt + (lsngInchWgt(i)) * L
'                    W = W + lsngInchWgt(i) * L1
'                    Call FillSummaryGrid(PN(i), QTruss(i), "Sections", GetSubCategory(PN(i)), 0, "Cut (1) to " & IIf(MeasU = 1, GetImpString(L), Round(L, 0) & " mm") & " (1) to " & IIf(MeasU = 1, GetImpString(L1), Round(L1, 0) & " mm"))
'                'pass door with truss on the bottom only
'                ElseIf i = 3 And (varPassDoor.btBottom = 2 Or varPassDoor.btBottom = 3) And varPassDoor.btTop <> 3 Then
'                    If QTruss(i) > 1 Then 'standard truss is chosen as special schedule on sections above the passdoor.
'                        L = DrWidth - 6
'                        If MeasU = 0 Then
'                            aCon.Inch = L
'                            aCon.mmcalc
'                            L = aCon.mm
'                        End If
'                        L1 = DrWidth - 2
'                        If MeasU = 0 Then
'                            aCon.Inch = L1
'                            aCon.mmcalc
'                            L1 = aCon.mm
'                        End If
'                        MRSBWgt = MRSBWgt + (lsngInchWgt(i)) * L
'                        W = W + lsngInchWgt(i) * (QTruss(i) - 1) * L1
'                        Call FillSummaryGrid(PN(i), QTruss(i), "Sections", GetSubCategory(PN(i)), 0, "Cut (1) to " & IIf(MeasU = 1, GetImpString(L), Round(L, 0) & " mm") & " (" & QTruss(i) - 1 & ") to " & IIf(MeasU = 1, GetImpString(L1), Round(L1, 0) & " mm"))
'                    Else
'                        L = DrWidth - 6
'                        If MeasU = 0 Then
'                            aCon.Inch = L
'                            aCon.mmcalc
'                        End If
'                        Call FillSummaryGrid(PN(i), QTruss(i), "Sections", GetSubCategory(PN(i)), 0, "Cut to " & IIf(MeasU = 1, GetImpString(DrWidth - 6), Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm"))
'                        MRSBWgt = MRSBWgt + (lsngInchWgt(i)) * QTruss(i) * L
'                    End If
'                Else 'pass door with truss on the top only
'                    L = DrWidth - 2
'                    If MeasU = 0 Then
'                        aCon.Inch = L
'                        aCon.mmcalc
'                    End If
'                    Call FillSummaryGrid(PN(i), QTruss(i), "Sections", GetSubCategory(PN(i)), 0, "Cut to " & IIf(MeasU = 1, GetImpString(L), Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm"))
'                    W = W + lsngInchWgt(i) * QTruss(i) * L
'                End If
'            Else 'no passdoor or pass door with no truss
'                L = DrWidth - 2
'                If MeasU = 0 Then
'                    aCon.Inch = DrWidth - 2
'                    aCon.mmcalc
'                End If
'                Call FillSummaryGrid(PN(i), QTruss(i), "Sections", GetSubCategory(PN(i)), 0, "Cut to " & IIf(MeasU = 1, GetImpString(DrWidth - 2), Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm"))
'                W = W + (lsngInchWgt(i)) * QTruss(i) * L
'            End If
'        End If
'    Next

    Dim ldblMM As Double

    For i = 0 To UBound(PN())
        If PN(i) <> "" And QTruss(i) > 0 Then
            If i = 3 And (varPassDoor.btBottom = 2 Or varPassDoor.btBottom = 3 Or varPassDoor.btTop = 3) Then
                'pass door with truss on the bottom and on the top
                If i = 3 And (varPassDoor.btBottom = 2 Or varPassDoor.btBottom = 3) And (varPassDoor.btTop = 3) Then
                    L = DrWidth - 6
                    If MeasU = 0 Then
                        aCon.Inch = L
                        aCon.mmcalc
                        L = aCon.mm
                    End If
                    L1 = DrWidth - 2
                    If MeasU = 0 Then
                        aCon.Inch = L1
                        aCon.mmcalc
                        L1 = aCon.mm
                    End If
                    MRSBWgt = MRSBWgt + (lsngInchWgt(i)) * L
                    W = W + lsngInchWgt(i) * L1
                    Call FillSummaryGrid(PN(i), QTruss(i), "Sections", GetSubCategory(PN(i)), 0, "Cut (1) to " & IIf(MeasU = 1, GetImpString(L), Round(L, 0) & " mm") & " (1) to " & IIf(MeasU = 1, GetImpString(L1), Round(L1, 0) & " mm"))
                'pass door with truss on the bottom only
                ElseIf i = 3 And (varPassDoor.btBottom = 2 Or varPassDoor.btBottom = 3) And varPassDoor.btTop <> 3 Then
                    If QTruss(i) > 1 Then 'standard truss is chosen as special schedule on sections above the passdoor.
                        L = DrWidth - 6
                        If MeasU = 0 Then
                            aCon.Inch = L
                            aCon.mmcalc
                            L = aCon.mm
                        End If
                        L1 = DrWidth - 2
                        If MeasU = 0 Then
                            aCon.Inch = L1
                            aCon.mmcalc
                            L1 = aCon.mm
                        End If
                        MRSBWgt = MRSBWgt + (lsngInchWgt(i)) * L
                        W = W + lsngInchWgt(i) * (QTruss(i) - 1) * L1
                        Call FillSummaryGrid(PN(i), QTruss(i), "Sections", GetSubCategory(PN(i)), 0, "Cut (1) to " & IIf(MeasU = 1, GetImpString(L), Round(L, 0) & " mm") & " (" & QTruss(i) - 1 & ") to " & IIf(MeasU = 1, GetImpString(L1), Round(L1, 0) & " mm"))
                    Else
                        L = DrWidth - 6
                        If MeasU = 0 Then
                            aCon.Inch = L
                            aCon.mmcalc
                            ldblMM = aCon.mm
                        End If
                        Call FillSummaryGrid(PN(i), QTruss(i), "Sections", GetSubCategory(PN(i)), 0, "Cut to " & IIf(MeasU = 1, GetImpString(L), Round(ldblMM, 0) & " mm"))
                        MRSBWgt = MRSBWgt + (lsngInchWgt(i)) * QTruss(i) * L
                    End If
                Else 'pass door with truss on the top only
                    L = DrWidth - 2
                    If MeasU = 0 Then
                        aCon.Inch = L
                        aCon.mmcalc
                        ldblMM = aCon.mm
                    End If
                    Call FillSummaryGrid(PN(i), QTruss(i), "Sections", GetSubCategory(PN(i)), 0, "Cut to " & IIf(MeasU = 1, GetImpString(L), Round(ldblMM, 0) & " mm"))
                    W = W + lsngInchWgt(i) * QTruss(i) * L
                End If
            Else 'no passdoor or pass door with no truss
                L = DrWidth - 2
                If MeasU = 0 Then
                    aCon.Inch = DrWidth - 2
                    aCon.mmcalc
                    ldblMM = aCon.mm
                End If
                Call FillSummaryGrid(PN(i), QTruss(i), "Sections", GetSubCategory(PN(i)), 0, "Cut to " & IIf(MeasU = 1, GetImpString(L), Round(ldblMM, 0) & " mm"))
                W = W + (lsngInchWgt(i)) * QTruss(i) * L
            End If
        End If
    Next
''''''''''END Change in .NET
    
    GetTrussWeight = W

    Exit Function

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Function

Public Function SelectThermatiteFaceHWPkgs(ByVal pblnDbl As Boolean) As Single
     
    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".SelectThermatiteFaceHWPkgs()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""

    'This routine selects Face HardWare Packages
    ''''''''''''''''''
    Dim i As Integer, PN(3) As String, Q(3) As Integer, Wgt(3) As Single, W As Single
    
    'Alumatite face hardware packages = thermatite.
    If (ProdType = "Rolltite") Then
        Exit Function
    End If
        PN(0) = "8-9028"
        Wgt(0) = 0.31
        PN(1) = "8-9034"
        Wgt(1) = 0.37
        PN(2) = "8-9040"
        Wgt(2) = 0.44
        PN(3) = "8-9098"
        Wgt(3) = 1.05
        'each element in the PN array will correspond to the same element in the Q array.
        'meaning ex. PN 8-9028 Qty 2.
        If (varDoorData.DblEndHinges) And (DrWidth <= 194) Then
            If (DrWidth <= 110) Then
                If (DrHgt <= 97) Then
                    Q(3) = 1    '98
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 121) Then
                    Q(0) = 4    '4*28=112
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 145) Then
                    Q(1) = 4    '4*34=136
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 169) Then
                    Q(0) = 2
                    Q(3) = 1    '152
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 193) Then
                    Q(1) = 1
                    Q(2) = 1
                    Q(3) = 1    '172
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 217) Then
                    Q(3) = 2    '196
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 241) Then
                    Q(0) = 1
                    Q(3) = 2    '224
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 265) Then
                    Q(2) = 1
                    Q(3) = 2    '236
                End If
            ElseIf (DrWidth <= 146) Then
                If (DrHgt <= 97) Then
                    Q(1) = 2
                    Q(2) = 1    '114
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 121) Then
                    Q(1) = 1
                    Q(3) = 1    '132
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 145) Then
                    Q(0) = 2
                    Q(3) = 1    '152
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 169) Then
                    Q(2) = 2
                    Q(3) = 1    '178
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 193) Then
                    Q(1) = 3    '200
                    Q(3) = 1
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 217) Then
                    Q(0) = 1
                    Q(3) = 2    '224
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 241) Then
                    Q(0) = 2
                    Q(3) = 2    '264
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 265) Then
                    Q(2) = 2
                    Q(3) = 2    '
                End If
            ElseIf (DrWidth <= 194) Then
                If (DrHgt <= 97) Then
                    Q(0) = 3
                    Q(1) = 1
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 121) Then
                    Q(1) = 2
                    Q(2) = 2
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 145) Then
                    Q(2) = 2
                    Q(3) = 1
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 169) Then
                    Q(2) = 5
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 193) Then
                    Q(0) = 1
                    Q(2) = 5
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 217) Then
                    Q(0) = 2
                    Q(2) = 5
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 241) Then
                    Q(3) = 3
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 265) Then
                    Q(0) = 1
                    Q(3) = 3
                End If
            End If
        Else                            'single EndCaps
            If (DrWidth <= 110) Then
                If (DrHgt <= 97) Then
                    Q(0) = 2
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 121) Then
                    Q(1) = 2
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 145) Then
                    Q(0) = 3
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 169) Then
                    Q(0) = 2
                    Q(1) = 1
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 193) Then
                    Q(1) = 3
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 217) Then
                    Q(1) = 1
                    Q(2) = 2
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 241) Then
                    Q(0) = 1
                    Q(3) = 1
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 265) Then
                    Q(2) = 1
                    Q(3) = 1
                End If
            ElseIf (DrWidth <= 146) Then
                If (DrHgt <= 97) Then
                    Q(1) = 2
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 121) Then
                    Q(0) = 3
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 145) Then
                    Q(3) = 1
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 169) Then
                    Q(1) = 1
                    Q(2) = 2
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 193) Then
                    Q(1) = 1
                    Q(3) = 1
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 217) Then
                    Q(1) = 2
                    Q(2) = 2 '148
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 241) Then
                    Q(1) = 2
                    Q(3) = 1
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 265) Then
                    Q(2) = 2
                    Q(3) = 1
                End If
            ElseIf (DrWidth <= 194) Then
                If (DrHgt <= 97) Then
                    Q(0) = 3
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 121) Then
                    Q(3) = 1
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 145) Then
                    Q(2) = 3
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 169) Then
                    Q(2) = 1
                    Q(3) = 1
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 193) Then
                    Q(2) = 4
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 217) Then
                    Q(2) = 2
                    Q(3) = 1
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 241) Then
                    Q(3) = 2
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 265) Then
                    Q(0) = 1
                    Q(3) = 2
                End If
            ElseIf (DrWidth <= 242) Then
                If (DrHgt <= 97) Then
                    Q(1) = 1
                    Q(3) = 1
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 121) Then
                    Q(0) = 1
                    Q(1) = 1
                    Q(3) = 1
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 145) Then
                    Q(3) = 2
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 169) Then
                    Q(0) = 1
                    Q(3) = 2
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 193) Then
                    Q(0) = 2
                    Q(2) = 5
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 217) Then
                    Q(3) = 3
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 241) Then
                    Q(2) = 1
                    Q(3) = 3
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 265) Then
                    Q(1) = 2
                    Q(3) = 3
                End If
            ElseIf (DrWidth <= 290) Then
                If (DrHgt <= 97) Then
                    Q(0) = 5
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 121) Then
                    Q(2) = 2
                    Q(3) = 1
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 145) Then
                    Q(0) = 1
                    Q(3) = 2
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 169) Then
                    Q(0) = 2
                    Q(3) = 2
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 193) Then
                    Q(3) = 3
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 217) Then
                    Q(0) = 1
                    Q(3) = 3
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 241) Then
                    Q(1) = 2
                    Q(3) = 3
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 265) Then
                    Q(3) = 4
                End If
            End If
        End If
    'End If
    
    Dim n As Integer
    For n = 0 To 3
        If Q(n) <> 0 Then
            Call FillSummaryGrid(PN(n), Q(n), "Sections", GetSubCategory(PN(n)), 0)
        End If
    Next
    
    W = Q(0) * Wgt(0) + Q(1) * Wgt(1) + Q(2) * Wgt(2) + Q(3) * Wgt(3)
    SelectThermatiteFaceHWPkgs = W

    Exit Function

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Function

Public Function SelectThermatiteFaceHWPkgs_T45(ByVal pblnDbl As Boolean) As Single
     
    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".SelectThermatiteFaceHWPkgs_T45()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""

    'This routine selects Face HardWare Packages
    ''''''''''''''''''
    Dim i As Integer, PN(3) As String, Q(3) As Integer, Wgt(3) As Single, W As Single
    
    'Alumatite face hardware packages = thermatite.
    If (ProdType = "Rolltite") Then
        Exit Function
    End If
        PN(0) = "8-9028"
        Wgt(0) = 0.31
        PN(1) = "8-9034"
        Wgt(1) = 0.37
        PN(2) = "8-9040"
        Wgt(2) = 0.44
        PN(3) = "8-9098"
        Wgt(3) = 1.05
        'each element in the PN array will correspond to the same element in the Q array.
        'meaning ex. PN 8-9028 Qty 2.
        If (varDoorData.DblEndHinges) And (DrWidth <= 194) Then
            If (DrWidth <= 110) Then
                If (DrHgt <= 97) Then
                    Q(1) = 3    '102
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 121) Then
                    Q(2) = 3    '120
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 145) Then
                    Q(0) = 5    '140
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 169) Then
                    Q(2) = 4    '160
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 193) Then
                    Q(1) = 3
                    Q(2) = 2    '182
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 217) Then
                    Q(2) = 5    '200
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 241) Then
                    Q(0) = 1
                    Q(3) = 2    '224
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 265) Then
                    Q(2) = 6    '240
                End If
            ElseIf (DrWidth <= 146) Then
                If (DrHgt <= 97) Then
                    Q(0) = 1
                    Q(3) = 1    '126
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 121) Then
                    Q(0) = 2
                    Q(3) = 1    '152
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 145) Then
                    Q(2) = 2
                    Q(3) = 1    '178
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 169) Then
                    Q(0) = 4
                    Q(3) = 1    '210
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 193) Then
                    Q(2) = 1    '236
                    Q(3) = 2
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 217) Then
                    Q(0) = 2
                    Q(3) = 2    '264
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 241) Then
                    Q(3) = 3    '294
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 265) Then
                    Q(0) = 1
                    Q(3) = 3    '322
                End If
            ElseIf (DrWidth <= 194) Then
                If (DrHgt <= 97) Then
                    Q(1) = 2
                    Q(2) = 2
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 121) Then
                    Q(3) = 2
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 145) Then
                    Q(0) = 1
                    Q(3) = 2
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 169) Then
                    Q(1) = 2
                    Q(3) = 2
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 193) Then
                    Q(3) = 3
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 217) Then
                    Q(1) = 1
                    Q(3) = 3
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 241) Then
                    Q(1) = 2
                    Q(3) = 3
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 265) Then
                    Q(0) = 4
                    Q(3) = 3
                End If
            End If
        Else                            'single EndCaps
            If (DrWidth <= 110) Then
                If (DrHgt <= 97) Then
                    Q(0) = 2
                    Q(1) = 1
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 121) Then
                    Q(1) = 3
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 145) Then
                    Q(2) = 3
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 169) Then
                    Q(1) = 5
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 193) Then
                    Q(1) = 1
                    Q(2) = 3
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 217) Then
                    Q(1) = 5
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 241) Then
                    Q(0) = 3
                    Q(3) = 1
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 265) Then
                    Q(2) = 5
                End If
            ElseIf (DrWidth <= 146) Then
                If (DrHgt <= 97) Then
                    Q(3) = 1
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 121) Then
                    Q(2) = 3
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 145) Then
                    Q(1) = 3
                    Q(2) = 1
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 169) Then
                    Q(2) = 4
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 193) Then
                    Q(0) = 3
                    Q(3) = 2
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 217) Then
                    Q(2) = 5
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 241) Then
                    Q(0) = 3
                    Q(3) = 3
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 265) Then
                    Q(2) = 6
                End If
            ElseIf (DrWidth <= 194) Then
                If (DrHgt <= 97) Then
                    Q(0) = 4
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 121) Then
                    Q(1) = 4
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 145) Then
                    Q(0) = 1
                    Q(1) = 4
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 169) Then
                    Q(0) = 1
                    Q(2) = 4
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 193) Then
                    Q(1) = 5
                    Q(2) = 1
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 217) Then
                    Q(1) = 1
                    Q(2) = 5
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 241) Then
                    Q(1) = 2
                    Q(3) = 2
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 265) Then
                    Q(2) = 7
                End If
            ElseIf (DrWidth <= 242) Then
                If (DrHgt <= 97) Then
                    Q(1) = 5
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 121) Then
                    Q(0) = 1
                    Q(3) = 2
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 145) Then
                    Q(1) = 2
                    Q(3) = 2
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 169) Then
                    Q(0) = 4
                    Q(3) = 2
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 193) Then
                    Q(0) = 2
                    Q(3) = 3
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 217) Then
                    Q(3) = 4
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 241) Then
                    Q(2) = 1
                    Q(3) = 4
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 265) Then
                    Q(0) = 3
                    Q(3) = 4
                End If
            ElseIf (DrWidth <= 290) Then
                If (DrHgt <= 97) Then
                    Q(3) = 2
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 121) Then
                    Q(0) = 2
                    Q(3) = 2
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 145) Then
                    Q(1) = 3
                    Q(2) = 5
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 169) Then
                    Q(0) = 2
                    Q(3) = 3
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 193) Then
                    Q(0) = 4
                    Q(3) = 3
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 217) Then
                    Q(1) = 2
                    Q(3) = 4
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 241) Then
                    Q(0) = 4
                    Q(3) = 4
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 265) Then
                    Q(1) = 2
                    Q(3) = 5
                End If
            End If
        End If
    'End If
    
    Dim n As Integer
    For n = 0 To 3
        If Q(n) <> 0 Then
            Call FillSummaryGrid(PN(n), Q(n), "Sections", GetSubCategory(PN(n)), 0)
        End If
    Next
    
    W = Q(0) * Wgt(0) + Q(1) * Wgt(1) + Q(2) * Wgt(2) + Q(3) * Wgt(3)
    SelectThermatiteFaceHWPkgs_T45 = W

    Exit Function

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Function

Public Function SelectRollTiteFaceHWPkgs(ByVal pblnDbl As Boolean, ByVal pblnClinchNuts As Boolean) As Single
    
    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".SelectRollTiteFaceHWPkgs()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""

    'This routine selects Face HardWare Packages for RollTite door and returns weight
    Dim Wgt As Single, PN(9) As String, Q(9) As Integer, W(9) As Single
    If ProdType <> "Rolltite" Then
        Exit Function
    End If
    
    PN(0) = "8-3030"
    W(0) = 0.35
    PN(1) = "8-3036"
    W(1) = 0.46
    PN(2) = "8-3044"
    W(2) = 0.6
    PN(3) = "8-3052"
    W(3) = 0.72
    PN(4) = "8-3060"
    W(4) = 0.88
    PN(5) = "8-7027"
    W(5) = 0.32
    PN(6) = "8-7033"
    W(6) = 0.42
    PN(7) = "8-7039"
    W(7) = 0.52

    W(8) = 0.57

    If (DrType = "SR16") Or (DrType = "SR1626") Then
        If (varDoorData.DblEndHinges) And (DrWidth <= 194) Then
            If (DrWidth <= 110) Then
                If (DrHgt <= 97) Then
                    Q(2) = 2
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 121) Then
                    Q(3) = 1
                    Q(4) = 1

                    Q(2) = 1

                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 145) Then

                    Q(1) = 4

                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 193) Then

                If (DrHgt <= 97) Then

                    Q(3) = 1

                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 217) Then
                    Q(0) = 1

                End If
            ElseIf (DrWidth <= 194) Then

                    ElseIf (DrHgt <= 121) Then
                        Q(2) = 1

                        Q(3) = 1

                    ElseIf (DrHgt <= 169) Then
                        Q(4) = 1

                    ElseIf (DrHgt <= 193) Then
                        Q(1) = 2

                    End If
                ElseIf (DrWidth <= 194) Then
                    If (DrHgt <= 97) Then
                        Q(1) = 1

                        Q(8) = 1
                    ElseIf (DrHgt <= 121) Then
                        Q(2) = 1
                        Q(5) = 2
                        Q(8) = 1
                    ElseIf (DrHgt <= 145) Then
                        Q(3) = 1
                        Q(5) = 1
                        Q(9) = 1
                    ElseIf (DrHgt <= 169) Then

                        Q(8) = 2
                    ElseIf (DrHgt <= 193) Then
                        Q(1) = 2
                        Q(6) = 2
                        Q(9) = 1
                    ElseIf (DrHgt <= 217) Then
                        Q(1) = 1
                        Q(2) = 1
                        Q(5) = 2
                        Q(6) = 1
                        Q(9) = 1
                    End If
                End If
            ElseIf pblnClinchNuts Then
                If (DrWidth <= 110) Then
                    If (DrHgt <= 97) Then
                        Q(1) = 1
                        Q(5) = 1
                    ElseIf (DrHgt <= 121) Then
                        Q(2) = 1
                        Q(5) = 1
                    ElseIf (DrHgt <= 145) Then
                        Q(3) = 1
                        Q(5) = 1
                    ElseIf (DrHgt <= 169) Then
                        Q(4) = 1

                    ElseIf (DrHgt <= 193) Then
                        Q(1) = 2
                        Q(6) = 1
                    ElseIf (DrHgt <= 217) Then
                        Q(1) = 1
                        Q(2) = 1
                        Q(7) = 1
                    End If
                ElseIf (DrWidth <= 146) Then
                    If (DrHgt <= 97) Then
                        Q(1) = 1
                        Q(5) = 1
                    ElseIf (DrHgt <= 121) Then
                        Q(2) = 1
                        Q(7) = 1
                    ElseIf (DrHgt <= 145) Then
                        Q(3) = 1
                        Q(5) = 2
                    ElseIf (DrHgt <= 169) Then
                        Q(4) = 1
                        Q(5) = 2
                    ElseIf (DrHgt <= 193) Then
                        Q(1) = 2
                        Q(6) = 2
                    ElseIf (DrHgt <= 217) Then
                        Q(1) = 1
                        Q(2) = 1
                        Q(6) = 1
                        Q(7) = 1
                    End If
                ElseIf (DrWidth <= 194) Then
                    If (DrHgt <= 97) Then
                        Q(1) = 1
                        Q(7) = 1
                    ElseIf (DrHgt <= 121) Then
                        Q(2) = 1
                        Q(5) = 2
                    ElseIf (DrHgt <= 145) Then
                        Q(3) = 1
                        Q(6) = 2
                    ElseIf (DrHgt <= 169) Then
                        Q(4) = 1
                        Q(6) = 1
                        Q(7) = 1
                    ElseIf (DrHgt <= 193) Then
                        Q(1) = 2
                        Q(9) = 1
                    ElseIf (DrHgt <= 217) Then
                        Q(1) = 1
                        Q(2) = 1
                        Q(6) = 3
                    End If
                End If
            ElseIf varDoorData.DblEndHinges Then
                If (DrWidth <= 110) Then
                    If (DrHgt <= 97) Then
                        Q(5) = 2
                        Q(6) = 1
                    ElseIf (DrHgt <= 121) Then
                        Q(6) = 2
                        Q(8) = 1
                    ElseIf (DrHgt <= 145) Then
                        Q(7) = 1
                        Q(9) = 1
                    ElseIf (DrHgt <= 169) Then
                        Q(5) = 1
                        Q(6) = 1

                    ElseIf (DrHgt <= 193) Then
                        Q(7) = 2
                        Q(9) = 1
                    ElseIf (DrHgt <= 217) Then
                        Q(6) = 3
                        Q(9) = 1
                    End If
                ElseIf (DrWidth <= 146) Then
                    If (DrHgt <= 97) Then
                        Q(6) = 3
                    ElseIf (DrHgt <= 121) Then
                        Q(6) = 1
                        Q(9) = 1
                    ElseIf (DrHgt <= 145) Then
                        Q(5) = 1
                        Q(7) = 1
                        Q(8) = 2
                    ElseIf (DrHgt <= 169) Then
                        Q(8) = 2
                        Q(9) = 1
                    ElseIf (DrHgt <= 193) Then
                        Q(6) = 2
                        Q(7) = 1
                        Q(9) = 1
                    ElseIf (DrHgt <= 217) Then
                        Q(7) = 1
                        Q(9) = 2
                    End If
                ElseIf (DrWidth <= 194) Then
                    If (DrHgt <= 97) Then
                        Q(6) = 1
                        Q(7) = 2
                    ElseIf (DrHgt <= 121) Then
                        Q(5) = 4
                        Q(6) = 1
                    ElseIf (DrHgt <= 145) Then
                        Q(7) = 2
                        Q(9) = 1
                    ElseIf (DrHgt <= 169) Then
                        Q(6) = 2
                        Q(7) = 1
                        Q(9) = 1
                    ElseIf (DrHgt <= 193) Then
                        Q(5) = 1
                        Q(6) = 1
                        Q(8) = 4
                    ElseIf (DrHgt <= 217) Then
                        Q(6) = 1
                        Q(7) = 1
                        Q(9) = 2
                    End If
                End If
            Else    'No double hinges and clinch nuts
                If (DrWidth <= 110) Then
                    If (DrHgt <= 97) Then
                        Q(5) = 2
                    ElseIf (DrHgt <= 121) Then
                        Q(6) = 2
                    ElseIf (DrHgt <= 145) Then
                        Q(7) = 2
                    ElseIf (DrHgt <= 169) Then
                        Q(9) = 1
                    ElseIf (DrHgt <= 193) Then
                        Q(6) = 3
                    ElseIf (DrHgt <= 217) Then
                        Q(5) = 1
                        Q(9) = 1
                    End If
                ElseIf (DrWidth <= 146) Then
                    If (DrHgt <= 97) Then
                        Q(6) = 2
                    ElseIf (DrHgt <= 121) Then
                        Q(8) = 2
                    ElseIf (DrHgt <= 145) Then
                        Q(6) = 3
                    ElseIf (DrHgt <= 169) Then
                        Q(5) = 1
                        Q(9) = 1
                    ElseIf (DrHgt <= 193) Then
                        Q(7) = 1
                        Q(9) = 1
                    ElseIf (DrHgt <= 217) Then
                        Q(5) = 2
                        Q(9) = 1
                    End If

                    If (DrHgt <= 97) Then
                        Q(6) = 1
                        Q(8) = 1
                    ElseIf (DrHgt <= 121) Then
                        Q(5) = 2
                        Q(8) = 1
                    ElseIf (DrHgt <= 145) Then
                        Q(5) = 1
                        Q(9) = 1
                    ElseIf (DrHgt <= 169) Then
                        Q(5) = 2
                        Q(8) = 2
                    ElseIf (DrHgt <= 193) Then
                        Q(6) = 2
                        Q(9) = 1
                    ElseIf (DrHgt <= 217) Then
                        Q(5) = 2
                        Q(6) = 1
                        Q(9) = 1
                    End If
                End If
            End If
        Else    'Door's width > 194
            If (DrWidth <= 218) Then
                If (DrHgt <= 97) Then
                    Q(6) = 1
                    Q(9) = 1
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 121) Then
                    Q(5) = 3
                    Q(6) = 1
                    Q(8) = 1
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 145) Then
                    Q(5) = 1
                    Q(6) = 2
                    Q(9) = 1
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 169) Then
                    Q(7) = 1
                    Q(9) = 2
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 193) Then
                    
                    Q(7) = 1
                    Q(6) = 1
                    Q(9) = 2
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 217) Then
                    
                    Q(5) = 5
                    Q(9) = 2
                End If
            ElseIf (DrWidth <= 290) Then
                If (DrHgt <= 97) Then
                    
                    Q(5) = 2
                    Q(8) = 2
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 121) Then
                    
                    Q(8) = 2
                    Q(9) = 1
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 145) Then
                    Q(5) = 2
                    Q(6) = 2
                    Q(9) = 1
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 169) Then
                    
                    Q(8) = 6
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 193) Then
                    
                    Q(6) = 1
                    Q(8) = 6
                ElseIf (DrHgt <= 217) Then
                    
                    Q(7) = 2
                    Q(8) = 6
                End If
            End If
        End If
    End If
    
    Dim i As Integer
    For i = 0 To 9
        If Q(i) <> 0 Then
            Call FillSummaryGrid(PN(i), Q(i), "Sections", GetSubCategory(PN(i)), 0)
        End If
    Next
    
    Wgt = W(0) * Q(0) + W(1) * Q(1) + W(2) * Q(2) + W(3) * Q(3) + W(4) * Q(4) + W(5) * Q(5) + W(6) * Q(6) + W(7) * Q(7) + W(8) * Q(8) + W(9) * Q(9)
    SelectRollTiteFaceHWPkgs = Wgt

    Exit Function

Error_Handler:

    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Function

Public Sub GetRollerHinges(ByVal pblnHW As Boolean, ByVal pblnDblEndRollers As Boolean)

    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".GetRollerHinges()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""
    Dim MaxSec As Integer
    
    If NewLinearHW = False Or ProdType = "Rolltite" Or ProdType = "Alumatite" Then
        If HWType < 3 Then
            MaxSec = 10
        Else
            MaxSec = 14
        End If
    Else
        If HWType = 2 And pblnHW = True Then
            MaxSec = 16
        Else
            MaxSec = 14
        End If
    End If

    If N21 + N24 > MaxSec Then
        MsgBox "Unable to find proper Roller Hinges for that height." & _
            vbCrLf & "Please contact product manufacturer.", vbApplicationModal + vbExclamation + vbOKOnly, "Roller Hinges"
            
        Exit Sub
    End If
    
    If pblnHW = True Then
        varDoorData.gblnHingeUpgrade = True
    Else
        varDoorData.gblnHingeUpgrade = False
    End If
    
    Dim i As Integer, n As Integer, PN(13) As String     'part numbers of roller hinges
    Dim NH As Integer                                    'number of roller hinges per side or 'end hinge'
    Dim EndHingeQTY As Integer                           'number of end hinges = number of roller brackets
    EndHingeQTY = 0
    
    NH = (N21 + N24) - 1
    If DrType = "T45" Or DrType = "T35" Then
        For i = 0 To (NH - 1)
            PN(i) = GetLinHinge_T45(i + 1, pblnHW)
            EndHingeQTY = EndHingeQTY + 1
        Next
    Else
        For i = 0 To (NH - 1)
            PN(i) = GetLinHinge(i + 1, pblnHW)
        Next
    End If
    For i = 0 To (NH - 1)
        If PN(i) <> "" Then
            If pblnDblEndRollers = False Then
                Call FillSummaryGrid(PN(i), 2, "Sections", GetSubCategory(PN(i)), 0)
            Else
                Call FillSummaryGrid(PN(i), 4, "Sections", GetSubCategory(PN(i)), 0) 'need 2 per side with double end caps
                varDoorData.DblEndHinges = True
            End If
        End If
    Next
    
    Dim sngEndHingeQTY As Integer
    Dim dblEndHingeQTY As Integer
    
    'T45, T35 only
    If EndHingeQTY > 0 Then
        If pblnDblEndRollers = False Then
            sngEndHingeQTY = EndHingeQTY * 2    '(ns - 1) x 2
            Call FillSummaryGrid("9-2360", sngEndHingeQTY, "Sections", GetSubCategory("9-2360"), 0)
        Else
            dblEndHingeQTY = EndHingeQTY * 4    '(ns - 1) x 4
            Call FillSummaryGrid("9-2360", dblEndHingeQTY, "Sections", GetSubCategory("9-2360"), 0)
        End If
    End If
    
    Exit Sub

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Sub

Public Sub GetRollerHingesSTD(ByVal pintUpgrade As Integer, ByVal pblnDblEndRollers As Boolean)
    'pintUgrade :   0 if no upgrade, default selected
    '               1 if upgraded
    '               2 if upgraded to 11/13 ga (2" hw only)
    
    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".GetRollerHingesSTD()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""
    Dim MaxSec As Integer
    
    If NewLinearHW = False Or ProdType = "Rolltite" Or ProdType = "Alumatite" Then
        If HWType < 3 Then
            MaxSec = 10
        Else
            MaxSec = 14
        End If
    Else
        If HWType = 2 And pintUpgrade > 0 Then
            MaxSec = 16
        Else
            MaxSec = 14
        End If
    End If
    
    If N21 + N24 > MaxSec Then
        MsgBox "Unable to find proper Roller Hinges for that height." & _
            vbCrLf & "Please contact product manufacturer.", vbApplicationModal + vbExclamation + vbOKOnly, "Roller Hinges"
            
        Exit Sub
    End If
    
    If pintUpgrade > 0 Then
        varDoorData.gblnHingeUpgrade = True
    Else
        varDoorData.gblnHingeUpgrade = False
    End If
    
    Dim i As Integer, n As Integer, PN(13) As String     'part numbers of roller hinges
    Dim NH As Integer, lintQty As Integer                'number of roller hinges per side
    NH = (N21 + N24) - 1
    For i = 0 To (NH - 1)
        PN(i) = GetStdHinge((i + 1), pintUpgrade)
    Next
    
    For i = 0 To (NH - 1)
        If PN(i) <> "" Then
            If pblnDblEndRollers = False Then
                lintQty = 2
            Else
                lintQty = 4
                varDoorData.DblEndHinges = True
            End If
            'select center hinges for rolltite (use same pn as roller hinge #1)
            If i = 0 And ProdType = "Rolltite" Then
                If PN(0) = "9-2211" Then 'if that hinge is used as a #1 roller hinge, add qty,
                    lintQty = lintQty + GetNumInsideHingesPerWidth * (N21 + N24 - 1)
                Else 'add that pn as center hinge seperately
                    Call FillSummaryGrid("9-2211", GetNumInsideHingesPerWidth * (N21 + N24 - 1), "Sections", GetSubCategory("9-2211"), 0)
                End If
            End If
            Call FillSummaryGrid(PN(i), lintQty, "Sections", GetSubCategory(PN(i)), 0)
        End If
    Next
    
    Exit Sub

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Sub

Function GetStdHinge(ByVal Ind As Integer, ByVal pintUpgrade As Integer)   'chose part numbers for standard HW

    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".GetStdHinge()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0

    lstrErrSource = ""


    If HWType = 2 Then
        If pintUpgrade = 0 Then

        ElseIf pintUpgrade = 1 Then
            GetStdHinge = Choose(Ind, "9-2221", "9-2222", "9-2223", "9-2224", "9-2225", "9-2226", "9-2227", "322-218", "322-219", "322-220")
        ElseIf pintUpgrade = 2 Then
            GetStdHinge = Choose(Ind, "9-2221", "9-2222", "9-2233", "9-2234", "9-2235", "9-2236", "9-2237", "322-218", "322-219", "322-220")
        End If
    ElseIf HWType = 3 Then
        If pintUpgrade = 0 Then
            GetStdHinge = Choose(Ind, "9-2223", "9-2224", "9-2225", "9-2226", "9-2227", "322-218", "322-219", "322-220", "7-3165", "7-3166", "7-3167", "7-3168", "7-3169", "7-3170")
        ElseIf pintUpgrade = 1 Then
            GetStdHinge = Choose(Ind, "9-2233", "9-2234", "9-2235", "9-2236", "9-2237", "322-218", "322-219", "322-220", "7-3165", "7-3166", "7-3167", "7-3168", "7-3169", "7-3170")
        End If
    ElseIf HWType = 3.25 Then
       GetStdHinge = Choose(Ind, "9-2233", "9-2234", "9-2235", "9-2236", "9-2237", "322-218", "322-219", "322-220", "7-3165", "7-3166", "7-3167", "7-3168", "7-3169", "7-3170")
    End If
        
    Exit Function

Error_Handler:

    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Function

Public Function GetRollerHingesWgt(ByVal Upgr As Byte, ByVal pblnDbl As Boolean) As Single

    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".GetRollerHingesWgt()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""


    Dim i As Integer
    Dim W As Single                      'weight of roller hinges
    Dim NH As Integer                    'number of roller hinges per side
    Dim MaxSec As Integer
    
    If NewLinearHW = False Or ProdType = "Rolltite" Or ProdType = "Alumatite" Then
        If HWType < 3 Then
            MaxSec = 10
        Else
            MaxSec = 14
        End If
    Else
        If HWType = 2 And Upgr > 0 Then
            MaxSec = 16
        Else
            MaxSec = 14
        End If
    End If
    
    
    NH = (N21 + N24) - 1
    
    If NH > MaxSec Then Exit Function
        
    If NewLinearHW = False Or ProdType = "Rolltite" Or ProdType = "Alumatite" Then
        For i = 0 To (NH - 1)
            W = W + GetStdHingeWgt(Upgr, i + 1)
        Next
    Else
        If DrType = "T45" Or DrType = "T35" Then
            For i = 0 To (NH - 1)
                W = W + GetLinHingeWgt_T45(i + 1)
            Next
        Else
            For i = 0 To (NH - 1)
                W = W + GetLinHingeWgt(i + 1)
            Next
        End If
    End If
    
    If varDoorData.DblEndHinges = False Then
        GetRollerHingesWgt = W * 2
    Else
        GetRollerHingesWgt = W * 4  'double hinges with dbl end caps
    End If
    Exit Function

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Function

Function GetStdHingeWgt(ByVal pintUpgrade As Byte, ByVal Ind As Integer)  'chose weight for standard HW

    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".GetStdHingeWgt()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""

    If HWType = 2 Then
        If pintUpgrade = 0 Then
            GetStdHingeWgt = Choose(Ind, 0.44, 0.56, 0.56, 0.56, 0.53, 0.61, 0.64, 0.87, 0.9, 0.91)
        ElseIf pintUpgrade = 1 Then
            GetStdHingeWgt = Choose(Ind, 0.5, 0.6, 0.6, 0.64, 0.63, 0.61, 0.64, 0.87, 0.9, 0.91)
        Else
            GetStdHingeWgt = Choose(Ind, 0.5, 0.6, 0.81, 0.81, 0.81, 0.9, 0.9, 0.87, 0.9, 0.91)
        End If
    ElseIf HWType = 3 Then
        If pintUpgrade = 0 Then
            GetStdHingeWgt = Choose(Ind, 0.6, 0.64, 0.63, 0.61, 0.64, 0.87, 0.9, 0.91, 1.29, 1.29, 1.303, 1.303, 1.303, 1.303)
        Else
            GetStdHingeWgt = Choose(Ind, 0.81, 0.81, 0.81, 0.9, 0.9, 0.87, 0.9, 0.91, 1.29, 1.29, 1.303, 1.303, 1.303, 1.303)
        End If
    ElseIf HWType = 3.25 Then
        GetStdHingeWgt = Choose(Ind, 0.81, 0.81, 0.81, 0.9, 0.9, 0.87, 0.9, 0.91, 1.29, 1.29, 1.303, 1.303, 1.303, 1.303)
    End If
    
    Exit Function

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Function

        End If
    ElseIf HWType = 3 Then
