Public Function GetBottomRollBrackets(ByVal pblnDbl As Boolean) As Single
       
    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".GetBottomRollBrackets()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""

    Dim PN(4) As String
    Dim W As Single
    Dim Q(4) As Integer
    If ProdType = "Thermatite" Or ProdType = "Thermalume" Or ProdType = "Alumatite" Or ProdType = "Replacement" Then
        If LiftType = "Low Headroom Rear Mount" Then
            If HWType = 2 Then
                PN(0) = "950-151"
                Q(0) = 1
                PN(1) = "950-152"
                Q(1) = 1
                W = 2.94
                If varDoorData.DblEndHinges = True Then
                    PN(2) = "7-2387"
                    Q(2) = 2
                    W = W + (0.77 * 2)
                End If
            Else
                PN(0) = "950-090"
                Q(0) = 1
                PN(1) = "950-091"
                Q(1) = 1
                W = 9.88
                If varDoorData.DblEndHinges = True Then
                    PN(2) = "7-2343"
                    Q(2) = 2
                    W = W + (0.76 * 2)
                End If
            End If
        Else
            If varDoorData.DblEndHinges = False Then
                If HWType = 2 Then
                    PN(0) = "436-0049"
                    Q(0) = 1
                    PN(1) = "436-0048"
                    Q(1) = 1
                    W = 1.78
                Else
                    PN(0) = "7-2362"
                    Q(0) = 1
                    PN(1) = "7-2361"
                    Q(1) = 1
                    W = 1.66
                End If
            Else
                If HWType = 2 Then
                    If DrWgt < 1000 - 3.33 Then     'the weight of the btm brkt
                        PN(0) = "436-0049"          'is added to the weigth after
                        Q(0) = 1                    'this routine is called
                        PN(1) = "436-0048"          'yet if the drweight total is 1000
                        Q(1) = 1                    'we need to use the heavy brkts.
                        PN(2) = "7-2387"            'this way if drwgt at this moment is
                        Q(2) = 2                    '996.67 then after adding 3.33 for the brkts,
                        W = 3.32                    'the wgt will be 999.99, which if fine for lite brkts.
                    Else
                         'NOTE: 7-3304 / 7-3305 should be picked only if HWSize = 3"/3.25"
'                        PN(0) = "7-3304"
'                        Q(0) = 1
'                        PN(1) = "7-3305"
'                        Q(1) = 1
'                        W = 9.78
                    End If
                Else
                    If DrWgt < 1000 - 3.19 Then
                        PN(0) = "7-2362"
                        Q(0) = 1
                        PN(1) = "7-2361"
                        Q(1) = 1
                        PN(2) = "7-2343"
                        Q(2) = 2
                        W = 3.18
                    Else
                        PN(0) = "7-3304"
                        Q(0) = 1
                        PN(1) = "7-3305"
                        Q(1) = 1
                        W = 9.78
                    End If
                End If
            End If
        End If
    ElseIf ProdType = "Rolltite" Then
        If LiftType = "Low Headroom Rear Mount" Then
            If HWType = 2 Then
                PN(0) = "950-151"
                Q(0) = 1
                PN(1) = "950-152"
                Q(1) = 1
                W = 2.94
                If varDoorData.DblEndHinges = True Then
                    PN(2) = "7-2387"
                    Q(2) = 2
                    W = W + (0.77 * 2)
                End If
            Else
                PN(0) = "950-090"
                Q(0) = 1
                PN(1) = "950-091"
                Q(1) = 1
                W = 9.88
                If varDoorData.DblEndHinges = True Then
                    PN(2) = "7-2343"
                    Q(2) = 2
                    W = W + (0.76 * 2)
                End If
            End If
        Else
            If HWType = 2 Then
                PN(0) = "7-2387"
                If varDoorData.DblEndHinges = False Then
                    Q(0) = 2
                    W = 0.77 * 2
                Else
                    Q(0) = 4
                    W = 0.77 * 4
                End If
            Else
                PN(0) = "7-2343"
                If varDoorData.DblEndHinges = False Then
                    Q(0) = 2
                    W = 0.76 * 2
                Else
                    Q(0) = 4
                    W = 0.76 * 4
                End If
            End If
        End If
    End If
    Call FillSummaryGrid("4-0032", 1, "Sections", GetSubCategory("4-0032"), 0)
    'pack of 2 clevis pins per any two bottom brackets
    W = W + 0.06 * IIf(DrWidth > 242, 2, 1)  ' add weight of pins

    '''''''''''''''''
    Dim i As Integer
    For i = 0 To 3
        If Q(i) <> 0 Then
            Call FillSummaryGrid(PN(i), Q(i), "Sections", GetSubCategory(PN(i)), 0)
        End If
    Next
    
    GetBottomRollBrackets = W

    Exit Function

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Function

Public Function GetTopRollBrackets(ByVal pbtQCloseFix As Byte) As Single
    
    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".GetTopRollBrackets()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""

    Dim PN(3) As String
    Dim W As Single
    Dim Q(3) As Integer
    If ProdType = "Thermatite" Or ProdType = "Thermalume" Or (ProdType = "Replacement") Then
        If (NewLinearHW = False) Or (varDoorData.TrussSchedule = 0) Then
            PN(0) = "322-225"
            Q(0) = IIf(varDoorData.DblEndHinges = True, 4, 2)
            PN(1) = ""
            Q(1) = 0
            PN(2) = ""
            Q(2) = 0

            W = IIf(varDoorData.DblEndHinges = True, 3.56, 1.78)
        Else
            PN(0) = "111-1474"
            Q(0) = IIf(varDoorData.DblEndHinges = True, 4, 2)
            PN(1) = "111-1475"
            Q(1) = 2
            PN(2) = "425-0121"
            Q(2) = IIf(varDoorData.DblEndHinges = True, 4, 2)
            W = IIf(varDoorData.DblEndHinges = True, 4.24, 2.46)
        End If
    End If
    If ProdType = "Alumatite" Then
        PN(0) = "322-225"
        Q(0) = IIf(varDoorData.DblEndHinges = True, 4, 2)
        PN(1) = ""
        Q(1) = 0
        PN(2) = ""
        Q(2) = 0
        W = IIf(varDoorData.DblEndHinges = True, 3.56, 1.78)
    End If
    If ProdType = "Rolltite" Then
        PN(0) = "111-1473"
        Q(0) = IIf(varDoorData.DblEndHinges = True, 4, 2)
        PN(1) = "425-0121"
        Q(1) = IIf(varDoorData.DblEndHinges = True, 4, 2)
        PN(2) = ""
        Q(2) = 0

        W = W = IIf(varDoorData.DblEndHinges = True, 4.52, 2.26)
    End If
    
    If LiftType = "Low Headroom Front Mount" Or LiftType = "Low Headroom Rear Mount" Then
        PN(0) = "950-083"
        Q(0) = IIf(varDoorData.DblEndHinges = True, 4, 2)
        PN(1) = ""
        Q(1) = 0
        PN(2) = ""
        Q(2) = 0


''''''''''Change in .NET''''''''''''''''
'        W = 0.9
        W = IIf(varDoorData.DblEndHinges = True, 4, 2) * 0.448
        
''''''''''END Change in .NET
    End If
    
    If pbtQCloseFix = 2 Then
        PN(0) = "322-228"
        Q(0) = IIf(varDoorData.DblEndHinges = True, 4, 2)
        PN(1) = ""
        Q(1) = 0
        PN(2) = ""
        Q(2) = 0

        GetTopRollBrackets = IIf(varDoorData.DblEndHinges = True, 8.36, 4.18) 'if selected will replace top bracket
    ElseIf pbtQCloseFix = 4 Then
        PN(0) = "322-228"
        Q(0) = 4
        PN(1) = ""
        Q(1) = 0
        PN(2) = ""
        Q(2) = 0

        GetTopRollBrackets = 8.36 'if selected will replace top bracket
    Else
        GetTopRollBrackets = W
    End If
    
    Dim i As Integer
    For i = 0 To 3
        If Q(i) <> 0 Then
            Call FillSummaryGrid(PN(i), Q(i), "Sections", GetSubCategory(PN(i)), 0)
        End If
    Next

    Exit Function

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Function

Public Function CalcFullViewWgt(ByVal pblnDbl As Boolean, ByVal NPanels As Integer) As Single

    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".CalcFullViewWgt()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""

    Dim RailTop As Single, RailBottom As Single, RailEnd As Single, RailCenter As Single
    Dim QTY As Single, Wdt As Single, Wgt As Single, H As Single 'width, weight and height
    Dim Area As Single      'glass area
    Dim Retainer As Single  'retainers' weight
    Dim L21 As Integer
    Dim L24 As Integer
    Dim Anodz As Boolean
    L21 = ModSections.GetNumberOfLiteSectionsPerHeight(21)
    L24 = ModSections.GetNumberOfLiteSectionsPerHeight(24)
    If Mid(CStr(varDoorData.LiteType), 1, 1) = "7" Then Anodz = True
    'calculate wgt of all needed rails and retainers
    If DrType = "T175" Or DrType = "T175-20" Or DrType = "T175A" Or DrType = "T175 Multi-Rib" Or DrType = "T200A" Or DrType = "ADV200" Or DrType = "ADV200 Multi-Rib" Then
        If Anodz Then
            QTY = ((DrWidth - 0.375) / 12) * (L21 + L24)
            RailBottom = QTY * 0.86484
            RailTop = QTY * 0.5425
            QTY = 0
            If L21 > 0 Then
                QTY = ((21 - 2.25) / 12) * (2 * L21)
            End If
            If L24 > 0 Then
                QTY = ((24 - 2.25) / 12) * (2 * L24) + QTY
            End If
            RailEnd = QTY * 1.053
            If varDoorData.DblEndCaps = True Then
                RailEnd = QTY * 0.9788 + RailEnd
            End If
            QTY = 0
            If L21 > 0 Then
                QTY = ((21 - 2.25) / 12) * ((NPanels - 1) * L21)
            End If
            If L24 > 0 Then
                QTY = ((24 - 2.25) / 12) * ((NPanels - 1) * L24) + QTY
            End If
            RailCenter = QTY * 0.6538
        
        Else
        
            QTY = ((DrWidth - 0.375) / 12) * (L21 + L24)
            RailBottom = QTY * 0.9109
            RailTop = QTY * 0.6151
            QTY = 0
            If L21 > 0 Then
                QTY = ((21 - 2.25) / 12) * (2 * L21)
            End If
            If L24 > 0 Then
                QTY = ((24 - 2.25) / 12) * (2 * L24) + QTY
            End If
            RailEnd = QTY * 1.053
            If varDoorData.DblEndCaps = True Then
                RailEnd = QTY * 1.0011 + RailEnd
            End If
            QTY = 0
            If L21 > 0 Then
                QTY = ((21 - 2.25) / 12) * ((NPanels - 1) * L21)
            End If
            If L24 > 0 Then
                QTY = ((24 - 2.25) / 12) * ((NPanels - 1) * L24) + QTY
            End If
            RailCenter = QTY * 0.6765
        End If
        
    ElseIf DrType = "T45" Then
        If Anodz Then
            QTY = ((DrWidth - 0.375) / 12) * (L21 + L24)
            RailBottom = QTY * 0.837
            RailTop = QTY * 1.138
            QTY = 0
            If L21 > 0 Then
                QTY = ((21 - 4.5) / 12) * (2 * L21)
            End If
            If L24 > 0 Then
                QTY = ((24 - 4.5) / 12) * (2 * L24) + QTY
            End If
            RailEnd = QTY * 1.07
            If varDoorData.DblEndCaps = True Then 'A45 full-view uses standard A175 double end rail
                RailEnd = QTY * 0.9788 + RailEnd
            End If
            QTY = 0
            If L21 > 0 Then
                QTY = ((21 - 4.5) / 12) * ((NPanels - 1) * L21)
            End If
            If L24 > 0 Then
                QTY = ((24 - 4.5) / 12) * ((NPanels - 1) * L24) + QTY
            End If
            RailCenter = QTY * 0.695
        Else ' for white Duracron finish
        
            QTY = ((DrWidth - 0.375) / 12) * (L21 + L24)
            RailBottom = QTY * 0.8795
            RailTop = QTY * 1.2106
            QTY = 0
            If L21 > 0 Then
                QTY = ((21 - 4.5) / 12) * (2 * L21)
            End If
            If L24 > 0 Then
                QTY = ((24 - 4.5) / 12) * (2 * L24) + QTY
            End If
            RailEnd = QTY * 1.1524
            If varDoorData.DblEndCaps = True Then 'A45 full-view uses standard A175 double end rail
                RailEnd = QTY * 1.0011 + RailEnd
            End If
            QTY = 0
            If L21 > 0 Then
                QTY = ((21 - 4.5) / 12) * ((NPanels - 1) * L21)
            End If
            If L24 > 0 Then
                QTY = ((24 - 4.5) / 12) * ((NPanels - 1) * L24) + QTY
            End If
            RailCenter = QTY * 0.7177
        End If
            
    
    ElseIf DrType = "T150" Or DrType = "T150 Multi-Rib" Then
        QTY = ((DrWidth - 0.375) / 12) * (L21 + L24)
        RailBottom = QTY * 0.862
        RailTop = QTY * 0.4834
        QTY = 0
        If L21 > 0 Then
            QTY = ((21 - 2.25) / 12) * (2 * L21)
        End If
        If L24 > 0 Then
            QTY = ((24 - 2.25) / 12) * (2 * L24) + QTY
        End If
        RailEnd = QTY * 0.9133
        QTY = 0
        If L21 > 0 Then
            QTY = ((21 - 2.25) / 12) * ((NPanels - 1) * L21)
        End If
        If L24 > 0 Then
            QTY = ((24 - 2.25) / 12) * ((NPanels - 1) * L24) + QTY
        End If
        RailCenter = QTY * 0.5928
    End If
    
    'calculate glass area
    If (varDoorData.DblEndCaps = False) Or DrType = "T150" Or DrType = "T150 Multi-Rib" Then
        Wdt = (((DrWidth - ((NPanels - 2) + 6.75)) / NPanels) - 0.25)    'width
    Else
        Wdt = (((DrWidth - ((NPanels - 2) + 12.75)) / NPanels) - 0.25) 'width
    End If
    
    Area = 0
    If L21 > 0 And NPanels >= 2 Then
        H = 21 - 2.5
        If DrType = "T45" Then H = 21 - 4.75
        
        Area = Wdt * H * NPanels * L21
    End If
    If L24 > 0 And NPanels >= 2 Then
        H = 24 - 2.5
        If DrType = "T45" Then H = 24 - 4.75
        
        Area = Wdt * H * NPanels * L24 + Area
    End If
    
    'calculate glass weight
    If varDoorData.LiteType > 700000 Then 'double
        If (Mid(CStr(varDoorData.LiteType), 1, 1) = "9" Or Mid(CStr(varDoorData.LiteType), 1, 5) = "9") And _
            (Mid(CStr(varDoorData.LiteType), 3, 1) = "0") Then
            
            Wgt = Area * 0.0214    '1/2" sealed unit (glass by others, but can calc weight as if with glass)
            
        ElseIf Mid(CStr(varDoorData.LiteType), 3, 1) = "1" Or Mid(CStr(varDoorData.LiteType), 7, 1) = "1" Or _
            Mid(CStr(varDoorData.LiteType), 3, 1) = "2" Or Mid(CStr(varDoorData.LiteType), 7, 1) = "2" Or _
                Mid(CStr(varDoorData.LiteType), 3, 1) = "3" Or Mid(CStr(varDoorData.LiteType), 7, 1) = "3" Then
            
            Wgt = Area * 0.01194  '1/2" sealed unit acrylic or lexan or other: plastic
            
        Else
            Wgt = Area * 0.0214   '1/2" sealed unit
        End If
    Else
'        If varDoorData.LiteType > 9000 Then 'no glass
'                Wgt = 0
        If (varDoorData.LiteType > 7000 And varDoorData.LiteType < 7010) Or (varDoorData.LiteType > 8000 And varDoorData.LiteType < 8010) Or _
                (varDoorData.LiteType = 9001) Then  '1/8" glass by other, weight can be calculated
                Wgt = Area * 0.0098 '1/8" glass
        ElseIf (varDoorData.LiteType = 7010 Or varDoorData.LiteType = 8010) Or (varDoorData.LiteType = 8030) Or _
                (varDoorData.LiteType = 9011) Then  '1/8" acrylic by other, weight can be calculated
                Wgt = Area * 0.00507 '1/8" acrylic
        ElseIf (varDoorData.LiteType = 7020 Or varDoorData.LiteType = 8020) Or (varDoorData.LiteType = 8030) Or _
                (varDoorData.LiteType = 9011) Then  '1/8" lexan=acrylic & Other: Plastic
                Wgt = Area * 0.00507 '1/8" lexan
        ElseIf varDoorData.LiteType = 7101 Or varDoorData.LiteType = 7102 Or varDoorData.LiteType = 7103 Or varDoorData.LiteType = 7104 Or _
            varDoorData.LiteType = 8101 Or varDoorData.LiteType = 8102 Or varDoorData.LiteType = 8103 Or varDoorData.LiteType = 8104 Or _
            varDoorData.LiteType = 9101 Then        '1/4" glass by other, can be calculated
                Wgt = Area * 0.0196 '1/4" glass
        ElseIf varDoorData.LiteType = 7111 Or varDoorData.LiteType = 7120 Or varDoorData.LiteType = 7130 Or varDoorData.LiteType = 7140 Or _
            varDoorData.LiteType = 8110 Or varDoorData.LiteType = 8120 Or varDoorData.LiteType = 8130 Or varDoorData.LiteType = 8104 Or _
            varDoorData.LiteType = 9111 Then        '1/4" acrylic by other
                Wgt = Area * 0.01014 '1/4" acrylic or lexan
        End If
    End If
    
    'calculate retainers weight
    Area = 0
    H = 0
    If L21 > 0 Then
        H = 21 - 2.5
        If DrType = "T45" Then H = 21 - 4.75
        Area = (Wdt + H) * 2 * NPanels * L21
    End If
    If L24 > 0 Then
        H = 24 - 2.5
        If DrType = "T45" Then H = 24 - 4.75
        Area = (Wdt + H) * 2 * NPanels * L24 + Area
    End If
    If DrType = "T175" Or DrType = "T175 Multi-Rib" Or _
        DrType = "T45" Then ' same weight for A45 retainers
        Retainer = Area * 0.0108
    Else
        Retainer = Area * 0.0086
    End If
    
    'no part numbers associated with full view sections, calculate weight only for springing.
    CalcFullViewWgt = Retainer + Wgt + RailCenter + RailEnd + RailTop + RailBottom

    Exit Function

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Function

Public Function GetTopWeatherSeal(ByVal WType As Integer) As Single
    
    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".GetTopWeatherSeal()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""

    'take door width for retainers, door width + 6" for seals.
    Dim PN(2) As String, W As Single, QTY(2) As Integer, lsngP(2) As Single
    Dim PLen(3, 2) As Single
    Dim i As Single
    Dim T As Single
    Dim T2 As Single
    Dim P As Integer
    Dim M As String
    Dim MetLenSeal As Single
    Dim MetLenRet As Single
    Dim LenSeal As Single
    Dim LenRet As Single
    
    ReDim varDoorData.varTopRetainer(3)
    
    LenSeal = DrWidth + 6
    ClearConObj
    aCon.Inch = LenSeal
    aCon.mmcalc
    MetLenSeal = aCon.mm
    
    LenRet = DrWidth
    ClearConObj
    aCon.Inch = LenRet
    aCon.mmcalc
    MetLenRet = aCon.mm

    Select Case WType
    Case 1                  '3" top with retainer (door < 22'2" wide)
        'comes in 20'4" roll
        If MeasU = 1 Then
            If DrWidth < 244 Then
                Call FillSummaryGrid("328-260", LenRet, "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-260"), LenRet, "Cut to " & GetImpString(LenRet))
                varDoorData.varTopRetainer(0).RetLen(0) = LenRet
            ElseIf DrWidth = 244 Then
                Call FillSummaryGrid("328-260", LenRet, "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-260"), LenRet)
                varDoorData.varTopRetainer(0).RetLen(0) = LenRet
            Else
                Call FillSummaryGrid("328-260", LenRet, "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-260"), LenRet, "Cut (1) to " & GetImpString(LenRet - 244))
                varDoorData.varTopRetainer(0).RetLen(0) = LenRet
                varDoorData.varTopRetainer(0).RetLen(1) = LenRet - 244
            End If
        Else
            If DrWidth < 244 Then
                Call FillSummaryGrid("328-260", MetLenRet, "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-260"), LenRet, "Cut to " & Round(MetLenRet, 0) & " mm")
                varDoorData.varTopRetainer(0).RetLen(0) = LenRet
            ElseIf DrWidth = 244 Then
                Call FillSummaryGrid("328-260", MetLenRet, "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-260"), LenRet)
                varDoorData.varTopRetainer(0).RetLen(0) = LenRet
            Else
                Call FillSummaryGrid("328-260", MetLenRet, "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-260"), LenRet, "Cut (1) to " & Round(MetLenRet - 6198, 0) & " mm") '244" = 6198 mm
                varDoorData.varTopRetainer(0).RetLen(0) = LenRet
                varDoorData.varTopRetainer(0).RetLen(1) = LenRet - 244
            End If
        End If
        
        varDoorData.varTopRetainer(0).PN = "328-260"
        
        'comes in 100' roll
        
        If MeasU = 1 Then
            Call FillSummaryGrid("152-324", LenSeal, "Sections", GetSubCategory("152-324"), LenSeal, "Cut to " & GetImpString(LenSeal))
        Else
            Call FillSummaryGrid("152-324", MetLenSeal, "Sections", GetSubCategory("152-324"), LenSeal, "Cut to " & Round(MetLenSeal, 0) & " mm")
        End If
        
        varDoorData.varTopRetainer(1).PN = "152-324"
        varDoorData.varTopRetainer(1).RetLen(0) = LenSeal
        W = (LenRet * 3 / 244) + (LenSeal / 12 * 0.16) 'calculate cut weight
        
    Case 2                  '4" top with retainer (door >= 22'2" wide)
        'comes in 20'4" roll
        If MeasU = 1 Then
            If DrWidth < 244 Then
                Call FillSummaryGrid("328-260", LenRet, "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-260"), LenRet, "Cut to " & GetImpString(LenRet))
                varDoorData.varTopRetainer(0).RetLen(0) = LenRet
            ElseIf DrWidth = 244 Then
                Call FillSummaryGrid("328-260", LenRet, "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-260"), LenRet)
                varDoorData.varTopRetainer(0).RetLen(0) = LenRet
            Else
                Call FillSummaryGrid("328-260", LenRet, "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-260"), LenRet, "Cut (1) to " & GetImpString(LenRet - 244))
                varDoorData.varTopRetainer(0).RetLen(0) = LenRet
                varDoorData.varTopRetainer(0).RetLen(1) = LenRet - 244
            End If
        Else
            If DrWidth < 244 Then
                Call FillSummaryGrid("328-260", MetLenRet, "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-260"), LenRet, "Cut to " & Round(MetLenRet, 0) & " mm")
                varDoorData.varTopRetainer(0).RetLen(0) = LenRet
            ElseIf DrWidth = 244 Then
                Call FillSummaryGrid("328-260", MetLenRet, "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-260"), LenRet)
                varDoorData.varTopRetainer(0).RetLen(0) = LenRet
            Else
                Call FillSummaryGrid("328-260", MetLenRet, "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-260"), LenRet, "Cut (1) to " & Round(MetLenRet - 6198, 0) & " mm") '244" = 6198 mm
                varDoorData.varTopRetainer(0).RetLen(0) = LenRet
                varDoorData.varTopRetainer(0).RetLen(1) = LenRet - 244
            End If
        End If
        varDoorData.varTopRetainer(0).PN = "328-260"
        'comes in 100' roll
        If MeasU = 1 Then
            Call FillSummaryGrid("152-323", LenSeal, "Sections", GetSubCategory("152-323"), LenSeal, "Cut to " & GetImpString(LenSeal))
        Else
            Call FillSummaryGrid("152-323", MetLenSeal, "Sections", GetSubCategory("152-323"), LenSeal, "Cut to " & Round(MetLenSeal, 0) & " mm")
        End If
        varDoorData.varTopRetainer(1).PN = "152-323"
        varDoorData.varTopRetainer(1).RetLen(0) = LenSeal
        W = (LenRet * 3 / 244) + (LenSeal / 12 * 0.2) 'calculate cut weight
        
    Case 3                  'one piece top (dual durometer) 1 3/4"
        'comes in 24'4" roll
        
        W = LenRet * 3 / 292
        
        If MeasU = 1 Then
            Call FillSummaryGrid("328-261", LenRet, "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-261"), LenRet, IIf(LenRet = 292, "", "Cut to " & GetImpString(LenRet)))
        Else
            Call FillSummaryGrid("328-261", MetLenRet, "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-261"), LenRet, IIf(LenRet = 292, "", "Cut to " & Round(MetLenRet, 0) & " mm"))
        End If
        varDoorData.varTopRetainer(0).PN = "328-261"
        varDoorData.varTopRetainer(0).RetLen(0) = LenRet
    Case 4                  'one piece top (dual durometer) 4"
        'comes in 24'4" roll
        W = LenRet * 3 / 292
        
        If MeasU = 1 Then
            Call FillSummaryGrid("328-262", LenRet, "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-262"), LenRet, IIf(LenRet = 292, "", "Cut to " & GetImpString(LenRet)))
        Else
            Call FillSummaryGrid("328-262", MetLenRet, "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-262"), LenRet, IIf(LenRet = 292, "", "Cut to " & Round(MetLenRet, 0) & " mm"))
        End If
        varDoorData.varTopRetainer(0).PN = "328-262"
        varDoorData.varTopRetainer(0).RetLen(0) = LenRet
    Case 5                  ' T150  -   channel only
        PN(0) = "432-0015"
        
        QTY(0) = RoundUp(Round((DrWidth - 1.5) / 120, 3))
'        If DrWidth - 1.5 <= 120 Then
'            QTY(0) = 1
'        ElseIf DrWidth - 1.5 > 120 And DrWidth - 1.5 < 240 Then
'            QTY(0) = 2
'        Else
'            QTY(0) = 3
'        End If
        
        If QTY(0) = 1 Then
            PLen(0, 0) = DrWidth - 1.5
            PLen(1, 0) = 0
        Else
            PLen(0, 0) = 120
            PLen(1, 0) = DrWidth - 1.5 - (120 * (QTY(0) - 1))
        End If
            
        If (PLen(0, 0) = 120 And PLen(1, 0) = 120) Or (PLen(0, 0) = 120 And PLen(1, 0) = 0) Then
            Call FillSummaryGrid(PN(0), QTY(0), "Sections", GetSubCategory(PN(0)), 0)
        Else
            If MeasU = 1 Then
                If QTY(0) = 1 Then
                    Call FillSummaryGrid(PN(0), QTY(0), "Sections", GetSubCategory(PN(0)), 0, "Cut to " & GetImpString(PLen(0, 0)))
                Else
                   Call FillSummaryGrid(PN(0), QTY(0), "Sections", GetSubCategory(PN(0)), 0, "Cut (1) to " & GetImpString(PLen(1, 0)))
                End If
            Else
                ClearConObj
                If QTY(0) = 1 Then
                    aCon.Inch = PLen(0, 0)
                    aCon.mmcalc
                    PLen(0, 0) = aCon.mm
                    Call FillSummaryGrid(PN(0), QTY(0), "Sections", GetSubCategory(PN(0)), 0, "Cut to " & Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm")
                Else
                    aCon.Inch = PLen(1, 0)
                    aCon.mmcalc
                    PLen(0, 0) = aCon.mm
                    Call FillSummaryGrid(PN(0), QTY(0), "Sections", GetSubCategory(PN(0)), 0, "Cut (1) to " & Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm")
                End If
            End If
        End If
        
        W = (DrWidth - 1.5) * (8.33 / 120)

    Case 6              ' T150  -   aluminum retainer only
        
        If MeasU = 1 Then
            If DrWidth = 294 Then
                Call FillSummaryGrid("328-215", LenRet, "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-215"), LenRet) ', "Cut to " & GetImpString(LenRet))
            ElseIf DrWidth > 294 Then
                Call FillSummaryGrid("328-215", LenRet, "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-215"), LenRet, "Cut (1) to " & GetImpString(LenRet - 294))
            Else
                Call FillSummaryGrid("328-215", LenRet, "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-215"), LenRet, "Cut to " & GetImpString(LenRet))
            End If
        Else
            If DrWidth = 294 Then
                Call FillSummaryGrid("328-215", MetLenRet, "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-215"), LenRet) ', "Cut to " & Round(MetLenRet, 0) & " mm")
            ElseIf DrWidth > 294 Then
                Call FillSummaryGrid("328-215", MetLenRet, "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-215"), LenRet, "Cut (1) to " & Round(MetLenRet - 7467.6, 0) & " mm")
            Else
                Call FillSummaryGrid("328-215", MetLenRet, "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-215"), LenRet, "Cut to " & Round(MetLenRet, 0) & " mm")
            End If
        End If
        varDoorData.varTopRetainer(0).PN = "328-215"
        varDoorData.varTopRetainer(0).RetLen(0) = LenRet
        W = LenRet * 8.5 / 294

    Case 7                  'T175/T175-20   -   channel only
        PN(0) = "432-0014"
        
        QTY(0) = RoundUp(Round((DrWidth - 1.5) / 120, 3))
'        If DrWidth - 1.5 <= 120 Then
'            QTY(0) = 1
'        ElseIf DrWidth - 1.5 > 120 And DrWidth - 1.5 < 360 Then
'            QTY(0) = 2
'        Else
'            QTY(0) = 3
'        End If
        
        If QTY(0) = 1 Then
            PLen(0, 0) = DrWidth - 1.5
            PLen(1, 0) = 0
        Else
            PLen(0, 0) = 120
            PLen(1, 0) = DrWidth - 1.5 - (120 * (QTY(0) - 1))
        End If
            
        If (PLen(0, 0) = 120 And PLen(1, 0) = 120) Or (PLen(0, 0) = 120 And PLen(1, 0) = 0) Then
            Call FillSummaryGrid(PN(0), QTY(0), "Sections", GetSubCategory(PN(0)), 0)
        Else
            If MeasU = 1 Then
                If QTY(0) = 1 Then
                    Call FillSummaryGrid(PN(0), QTY(0), "Sections", GetSubCategory(PN(0)), 0, "Cut to " & GetImpString(PLen(0, 0)))
                Else
                   Call FillSummaryGrid(PN(0), QTY(0), "Sections", GetSubCategory(PN(0)), 0, "Cut (1) to " & GetImpString(PLen(1, 0)))
                End If
            Else
                ClearConObj
                If QTY(0) = 1 Then
                    aCon.Inch = PLen(0, 0)
                    aCon.mmcalc
                    PLen(0, 0) = aCon.mm
                    Call FillSummaryGrid(PN(0), QTY(0), "Sections", GetSubCategory(PN(0)), 0, "Cut to " & Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm")
                Else
                    aCon.Inch = PLen(1, 0)
                    aCon.mmcalc
                    PLen(0, 0) = aCon.mm
                    Call FillSummaryGrid(PN(0), QTY(0), "Sections", GetSubCategory(PN(0)), 0, "Cut (1) to " & Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm")
                End If
            End If
        End If
        
        W = (DrWidth - 1.5) * 8.85 / 120

    Case 8                 'T175/T175-20/T175A   -   aluminum retainer only
        If MeasU = 1 Then
            If DrWidth = 294 Then
                Call FillSummaryGrid("328-216", LenRet, "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-216"), LenRet)
            ElseIf DrWidth > 294 Then
                Call FillSummaryGrid("328-216", LenRet, "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-216"), LenRet, "Cut (1) to " & GetImpString(LenRet - 294))
            Else
                Call FillSummaryGrid("328-216", LenRet, "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-216"), LenRet, "Cut to " & GetImpString(LenRet))
            End If
        Else
            If DrWidth = 294 Then
                Call FillSummaryGrid("328-216", MetLenRet, "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-216"), LenRet)
            ElseIf DrWidth > 294 Then
                Call FillSummaryGrid("328-216", MetLenRet, "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-216"), LenRet, "Cut (1) to " & Round(MetLenRet - 7467.6, 0) & " mm")
            Else
                Call FillSummaryGrid("328-216", MetLenRet, "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-216"), LenRet, "Cut to " & Round(MetLenRet, 0) & " mm")
            End If
        End If
        W = LenRet * 9 / 294
        varDoorData.varTopRetainer(0).PN = "328-216"
        varDoorData.varTopRetainer(0).RetLen(0) = LenRet
        
    Case 9          'T200/ADV200/T200A/T200A Multi-Rib/ADV200 MR/T200 MR  -   'Galv. MRSB & channel only
        
        Dim LenExt As Double    'stores the imperial value for calculation
        Dim LenInt As Double
        Dim LenExt1 As Double   'stores the metric value for display purposes
        Dim LenInt1 As Double
        Dim PNAngle(3) As String
        Dim QtyAngle(3) As Integer
        Dim ldblCutExt As Double, ldblCutInt As Double 'cut to values
        Dim W71 As Double, W72 As Double, W73 As Double, W74 As Double
        Dim lstrCutInstr As String
        
        LenExt = DrWidth - 1.5 'length of the exterior piece
        LenInt = DrWidth - IIf(varDoorData.DblEndCaps = True, 12, 6) 'length of the interior piece
        
        W71 = 6.33
        W72 = 5.11
        W73 = 4.89
        W74 = 3.67
        
        Select Case LenExt
        Case Is <= 72
            'lenint cannot be equal to 72 being shorter than lenext
            'pick (2) "421-0074", one for exterior, one for interior
            If MeasU = 1 Then
                Call FillSummaryGrid("421-0074", 2, "Sections", GetSubCategory("421-0074"), 0, IIf(LenExt = 72, "Cut (1) to " & GetImpString(LenInt), "Cut (1) to " & GetImpString(LenInt) & _
                    " (1) to " & GetImpString(LenExt)))
            Else
                ClearConObj
                aCon.Inch = LenExt
                aCon.mmcalc
                LenExt1 = aCon.mm
                
                ClearConObj
                aCon.Inch = LenInt
                aCon.mmcalc
                
                Call FillSummaryGrid("421-0074", 2, "Sections", GetSubCategory("421-0074"), 0, IIf(LenExt = 72, ("Cut (1) to " & Round(aCon.mm, 0) & "mm"), ("Cut (1) to " & Round(aCon.mm, 0) & "mm" & _
                    " (1) to " & Round(LenExt1, 0) & "mm")))
            End If
            W = ((W74 / 72) * LenExt) + ((W74 / 72) * LenInt)
            
        Case Is <= 96
            'if interior is <=72, then it falls into the previous group.
            'pick (1) "421-0074" for interior and (1) "421-0073" for exterior
            'else pick (2) "421-0073".
            
            If LenInt <= 72 Then
                If MeasU = 1 Then
                    Call FillSummaryGrid("421-0074", 1, "Sections", GetSubCategory("421-0074"), 0, IIf(LenInt = 72, "", "Cut to " & GetImpString(LenInt)))
                    Call FillSummaryGrid("421-0073", 1, "Sections", GetSubCategory("421-0073"), 0, "Cut to " & GetImpString(LenExt))
                Else
                    ClearConObj
                    aCon.Inch = LenInt
                    aCon.mmcalc
                    Call FillSummaryGrid("421-0074", 1, "Sections", GetSubCategory("421-0074"), 0, IIf(LenInt = 72, "", "Cut to " & Round(aCon.mm, 0) & "mm"))
                    
                    ClearConObj
                    aCon.Inch = LenExt
                    aCon.mmcalc

                        " (1) to " & GetImpString(120 - (360 - LenExt))))
                Else
                    ClearConObj
                    aCon.Inch = 120 - (360 - LenExt)
                    aCon.mmcalc
                    LenExt1 = aCon.mm
                    
                    ClearConObj
                    aCon.Inch = 120 - (360 - LenInt)
                    aCon.mmcalc
                    Call FillSummaryGrid("421-0072", 6, "Sections", GetSubCategory("421-0072"), 0, IIf(LenExt = 360, ("Cut (1) to " & Round(aCon.mm, 0) & "mm"), ("Cut (1) to " & Round(aCon.mm, 0) & "mm" & _
                        " (1) to " & Round(LenExt1, 0) & "mm")))
                End If
                W = ((W72 / 120) * (120 - (360 - LenInt))) + ((W72 / 120) * (120 - (360 - LenExt))) + (W72 * 4)
            End If
        Case Is <= 384
            If LenInt <= 360 Then
                If MeasU = 1 Then
                    Call FillSummaryGrid("421-0072", 5, "Sections", GetSubCategory("421-0072"), 0, IIf(LenInt = 360, "", "Cut (1) to " & GetImpString(120 - (384 - LenInt))))
                    Call FillSummaryGrid("421-0071", 1, "Sections", GetSubCategory("421-0071"), 0, "Cut to " & 144 - (384 - LenExt))
                Else
                    ClearConObj
                    aCon.Inch = 120 - (384 - LenInt)
                    aCon.mmcalc
                    LenInt1 = aCon.mm
                    
                    ClearConObj
                    aCon.Inch = 144 - (384 - LenExt)
                    aCon.mmcalc
                    Call FillSummaryGrid("421-0072", 5, "Sections", GetSubCategory("421-0072"), 0, IIf(LenInt = 360, "", "Cut (1) to " & Round(LenInt1, 0) & "mm"))
                End If
                W = ((W72 / 120) * (120 - (384 - LenInt))) + ((W71 / 144) * (144 - (384 - LenExt))) + (W72 * 4)
            Else
                If MeasU = 1 Then
                    Call FillSummaryGrid("421-0071", 2, "Sections", GetSubCategory("421-0071"), 0, IIf(LenExt = 384, "Cut (1) to " & GetImpString(144 - (384 - LenInt)), "Cut (1) to " & GetImpString(144 - (384 - LenInt)) & _
                        " (1) to " & GetImpString(144 - (384 - LenExt))))
                Else
                    ClearConObj
                    aCon.Inch = 144 - (384 - LenExt)
                    aCon.mmcalc
                    LenExt1 = aCon.mm
                    
                    ClearConObj
                    aCon.Inch = 144 - (384 - LenInt)
                    aCon.mmcalc
                    Call FillSummaryGrid("421-0071", 2, "Sections", GetSubCategory("421-0071"), 0, IIf(LenExt = 384, ("Cut (1) to " & Round(aCon.mm, 0) & "mm"), ("Cut (1) to " & Round(aCon.mm, 0) & "mm" & _
                        " (1) to " & Round(LenExt1, 0) & "mm")))
                End If
                Call FillSummaryGrid("421-0072", 4, "Sections", GetSubCategory("421-0072"), 0)
                W = ((W71 / 144) * (144 - (384 - LenInt))) + ((W71 / 144) * (144 - (384 - LenExt))) + (W72 * 4)
            End If
        End Select
        
    Case 10             'T200/ADV200/T200A/T200A Multi-Rib/ADV200 MR/T200 MR  -   aluminum retainer only + passdoor option (varpassdorr.top = 2)
        
        If MeasU = 1 Then
            If DrWidth = 294 Then
                Call FillSummaryGrid("328-217", LenRet, "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-217"), LenRet)
            ElseIf DrWidth > 294 Then
                Call FillSummaryGrid("328-217", LenRet, "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-217"), LenRet, "Cut (1) to " & GetImpString(LenRet - 294))
            Else
                Call FillSummaryGrid("328-217", LenRet, "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-217"), LenRet, "Cut to " & GetImpString(LenRet))
            End If
        Else
            If DrWidth = 294 Then
                Call FillSummaryGrid("328-217", MetLenRet, "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-217"), LenRet)
            ElseIf DrWidth > 294 Then
                Call FillSummaryGrid("328-217", MetLenRet, "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-217"), LenRet, "Cut (1) to " & Round(MetLenRet - 7467.6, 0) & " mm")
            Else
                Call FillSummaryGrid("328-217", MetLenRet, "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-217"), LenRet, "Cut to " & Round(MetLenRet, 0) & " mm")
            End If
        End If
        W = LenRet * 9.114 / 294
        
        varDoorData.varTopRetainer(0).PN = "328-217"
        varDoorData.varTopRetainer(0).RetLen(0) = LenRet
        
    Case 11 'passdoor option B
        
        W = (DrWidth - 2) * (4.86 / 122)
        
        QTY(0) = RoundUp((DrWidth - 2) / 122)
        
        If MeasU = 1 Then
            If QTY(0) = 1 Then
                Call FillSummaryGrid("328-648", QTY(0), "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-648"), 0, "Cut to " & GetImpString(DrWidth - 2))
            Else
                Call FillSummaryGrid("328-648", QTY(0), "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-648"), 0, "Cut (1) to " & GetImpString((DrWidth - 2) - 122))
            End If
        Else
            If QTY(0) = 1 Then
                ClearConObj
                aCon.Inch = DrWidth - 2
                aCon.mmcalc
                Call FillSummaryGrid("328-648", QTY(0), "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-648"), 0, "Cut to " & Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm")
            Else
                ClearConObj
                aCon.Inch = (DrWidth - 2) - 122
                aCon.mmcalc
                Call FillSummaryGrid("328-648", QTY(0), "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-648"), 0, "Cut (1) to " & Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm")
            End If
        End If
        
    Case 12 'passdoor option A
    
        If DrWidth <= 122 Then
            If MeasU = 1 Then
                Call FillSummaryGrid("328-653", 1, "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-653"), 0, "Cut to " & GetImpString(DrWidth - 2))
            Else
                ClearConObj
                aCon.Inch = DrWidth - 2
                aCon.mmcalc
                Call FillSummaryGrid("328-653", 1, "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-653"), 0, "Cut to " & Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm")
            End If
            W = (DrWidth - 2) * 5.14 / 122
        Else
            If MeasU = 1 Then
                Call FillSummaryGrid("328-654", DrWidth - 2, "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-654"), DrWidth - 2, "Cut to " & GetImpString(DrWidth - 2))
            Else
                ClearConObj
                aCon.Inch = DrWidth - 2
                aCon.mmcalc
                Call FillSummaryGrid("328-654", aCon.mm, "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-654"), DrWidth - 2, "Cut to " & Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm")
            End If
            varDoorData.varTopRetainer(0).PN = "328-654"
            varDoorData.varTopRetainer(0).RetLen(0) = DrWidth - 2
            W = (DrWidth - 2) * (12.5 / 288)
        End If
    
    Case 13 'T45: One piece top retainer and seal 'copy from case 3
        W = LenRet * 3 / 294    'stock length has been changed to 24'-6"
        
        If MeasU = 1 Then
            Call FillSummaryGrid("328-269", LenRet, "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-269"), LenRet, IIf(LenRet = 292, "", "Cut to " & GetImpString(LenRet)))
        Else
            Call FillSummaryGrid("328-269", MetLenRet, "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-269"), LenRet, IIf(LenRet = 292, "", "Cut to " & Round(MetLenRet, 0) & " mm"))
        End If
        varDoorData.varTopRetainer(0).PN = "328-269"
        varDoorData.varTopRetainer(0).RetLen(0) = LenRet
    
    Case 14 'pass door option A: T45
        If DrWidth <= 122 Then
            If MeasU = 1 Then
                Call FillSummaryGrid("FP0-0089", 1, "Sections", GetSubCategory("FP0-0089"), 0, "Cut to " & GetImpString(DrWidth - 2))
            Else
                ClearConObj
                aCon.Inch = DrWidth - 2
                aCon.mmcalc
                Call FillSummaryGrid("FP0-0089", 1, "Sections", GetSubCategory("FP0-0089"), 0, "Cut to " & Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm")
            End If
            W = (DrWidth - 2) * 5.14 / 122
        Else
            If MeasU = 1 Then
                Call FillSummaryGrid("FP0-0090", DrWidth - 2, "Sections", GetSubCategory("FP0-0090"), DrWidth - 2, "Cut to " & GetImpString(DrWidth - 2))
            Else
                ClearConObj
                aCon.Inch = DrWidth - 2
                aCon.mmcalc
                Call FillSummaryGrid("FP0-0090", aCon.mm, "Sections", GetSubCategory("FP0-0090"), DrWidth - 2, "Cut to " & Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm")
            End If
            varDoorData.varTopRetainer(0).PN = "FP0-0090"
            varDoorData.varTopRetainer(0).RetLen(0) = DrWidth - 2
            W = (DrWidth - 2) * (12.5 / 288)
        End If
    End Select
    
    GetTopWeatherSeal = W
    
    Exit Function

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Function

Public Function GetBottomWeatherSeal(ByVal WType As Integer, ByVal lblnLowTemp As Boolean) As Single

    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".GetBottomWeatherSeal()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""

    Dim PN(3) As String, W As Single, QTY(3) As Single, PLen(3, 2) As Single
    Dim i As Integer, T As Single, T2 As Single
    Dim X As Integer
    Dim M As String
    Dim P As Integer
    Dim lsngTempW As Single
    Dim lsngTempWeight As Single
    Dim MetLenSeal As Single
    Dim MetLenRet As Single
    Dim lbncheck As Boolean
    Dim lstrSealPN As String
    Dim lsngSealWgt As Single
    Dim LenSeal As Single
    Dim LenRet As Single
    
    ReDim varDoorData.varBtmRetainer(3)
    
'    If DrType = "T45" Then
'
'        If lblnLowTemp = True Then
'            lstrSealPN = "152-318"
'            lsngSealWgt = 0.12 / 12 'per inch
'            LenSeal = DrWidth + 6
'
'            ClearConObj
'            aCon.Inch = DrWidth + 6
'            aCon.mmcalc
'            MetLenSeal = aCon.mm
'
'        Else
'            lstrSealPN = "152-312"
'            lsngSealWgt = 23.5 / 1200 'per inch
'            LenSeal = DrWidth
'
'            ClearConObj
'            aCon.Inch = DrWidth
'            aCon.mmcalc
'            MetLenSeal = aCon.mm
'        End If
'
'        LenRet = DrWidth
'
'        ClearConObj
'        aCon.Inch = DrWidth
'        aCon.mmcalc
'        MetLenSeal = aCon.mm
'
'    Else
'        If lblnLowTemp = True Then
'            lstrSealPN = "152-318"
'            lsngSealWgt = 0.12 / 12 'per inch
'        Else
'            lstrSealPN = "152-319"
'            lsngSealWgt = 0.12 / 12 'per inch
'        End If
'
'        LenSeal = DrWidth + 6
'        LenRet = DrWidth
'
'        ClearConObj
'        aCon.Inch = DrWidth + 6
'        aCon.mmcalc
'        MetLenSeal = aCon.mm
'
'        ClearConObj
'        aCon.Inch = DrWidth
'        aCon.mmcalc
'        MetLenRet = aCon.mm
'    End If
    
    'take door width for retainers, door width + 6" for seals.
    
    If lblnLowTemp = True Then
        lstrSealPN = "152-318"
        lsngSealWgt = 0.12 / 12 'per inch
        LenSeal = DrWidth + 6
        
'        ClearConObj
'        aCon.Inch = DrWidth + 6
'        aCon.mmcalc
'        MetLenSeal = aCon.mm
    Else
        If DrType = "T35" Then
            lstrSealPN = "152-313"
            lsngSealWgt = 24 / 2400 'per inch
            LenSeal = DrWidth

'            ClearConObj
'            aCon.Inch = DrWidth
'            aCon.mmcalc
'            MetLenSeal = aCon.mm
            
        ElseIf DrType = "T45" Then
            If CECompliant = 1 Then 'Future options, upgrade (heavy duty) downgrade (w/motor operator)
                lstrSealPN = "152-312"
                lsngSealWgt = 23.5 / 1200 'per inch
                LenSeal = DrWidth
                
'                ClearConObj
'                aCon.Inch = DrWidth
'                aCon.mmcalc
'                MetLenSeal = aCon.mm
            Else    'w/o CE
                lstrSealPN = "152-319"
                lsngSealWgt = 0.12 / 12 'per inch
                LenSeal = DrWidth + 6
                
'                ClearConObj
'                aCon.Inch = DrWidth + 6
'                aCon.mmcalc
'                MetLenSeal = aCon.mm
            End If
            
            
        ElseIf DrType = "T300" Or DrType = "T300 Multi-Rib" Then  'Btm seal

            lstrSealPN = "152-318"
            lsngSealWgt = 0.12 / 12 'per inch
            LenSeal = DrWidth + 6

        End If
        
    ClearConObj
    aCon.Inch = LenSeal
    aCon.mmcalc
    MetLenSeal = aCon.mm
      
        
    End If
    

    
    
    
    'retainer
    LenRet = DrWidth
    ClearConObj
    aCon.Inch = DrWidth
    aCon.mmcalc
    MetLenRet = aCon.mm
    
    If WType = 10 Then  ' for T200/ADV200/T200A/T200A Multi-Rib/ADV200 Multi-Rib/T200 Multi-Rib for that type assume type 5 plus additional code at the end
        WType = 11
        lbncheck = True
    End If
    
    Select Case WType
    Case 1                  'bottom seal w/ PVC retainer
        If DrType = "T150" Or DrType = "T150 Multi-Rib" Then
            'comes in 24'4" roll, .147 lb per lin ft.
            W = LenRet * (4 / 292)  'calculate cut weight
            If MeasU = 1 Then
                Call FillSummaryGrid("328-266", LenRet, "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-266"), LenRet, "Cut to " & GetImpString(LenRet))
            Else
                Call FillSummaryGrid("328-266", MetLenRet, "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-266"), LenRet, "Cut to " & Round(MetLenRet, 0) & " mm")
            End If
            varDoorData.varBtmRetainer(0).PN = "328-266"
            varDoorData.varBtmRetainer(0).RetLen(0) = LenRet
        Else
            'comes in 24'4" roll, .164 lb per lin ft.
            W = (LenSeal * lsngSealWgt) + (LenRet * (3 / 244))  'calculate cut weight
            If MeasU = 1 Then
                Call FillSummaryGrid("328-265", LenRet, "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-265"), LenRet, "Cut to " & GetImpString(LenRet))
                Call FillSummaryGrid(lstrSealPN, LenSeal, "Sections", GetSubCategory(lstrSealPN), LenSeal, "Cut to " & GetImpString(LenSeal))
            Else
                Call FillSummaryGrid("328-265", MetLenRet, "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-265"), LenRet, "Cut to " & Round(MetLenRet, 0) & " mm")
                Call FillSummaryGrid(lstrSealPN, MetLenSeal, "Sections", GetSubCategory(lstrSealPN), LenSeal, "Cut to " & Round(MetLenSeal, 0) & " mm")
            End If
            varDoorData.varBtmRetainer(0).PN = "328-265"
            varDoorData.varBtmRetainer(0).RetLen(0) = LenRet
            varDoorData.varBtmRetainer(1).PN = lstrSealPN
            varDoorData.varBtmRetainer(1).RetLen(0) = LenSeal
        End If
            
    Case 2                    'bottom seal w/ Aluminum retainer
                              
        Select Case DrType
            Case "T150", "T150 Multi-Rib"
                'comes in 24'6" roll
                W = (LenRet * (8.5 / 294)) 'calculate cut weight
                
                If MeasU = 1 Then
                    Call FillSummaryGrid("328-215", LenRet, "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-215"), LenRet, "Cut to " & GetImpString(LenRet))
                Else
                    Call FillSummaryGrid("328-215", MetLenRet, "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-215"), LenRet, "Cut to " & Round(MetLenRet, 0) & " mm")
                End If
                varDoorData.varBtmRetainer(0).PN = "328-215"
                varDoorData.varBtmRetainer(0).RetLen(0) = LenRet
                
            Case "T138", "T140" 'add retainers from db, of appr. length and use the same seals as for T150.
                lsngTempW = DrWidth - 0.25
                QTY(0) = 1
                Select Case lsngTempW
                Case Is <= 95.75
                    W = LenRet * 1.968 / 95.75
                    If MeasU = 1 Then
                        Call FillSummaryGrid("328-315", 1, "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-315"), 0, "Cut to " & GetImpString(LenRet))
                    Else
                        Call FillSummaryGrid("328-315", 1, "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-315"), 0, "Cut to " & Round(MetLenRet, 0) & " mm")
                    End If
                    varDoorData.varBtmRetainer(0).PN = "328-315"
                    varDoorData.varBtmRetainer(0).RetLen(0) = LenRet
                Case Is <= 107.75
                    W = LenRet * 2.214 / 107.75
                    If MeasU = 1 Then
                        Call FillSummaryGrid("328-316", 1, "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-316"), 0, "Cut to " & GetImpString(LenRet))
                    Else
                        Call FillSummaryGrid("328-316", 1, "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-316"), 0, "Cut to " & Round(MetLenRet, 0) & " mm")
                    End If
                    varDoorData.varBtmRetainer(0).PN = "328-316"
                    varDoorData.varBtmRetainer(0).RetLen(0) = LenRet
                Case Is <= 119.75
                    W = LenRet * 2.46 / 119.75
                    If MeasU = 1 Then
                        Call FillSummaryGrid("328-317", 1, "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-317"), 0, "Cut to " & GetImpString(LenRet))
                    Else
                        Call FillSummaryGrid("328-317", 1, "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-317"), 0, "Cut to " & Round(MetLenRet, 0) & " mm")
                    End If
                    varDoorData.varBtmRetainer(0).PN = "328-317"
                    varDoorData.varBtmRetainer(0).RetLen(0) = LenRet
                Case Is <= 143.75
                    W = LenRet * 2.952 / 143.75
                    If MeasU = 1 Then
                        Call FillSummaryGrid("328-318", 1, "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-318"), 0, "Cut to " & GetImpString(LenRet))
                    Else
                        Call FillSummaryGrid("328-318", 1, "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-318"), 0, "Cut to " & Round(MetLenRet, 0) & " mm")
                    End If
                    varDoorData.varBtmRetainer(0).PN = "328-318"
                    varDoorData.varBtmRetainer(0).RetLen(0) = LenRet
                Case Is <= 167.75
                    W = LenRet * 3.444 / 167.75
                    If MeasU = 1 Then
                        Call FillSummaryGrid("328-319", 1, "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-319"), 0, "Cut to " & GetImpString(LenRet))
                    Else
                        Call FillSummaryGrid("328-319", 1, "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-319"), 0, "Cut to " & Round(MetLenRet, 0) & " mm")
                    End If
                    varDoorData.varBtmRetainer(0).PN = "328-319"
                    varDoorData.varBtmRetainer(0).RetLen(0) = LenRet
                Case Is <= 191.75
                    W = LenRet * 3.936 / 191.75
                    If MeasU = 1 Then
                        Call FillSummaryGrid("328-320", 1, "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-320"), 0, "Cut to " & GetImpString(LenRet))
                    Else
                        Call FillSummaryGrid("328-320", 1, "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-320"), 0, "Cut to " & Round(MetLenRet, 0) & " mm")
                    End If
                    varDoorData.varBtmRetainer(0).PN = "328-320"
                    varDoorData.varBtmRetainer(0).RetLen(0) = LenRet
                Case Is <= 215.75
                    W = LenRet * 4.428 / 215.75
                    If MeasU = 1 Then
                        Call FillSummaryGrid("328-321", 1, "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-321"), 0, "Cut to " & GetImpString(LenRet))
                    Else
                        Call FillSummaryGrid("328-321", 1, "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-321"), 0, "Cut to " & Round(MetLenRet, 0) & " mm")
                    End If
                    varDoorData.varBtmRetainer(0).PN = "328-321"
                    varDoorData.varBtmRetainer(0).RetLen(0) = LenRet
                Case Is <= 313
                    W = LenRet * (6.396 / 313)
                    If MeasU = 1 Then
                        Call FillSummaryGrid("328-322", LenRet, "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-322"), LenRet, "Cut to " & GetImpString(LenRet))
                    Else
                        Call FillSummaryGrid("328-322", MetLenRet, "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-322"), LenRet, "Cut to " & Round(MetLenRet, 0) & " mm")
                    End If
                    varDoorData.varBtmRetainer(0).PN = "328-322"
                    varDoorData.varBtmRetainer(0).RetLen(0) = LenRet
                Case Else
                    MsgBox "Unable to find bottom retainer for that width." & vbCrLf & _
                        "Please contact product manufacturer.", vbExclamation + vbApplicationModal + vbOKOnly, "Bottom Retainer"
                    Exit Function
                End Select
            Case "T165" 'add retainers from db, of appr. length and use the same seals as for T175.
                lsngTempW = DrWidth - 0.25
                Select Case lsngTempW
                Case Is <= 95.75
                    W = LenRet * 2.088 / 95.75
                    If MeasU = 1 Then
                        Call FillSummaryGrid("328-325", 1, "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-325"), 0, "Cut to " & GetImpString(LenRet))
                    Else
                        Call FillSummaryGrid("328-325", 1, "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-325"), 0, "Cut to " & Round(MetLenRet, 0) & " mm")
                    End If
                    varDoorData.varBtmRetainer(0).PN = "328-325"
                    varDoorData.varBtmRetainer(0).RetLen(0) = LenRet
                Case Is <= 107.75
                    W = LenRet * 2.088 / 107.75
                    If MeasU = 1 Then
                        Call FillSummaryGrid("328-326", 1, "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-326"), 0, "Cut to " & GetImpString(LenRet))
                    Else
                        Call FillSummaryGrid("328-326", 1, "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-326"), 0, "Cut to " & Round(MetLenRet, 0) & " mm")
                    End If
                    varDoorData.varBtmRetainer(0).PN = "328-326"
                    varDoorData.varBtmRetainer(0).RetLen(0) = LenRet
                Case Is <= 119.75
                    W = LenRet * 2.61 / 119.75
                    If MeasU = 1 Then
                        Call FillSummaryGrid("328-327", 1, "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-327"), 0, "Cut to " & GetImpString(LenRet))
                    Else
                        Call FillSummaryGrid("328-327", 1, "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-327"), 0, "Cut to " & Round(MetLenRet, 0) & " mm")
                    End If
                    varDoorData.varBtmRetainer(0).PN = "328-327"
                    varDoorData.varBtmRetainer(0).RetLen(0) = LenRet
                Case Is <= 143.75
                    W = LenRet * 3.132 / 143.75
                    If MeasU = 1 Then
                        Call FillSummaryGrid("328-328", 1, "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-328"), 0, "Cut to " & GetImpString(LenRet))
                    Else
                        Call FillSummaryGrid("328-328", 1, "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-328"), 0, "Cut to " & Round(MetLenRet, 0) & " mm")
                    End If
                    varDoorData.varBtmRetainer(0).PN = "328-328"
                    varDoorData.varBtmRetainer(0).RetLen(0) = LenRet
                Case Is <= 167.75
                    W = LenRet * 3.654 / 167.75
                    If MeasU = 1 Then
                        Call FillSummaryGrid("328-329", 1, "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-329"), 0, "Cut to " & GetImpString(LenRet))
                    Else
                        Call FillSummaryGrid("328-329", 1, "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-329"), 0, "Cut to " & Round(MetLenRet, 0) & " mm")
                    End If
                    varDoorData.varBtmRetainer(0).PN = "328-329"
                    varDoorData.varBtmRetainer(0).RetLen(0) = LenRet
                Case Is <= 191.75
                    W = LenRet * 4.176 / 191.75
                    If MeasU = 1 Then
                        Call FillSummaryGrid("328-330", 1, "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-330"), 0, "Cut to " & GetImpString(LenRet))
                    Else
                        Call FillSummaryGrid("328-330", 1, "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-330"), 0, "Cut to " & Round(MetLenRet, 0) & " mm")
                    End If
                    varDoorData.varBtmRetainer(0).PN = "328-330"
                    varDoorData.varBtmRetainer(0).RetLen(0) = LenRet
                Case Is <= 215.75
                    W = LenRet * 4.698 / 215.75
                    If MeasU = 1 Then
                        Call FillSummaryGrid("328-331", 1, "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-331"), 0, "Cut to " & GetImpString(LenRet))
                    Else
                        Call FillSummaryGrid("328-331", 1, "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-331"), 0, "Cut to " & Round(MetLenRet, 0) & " mm")
                    End If
                    varDoorData.varBtmRetainer(0).PN = "328-331"
                    varDoorData.varBtmRetainer(0).RetLen(0) = LenRet
                Case Is <= 313

                        Call FillSummaryGrid("328-332", LenRet, "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-332"), LenRet, "Cut to " & GetImpString(LenRet))

             Case "T300", "T300 Multi-Rib"

                    Call FillSummaryGrid("328-224", LenRet, "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-224"), LenRet, "Cut to " & GetImpString(LenRet))

                    Call FillSummaryGrid("328-224", MetLenRet, "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-224"), LenRet, "Cut to " & Round(MetLenRet, 0) & " mm")

                varDoorData.varBtmRetainer(0).PN = "328-217"
                varDoorData.varBtmRetainer(0).RetLen(0) = LenRet
        End Select
        W = (LenSeal * lsngSealWgt) + W

        If MeasU = 1 Then
            Call FillSummaryGrid(lstrSealPN, LenSeal, "Sections", GetSubCategory(lstrSealPN), LenSeal, "Cut to " & GetImpString(LenSeal))
        Else
            Call FillSummaryGrid(lstrSealPN, MetLenSeal, "Sections", GetSubCategory(lstrSealPN), LenSeal, "Cut to " & Round(MetLenSeal, 0) & " mm")
        End If

        varDoorData.varBtmRetainer(1).RetLen(0) = LenSeal

                lsngTempW = DrWidth - 0.25

                Case Is <= 107.75

'                    varDoorData.varBtmRetainer(0).RetLen(0) = LenRet
                Case Is <= 119.75

                    If MeasU = 1 Then
                        Call FillSummaryGrid("328-317", 1, "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-317"), 0, "Cut to " & GetImpString(LenRet))

'                    varDoorData.varBtmRetainer(0).RetLen(0) = LenRet

                        "Please contact product manufacturer.", vbExclamation + vbApplicationModal + vbOKOnly, "Bottom Retainer"
                    Exit Function
                End Select
                
              varDoorData.varBtmRetainer(0).RetLen(0) = LenRet
 
                
            Case "T165" 'add retainers from db, of appr. length and use the same seals as for T175.
                lsngTempW = DrWidth - 0.25

                Case Is <= 95.75

                    Else

                Case Is <= 191.75
                    W = LenRet * 4.176 / 191.75
                    If MeasU = 1 Then
                        Call FillSummaryGrid("328-330", 1, "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-330"), 0, "Cut to " & GetImpString(LenRet))
                    Else
                        Call FillSummaryGrid("328-330", 1, "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-330"), 0, "Cut to " & Round(MetLenRet, 0) & " mm")
                    End If
                    varDoorData.varBtmRetainer(0).PN = "328-330"
                    varDoorData.varBtmRetainer(0).RetLen(0) = LenRet
                Case Is <= 215.75

                    Else

                    Else

                    End If

                    Exit Function
                End Select

             Case "T300", "T300 Multi-Rib"

                Else

                End If
