                varDoorData.varBtmRetainer(0).PN = "328-224"
                varDoorData.varBtmRetainer(0).RetLen(0) = LenRet

            
            
            Case "T200", "T200 Multi-Rib", "ADV200", "T200A", "T200A Multi-Rib", "ADV200 Multi-Rib"
                'comes in 24'6" roll
                If MeasU = 1 Then
                    Call FillSummaryGrid("328-217", LenRet, "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-217"), LenRet, "Cut to " & GetImpString(LenRet))
                Else
                    Call FillSummaryGrid("328-217", MetLenRet, "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-217"), LenRet, "Cut to " & Round(MetLenRet, 0) & " mm")
                End If
                W = LenRet * (9.114 / 294)
                varDoorData.varBtmRetainer(0).PN = "328-217"
                varDoorData.varBtmRetainer(0).RetLen(0) = LenRet
        End Select
    Case 4
        'Galv. MRSB
        If ProdType = "Rolltite" Then
            QTY(0) = RoundUp(Round((DrWidth - 1.5) / 120, 3))
'            If DrWidth <= 120 Then
'                QTY(0) = 1
'            ElseIf DrWidth > 120 And DrWidth < 360 Then
'                QTY(0) = 2
'            Else
'                QTY(0) = 3
'            End If
        
'            W = LenRet * 6.2 / 120
            MRSBWgt = MRSBWgt + (LenRet * 6.2 / 120) 'wgt of 421-0055 goes to extra wgt on bottom.
            
            If QTY(0) = 1 Then
                PLen(0, 0) = LenRet
                PLen(1, 0) = 0
            Else
                PLen(0, 0) = 120
                PLen(1, 0) = DrWidth - 1.5 - (120 * (QTY(0) - 1))
            End If
            
            If (PLen(0, 0) = 120 And PLen(1, 0) = 120) Or (PLen(0, 0) = 120 And PLen(1, 0) = 0) Then
                Call FillSummaryGrid("421-0055", QTY(0), "Sections", GetSubCategory("421-0055"), 0)
            Else
                If MeasU = 1 Then
                    If QTY(0) = 1 Then
                        Call FillSummaryGrid("421-0055", QTY(0), "Sections", GetSubCategory("421-0055"), 0, "Cut to " & GetImpString(PLen(0, 0)))
                    Else
                       Call FillSummaryGrid("421-0055", QTY(0), "Sections", GetSubCategory("421-0055"), 0, "Cut (1) to " & GetImpString(PLen(1, 0)))
                    End If
                Else
                    ClearConObj
                    If QTY(0) = 1 Then
                        aCon.Inch = PLen(0, 0)
                        aCon.mmcalc
                        PLen(0, 0) = aCon.mm
                        Call FillSummaryGrid("421-0055", QTY(0), "Sections", GetSubCategory("421-0055"), 0, "Cut to " & Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm")
                    Else
                        aCon.Inch = PLen(1, 0)
                        aCon.mmcalc
                        PLen(0, 0) = aCon.mm
                        Call FillSummaryGrid("421-0055", QTY(0), "Sections", GetSubCategory("421-0055"), 0, "Cut (1) to " & Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm")
                    End If
                End If
            End If
        Else
            ' inches left over from an even panel width
            i = DrWidth
            If ((i Mod 12) <= 2) Then
                i = i - (i Mod 12)
                i = i / 12
            Else
                i = (i / 12) + 1
            End If 'set i to be the whole feet value (find the next bar up)
            T = (i * 12) ' feet converted to inches (for 10' T = 120)
            
            If (i < 24) Then
                If ((i Mod 2) = 1) Then
                    i = i + 1
                End If
            End If
            If (i < 6) Then
                i = 6
            End If
            
            T = (i * 12)
            T2 = 0
            QTY(0) = 1
            
            Select Case i
                Case 6, 8, 10, 12
                    i = ((i / 2) * (-1)) + 7
                    PN(0) = "421-007" & Trim(CStr(i))
                    
                Case 16, 20, 24
                    T2 = (i / 2) * 12
                    i = ((i / 4) * (-1)) + 7
                    PN(0) = "421-007" & Trim(CStr(i))
                    QTY(0) = 2
                   
                Case 14
                    PN(0) = "421-0073"
                    PN(1) = "421-0074"
                    QTY(1) = 1
                    T2 = (6 * 12)
                Case 18
                    PN(0) = "421-0072"
                    PN(1) = "421-0073"
                    QTY(1) = 1
                    T2 = (8 * 12)
                Case 22
                    PN(0) = "421-0071"
                    PN(1) = "421-0072"
                    QTY(1) = 1
                    T2 = (10 * 12)
            End Select
            'at this point T = length of the bar(s combined) in inches
            'T2 is the length of the longer piece
            If DrWidth < T Then
                T = DrWidth - T2  'cut longer piece to T
            ElseIf DrWidth = T Then
                If T2 > 0 Then T = T2
            End If
            Dim TempWgt As Single
            Select Case PN(0)
                Case "421-0074"
                    If QTY(0) = 1 Then
                        TempWgt = ((3.67 / 72) * T)
                        P = 72
                    ElseIf QTY(0) = 2 Then
                        TempWgt = 3.67 + ((3.67 / 72) * T)
                        P = 72
                    End If
                    
                Case "421-0073"
                    If QTY(0) = 1 Then
                        TempWgt = ((4.89 / 96) * T)
                        P = 96
                    ElseIf QTY(0) = 2 Then
                        TempWgt = 4.89 + ((4.89 / 96) * T)
                        P = 96
                    End If
                    
                Case "421-0072"
                    If QTY(0) = 1 Then
                        TempWgt = ((5.11 / 120) * T)
                        P = 120
                    ElseIf QTY(0) = 2 Then
                        TempWgt = 5.11 + ((5.11 / 120) * T)
                        P = 120
                    End If
                    
                Case "421-0071"
                    If QTY(0) = 1 Then
                        TempWgt = ((6.33 / 144) * T)
                        P = 144
                    ElseIf QTY(0) = 2 Then
                        TempWgt = 6.33 + ((6.33 / 144) * T)
                        P = 144
                    End If
            End Select
            
            Select Case PN(1)
                Case "421-0074"
                    TempWgt = TempWgt + 3.67
                Case "421-0073"
                    TempWgt = TempWgt + 4.89
                Case "421-0072"
                    TempWgt = TempWgt + 5.11
                Case "421-0071"
                    TempWgt = TempWgt + 6.33
            End Select
            
            MRSBWgt = MRSBWgt + TempWgt
            
            PLen(0, 0) = T
            If QTY(1) > 0 Then PLen(1, 0) = T2
            
            If P = T Then  'if no cut needed.
                Call FillSummaryGrid(PN(0), QTY(0), "Sections", GetSubCategory(PN(0)), 0)
            Else
                Call ClearConObj
                If MeasU = 0 Then
                    aCon.Inch = T
                    aCon.mmcalc
                    T = Round(aCon.mm, 0)
                    M = T & "mm"
                Else
                    M = GetImpString(T)
                End If
                
                Call FillSummaryGrid(PN(0), QTY(0), "Sections", GetSubCategory(PN(0)), 0, "Cut (1) to " & M)
                
            End If
            If QTY(1) > 0 Then Call FillSummaryGrid(PN(1), QTY(1), "Sections", GetSubCategory(PN(1)), PLen(1, 0), "Cut to " & GetImpString(DrWidth + 6))
            
        End If
        
    Case 5 'channel cap
        If DrType = "T150" Or DrType = "T150 Multi-Rib" Then
            PN(0) = "432-0015"   'comes in 10' roll
            MRSBWgt = MRSBWgt + (DrWidth - 1.5) * (8.33 / 120)
        
        ElseIf DrType = "T175" Or DrType = "T175A" Or DrType = "T175 Multi-Rib" Then
            PN(0) = "432-0014"
            MRSBWgt = MRSBWgt + (DrWidth - 1.5) * (8.85 / 120)
        
        ElseIf DrType = "T300" Or DrType = "T300 Multi-Rib" Then
            PN(0) = "432-0028"
            MRSBWgt = MRSBWgt + (DrWidth - 1.5) * (11.46 / 120)
        End If
        
'        If DrWidth - 1.5 <= 120 Then
'            QTY(0) = 1
'        ElseIf DrWidth - 1.5 > 120 And DrWidth - 1.5 < 360 Then
'            QTY(0) = 2
'        Else
'            QTY(0) = 3
'        End If

        QTY(0) = RoundUp((DrWidth - 1.5) / 120)
        
        If QTY(0) = 1 Then
            PLen(0, 0) = DrWidth - 1.5
            PLen(1, 0) = 0
        Else
            PLen(0, 0) = 120
            PLen(1, 0) = DrWidth - 1.5 - 120
        End If
            
        If (PLen(0, 0) = 120 And PLen(1, 0) = 120) Or (PLen(0, 0) = 120 And PLen(1, 0) = 0) Then
            Call FillSummaryGrid(PN(0), QTY(0), "Sections", GetSubCategory(PN(0)), 0)
        Else
            If MeasU = 1 Then
                If QTY(0) = 1 Then
                    Call FillSummaryGrid(PN(0), QTY(0), "Sections", GetSubCategory(PN(0)), 0, "Cut to " & GetImpString(PLen(0, 0)))
                Else
                   Call FillSummaryGrid(PN(0), QTY(0), "Sections", GetSubCategory(PN(0)), 0, "Cut (1) to " & GetImpString(PLen(1, 0)))
                End If
            Else
                ClearConObj
                If QTY(0) = 1 Then
                    aCon.Inch = PLen(0, 0)
                    aCon.mmcalc
                    PLen(0, 0) = aCon.mm
                    Call FillSummaryGrid(PN(0), QTY(0), "Sections", GetSubCategory(PN(0)), 0, "Cut to " & Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm")
                Else
                    aCon.Inch = PLen(1, 0)
                    aCon.mmcalc
                    PLen(0, 0) = aCon.mm
                    Call FillSummaryGrid(PN(0), QTY(0), "Sections", GetSubCategory(PN(0)), 0, "Cut (1) to " & Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm")
                End If
            End If
        End If
    
    Case 6 'pass door option A
        If DrWidth <= 122 Then
            If MeasU = 1 Then
                Call FillSummaryGrid("328-653", 1, "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-653"), 0, "Cut to " & GetImpString(DrWidth - 2))
            Else
                ClearConObj
                aCon.Inch = DrWidth - 2
                aCon.mmcalc
                Call FillSummaryGrid("328-653", 1, "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-653"), 0, "Cut to " & Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm")
            End If
            varDoorData.varBtmRetainer(0).PN = lstrSealPN
            varDoorData.varBtmRetainer(0).RetLen(0) = LenSeal
            W = (DrWidth - 2) * 5.14 / 122
        Else
            If MeasU = 1 Then
                Call FillSummaryGrid("328-654", DrWidth - 2, "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-654"), DrWidth - 2, "Cut to " & GetImpString(DrWidth - 2))
            Else
                ClearConObj
                aCon.Inch = DrWidth - 2
                aCon.mmcalc
                Call FillSummaryGrid("328-654", aCon.mm, "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-654"), DrWidth - 2, "Cut to " & Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm")
            End If
            varDoorData.varBtmRetainer(0).PN = "328-654"
            varDoorData.varBtmRetainer(0).RetLen(0) = DrWidth - 2
            varDoorData.varBtmRetainer(1).PN = lstrSealPN
            varDoorData.varBtmRetainer(1).RetLen(0) = LenSeal
            W = (DrWidth - 2) * (12.5 / 288)
        End If
        
        If MeasU = 1 Then
            Call FillSummaryGrid(lstrSealPN, LenSeal, "Sections", GetSubCategory(lstrSealPN), LenSeal, "Cut to " & GetImpString(LenSeal))
        Else
            Call FillSummaryGrid(lstrSealPN, MetLenSeal, "Sections", GetSubCategory(lstrSealPN), LenSeal, "Cut to " & Round(MetLenSeal, 0) & " mm")
        End If
        
        W = W + (LenSeal * lsngSealWgt)
        
    Case 7  'Passdoor option B
        
'        If DrWidth > 122 Then
'            QTY(0) = 2
'            W = 4.86 + ((DrWidth - 2) * 4.86 / 122)
'        Else
'            QTY(0) = 1
'            W = (DrWidth - 2) * 4.86 / 122
'        End If
        QTY(0) = RoundUp((DrWidth - 2) / 122)
        W = 4.86 * QTY(0)
        
        If MeasU = 1 Then
            If QTY(0) = 1 Then
                Call FillSummaryGrid("328-648", QTY(0), "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-648"), 0, "Cut to " & GetImpString(DrWidth - 2))
            Else
                Call FillSummaryGrid("328-648", QTY(0), "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-648"), 0, "Cut (1) to " & GetImpString((DrWidth - 2) - 122))
            End If
        Else
            If QTY(0) = 1 Then
                ClearConObj
                aCon.Inch = DrWidth - 2
                aCon.mmcalc
                Call FillSummaryGrid("328-648", QTY(0), "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-648"), 0, "Cut to " & Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm")
            Else
                ClearConObj
                aCon.Inch = (DrWidth - 2) - 122
                aCon.mmcalc
                Call FillSummaryGrid("328-648", QTY(0), "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-648"), 0, "Cut (1) to " & Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm")
            End If
        End If
        
        If MeasU = 1 Then
            Call FillSummaryGrid(lstrSealPN, LenSeal, "Sections", GetSubCategory(lstrSealPN), LenSeal, "Cut to " & GetImpString(LenSeal))
        Else
            Call FillSummaryGrid(lstrSealPN, MetLenSeal, "Sections", GetSubCategory(lstrSealPN), LenSeal, "Cut to " & Round(MetLenSeal, 0) & " mm")
        End If
        varDoorData.varBtmRetainer(0).PN = lstrSealPN
        varDoorData.varBtmRetainer(0).RetLen(0) = LenSeal
        W = (LenSeal * lsngSealWgt) + W
    
    Case 8 'Passdoor option C. No replacement parts available yet for T45
        
        If MeasU = 1 Then
            Call FillSummaryGrid("328-218", LenRet, "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-218"), LenRet, "Cut to " & GetImpString(LenRet))
        Else
            Call FillSummaryGrid("328-218", MetLenRet, "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-218"), LenRet, "Cut to " & MetLenRet & " mm")
        End If
        varDoorData.varBtmRetainer(0).PN = "328-218"
        varDoorData.varBtmRetainer(0).RetLen(0) = LenRet
        If MeasU = 1 Then
            Call FillSummaryGrid("432-0013", 2, "Sections", GetSubCategory("432-0013"), 0, "Cut (1) to " & GetImpString(DrWidth - 2) & " (1) to " & GetImpString(DrWidth - 6))
        Else
            Dim MPlen As Single
            aCon.Inch = DrWidth - 6
            aCon.mmcalc
            MPlen = aCon.mm
            aCon.Inch = DrWidth - 2
            aCon.mmcalc
            Call FillSummaryGrid("432-0013", 2, "Sections", GetSubCategory("432-0013"), 0, "Cut (1) to " & Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm" & " (1) to " & Round(MPlen, 0) & " mm")
        End If
        
        If MeasU = 1 Then
            Call FillSummaryGrid(lstrSealPN, LenSeal, "Sections", GetSubCategory(lstrSealPN), LenSeal, "Cut to " & GetImpString(LenSeal))
        Else
            Call FillSummaryGrid(lstrSealPN, MetLenSeal, "Sections", GetSubCategory(lstrSealPN), LenSeal, "Cut to " & Round(MetLenSeal, 0) & " mm")
        End If
        varDoorData.varBtmRetainer(1).PN = lstrSealPN
        varDoorData.varBtmRetainer(1).RetLen(0) = LenSeal
        W = (LenRet * (2.7 / 192)) + (LenSeal * lsngSealWgt)

''''''''''Change in .NET''''''''''''''''
'        MRSBWgt = MRSBWgt + ((DrWidth - 2) * (16.24 / 192)) + ((DrWidth - 6) * (16.24 / 192)) '432-0013 weight goes to extra wgt on bottom.
        MRSBWgt = MRSBWgt + ((DrWidth - 2) * (18.56 / 192)) + ((DrWidth - 6) * (18.56 / 192)) '432-0013 weight goes to extra wgt on bottom.

''''''''''END Change in .NET
        
    Case 9  ' channel w/aluminum retainer and seal
        If DrType = "T150" Or DrType = "T175" Or DrType = "T175-20" Or DrType = "T175A" Or _
            DrType = "T150 Multi-Rib" Or DrType = "T175 Multi-Rib" Then
            If DrType = "T150" Or DrType = "T150 Multi-Rib" Then
                PN(0) = "432-0015"
                TempWgt = (DrWidth - 1.5) * (8.33 / 120)
            Else
                PN(0) = "432-0014"
                TempWgt = (DrWidth - 1.5) * (8.85 / 120)
            End If
             
            QTY(0) = RoundUp((DrWidth - 1.5) / 120)
            
            If QTY(0) = 1 Then
                PLen(0, 0) = DrWidth - 1.5
                PLen(1, 0) = 0
            Else
                PLen(0, 0) = 120
                PLen(1, 0) = DrWidth - 1.5 - 120
            End If
                
            If (PLen(0, 0) = 120 And PLen(1, 0) = 120) Or (PLen(0, 0) = 120 And PLen(1, 0) = 0) Then
                Call FillSummaryGrid(PN(0), QTY(0), "Sections", GetSubCategory(PN(0)), 0)
            Else
                If MeasU = 1 Then
                    If QTY(0) = 1 Then
                        Call FillSummaryGrid(PN(0), QTY(0), "Sections", GetSubCategory(PN(0)), 0, "Cut to " & GetImpString(PLen(0, 0)))
                    Else
                       Call FillSummaryGrid(PN(0), QTY(0), "Sections", GetSubCategory(PN(0)), 0, "Cut (1) to " & GetImpString(PLen(1, 0)))
                    End If
                Else
                    ClearConObj
                    If QTY(0) = 1 Then
                        aCon.Inch = PLen(0, 0)
                        aCon.mmcalc
                        PLen(0, 0) = aCon.mm
                        Call FillSummaryGrid(PN(0), QTY(0), "Sections", GetSubCategory(PN(0)), 0, "Cut to " & Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm")
                    Else
                        aCon.Inch = PLen(1, 0)
                        aCon.mmcalc
                        PLen(0, 0) = aCon.mm
                        Call FillSummaryGrid(PN(0), QTY(0), "Sections", GetSubCategory(PN(0)), 0, "Cut (1) to " & Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm")
                    End If
                End If
            End If
                      
            If MeasU = 1 Then
                Call FillSummaryGrid("328-218", LenRet, "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-218"), LenRet, "Cut to " & GetImpString(LenRet))
            Else
                Call FillSummaryGrid("328-218", MetLenRet, "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-218"), LenRet, "Cut to " & Round(MetLenRet, 0) & " mm")
            End If
            varDoorData.varBtmRetainer(0).PN = "328-218"
            varDoorData.varBtmRetainer(0).RetLen(0) = LenRet
            varDoorData.varBtmRetainer(1).PN = lstrSealPN
            varDoorData.varBtmRetainer(1).RetLen(0) = LenSeal
            If MeasU = 1 Then
                Call FillSummaryGrid(lstrSealPN, LenSeal, "Sections", GetSubCategory(lstrSealPN), LenSeal, "Cut to " & GetImpString(LenSeal))
            Else
                Call FillSummaryGrid(lstrSealPN, MetLenSeal, "Sections", GetSubCategory(lstrSealPN), LenSeal, "Cut to " & Round(MetLenSeal, 0) & " mm")
            End If
            
            MRSBWgt = MRSBWgt + TempWgt
            W = (LenSeal * lsngSealWgt) + (LenRet * 2.7 / 192)
        End If
    Case 11          'T200/ADV200/T200A/T200A MUlti-Rib/ADV200 Multi-Rib/T200 Multi-Rib  -   'Galv. MRSB & channel only
        
        Dim LenExt As Double
        Dim LenInt As Double
        Dim PNAngle(3) As String
        Dim QtyAngle(3) As Integer
        Dim ldblCutExt As Double, ldblCutInt As Double 'cut to values
        Dim W71 As Double, W72 As Double, W73 As Double, W74 As Double
        Dim lstrCutInstr As String
        LenExt = DrWidth - 1.5 'length of the exterior piece
        LenInt = DrWidth - IIf(varDoorData.DblEndCaps = True, 12, 6) 'length of the interior piece
        
        W71 = 6.33
        W72 = 5.11
        W73 = 4.89
        W74 = 3.67
        
        Select Case LenExt
        Case Is <= 72
            'lenint cannot be equal to 72 being shorter than lenext
            'pick (2) "421-0074", one for exterior, one for interior
            If MeasU = 1 Then
                Call FillSummaryGrid("421-0074", 2, "Sections", GetSubCategory("421-0074"), 0, IIf(LenExt = 72, "Cut (1) to " & GetImpString(LenInt), "Cut (1) to " & GetImpString(LenInt) & _
                    " (1) to " & GetImpString(LenExt)))
            Else
                ClearConObj
                aCon.Inch = LenExt
                aCon.mmcalc
                LenExt = aCon.mm
                aCon.Inch = LenInt
                aCon.mmcalc
                Call FillSummaryGrid("421-0074", 2, "Sections", GetSubCategory("421-0074"), 0, IIf(LenExt = 72, ("Cut (1) to " & Round(aCon.mm, 0) & "mm"), ("Cut (1) to " & Round(aCon.mm, 0) & "mm" & _
                    " (1) to " & Round(LenExt, 0) & "mm")))
            End If
            MRSBWgt = MRSBWgt + ((W74 / 72) * LenExt) + ((W74 / 72) * LenInt)
'            W = ((W74 / 72) * LenExt) + ((W74 / 72) * LenInt)
            
        Case Is <= 96
            'if interior is <=72, then it falls into the previous group.
            'pick (1) "421-0074" for interior and (1) "421-0073" for exterior
            'else pick (2) "421-0073".
            
            If LenInt <= 72 Then
                If MeasU = 1 Then
                    Call FillSummaryGrid("421-0074", 1, "Sections", GetSubCategory("421-0074"), 0, IIf(LenInt = 72, "", "Cut to " & GetImpString(LenInt)))
                    Call FillSummaryGrid("421-0073", 1, "Sections", GetSubCategory("421-0073"), 0, "Cut to " & GetImpString(LenExt))
                Else
                    ClearConObj
                    aCon.Inch = LenInt
                    aCon.mmcalc
                    Call FillSummaryGrid("421-0074", 1, "Sections", GetSubCategory("421-0074"), 0, IIf(LenInt = 72, "", "Cut to " & Round(aCon.mm, 0) & "mm"))
                    ClearConObj
                    aCon.Inch = LenExt
                    aCon.mmcalc
                    Call FillSummaryGrid("421-0073", 1, "Sections", GetSubCategory("421-0073"), 0, "Cut to " & Round(aCon.mm, 0) & "mm")
                    
                End If
                MRSBWgt = MRSBWgt + ((W74 / 72) * LenInt) + ((W73 / 96) * LenExt)
'                W = ((W74 / 72) * LenInt) + ((W73 / 96) * LenExt)
            Else
                If MeasU = 1 Then
                    Call FillSummaryGrid("421-0073", 2, "Sections", GetSubCategory("421-0073"), 0, IIf(LenExt = 96, "Cut (1) to " & GetImpString(LenInt), "Cut (1) to " & GetImpString(LenInt) & _
                        " (1) to " & GetImpString(LenExt)))
                Else
                    ClearConObj
                    aCon.Inch = LenExt
                    aCon.mmcalc
                    LenExt = aCon.mm
                    aCon.Inch = LenInt
                    aCon.mmcalc
                    Call FillSummaryGrid("421-0073", 2, "Sections", GetSubCategory("421-0073"), 0, IIf(LenExt = 96, ("Cut (1) to " & Round(aCon.mm, 0) & "mm"), ("Cut (1) to " & Round(aCon.mm, 0) & "mm" & _
                        " (1) to " & Round(LenExt, 0) & "mm")))
                End If
                MRSBWgt = MRSBWgt + ((W73 / 96) * LenInt) + ((W73 / 96) * LenExt)
'                W = ((W73 / 96) * LenInt) + ((W73 / 96) * LenExt)
            End If
        Case Is <= 120
            If LenInt <= 96 Then
                If MeasU = 1 Then
                    Call FillSummaryGrid("421-0073", 1, "Sections", GetSubCategory("421-0073"), 0, IIf(LenInt = 96, "", "Cut to " & GetImpString(LenInt)))
                    Call FillSummaryGrid("421-0072", 1, "Sections", GetSubCategory("421-0072"), 0, "Cut to " & GetImpString(LenExt))
                Else
                    ClearConObj
                    aCon.Inch = LenInt
                    aCon.mmcalc
                    Call FillSummaryGrid("421-0073", 1, "Sections", GetSubCategory("421-0073"), 0, IIf(LenInt = 96, "", "Cut to " & Round(aCon.mm, 0) & "mm"))
                    ClearConObj
                    aCon.Inch = LenExt
                    aCon.mmcalc
                    Call FillSummaryGrid("421-0072", 1, "Sections", GetSubCategory("421-0072"), 0, "Cut to " & Round(aCon.mm, 0) & "mm")
                End If
                MRSBWgt = MRSBWgt + ((W73 / 96) * LenInt) + ((W72 / 120) * LenExt)
'                W = ((W73 / 96) * LenInt) + ((W72 / 120) * LenExt)
            Else
                If MeasU = 1 Then
                    Call FillSummaryGrid("421-0072", 2, "Sections", GetSubCategory("421-0072"), 0, IIf(LenExt = 120, "Cut (1) to " & GetImpString(LenInt), "Cut (1) to " & GetImpString(LenInt) & _
                        " (1) to " & GetImpString(LenExt)))
                Else
                    ClearConObj
                    aCon.Inch = LenExt
                    aCon.mmcalc
                    LenExt = aCon.mm
                    aCon.Inch = LenInt
                    aCon.mmcalc
                    Call FillSummaryGrid("421-0072", 2, "Sections", GetSubCategory("421-0072"), 0, IIf(LenExt = 120, ("Cut (1) to " & Round(aCon.mm, 0) & "mm"), ("Cut (1) to " & Round(aCon.mm, 0) & "mm" & _
                        " (1) to " & Round(LenExt, 0) & "mm")))
                End If
                MRSBWgt = MRSBWgt + ((W72 / 120) * LenInt) + ((W72 / 120) * LenExt)
'                W = ((W72 / 120) * LenInt) + ((W72 / 120) * LenExt)
            End If
        Case Is <= 144
            If LenInt <= 120 Then
                If MeasU = 1 Then
                    Call FillSummaryGrid("421-0072", 1, "Sections", GetSubCategory("421-0072"), 0, IIf(LenInt = 120, "", "Cut to " & GetImpString(LenInt)))
                    Call FillSummaryGrid("421-0071", 1, "Sections", GetSubCategory("421-0071"), 0, "Cut to " & GetImpString(LenExt))
                Else
                    ClearConObj
                    aCon.Inch = LenInt
                    aCon.mmcalc
                    Call FillSummaryGrid("421-0072", 1, "Sections", GetSubCategory("421-0072"), 0, IIf(LenInt = 120, "", "Cut to " & Round(aCon.mm, 0) & "mm"))
                    ClearConObj
                    aCon.Inch = LenExt
                    aCon.mmcalc
                    Call FillSummaryGrid("421-0071", 1, "Sections", GetSubCategory("421-0071"), 0, "Cut to " & Round(aCon.mm, 0) & "mm")
                End If
                MRSBWgt = MRSBWgt + ((W72 / 120) * LenInt) + ((W71 / 144) * LenExt)
'                W = ((W72 / 120) * LenInt) + ((W71 / 144) * LenExt)
            Else
                If MeasU = 1 Then
                    Call FillSummaryGrid("421-0071", 2, "Sections", GetSubCategory("421-0071"), 0, IIf(LenExt = 144, "Cut (1) to " & GetImpString(LenInt), "Cut (1) to " & GetImpString(LenInt) & _
                        " (1) to " & GetImpString(LenExt)))
                Else
                    ClearConObj
                    aCon.Inch = LenExt
                    aCon.mmcalc
                    LenExt = aCon.mm
                    aCon.Inch = LenInt
                    aCon.mmcalc
                    Call FillSummaryGrid("421-0071", 2, "Sections", GetSubCategory("421-0071"), 0, IIf(LenExt = 144, ("Cut (1) to " & Round(aCon.mm, 0) & "mm"), ("Cut (1) to " & Round(aCon.mm, 0) & "mm" & _
                        " (1) to " & Round(LenExt, 0) & "mm")))
                End If
                MRSBWgt = MRSBWgt + ((W71 / 144) * LenInt) + ((W71 / 144) * LenExt)
'                W = ((W71 / 144) * LenInt) + ((W71 / 144) * LenExt)
            End If
        Case Is <= 168
            If LenInt <= 144 Then
                If MeasU = 1 Then
                    Call FillSummaryGrid("421-0071", 1, "Sections", GetSubCategory("421-0071"), 0, IIf(LenInt = 144, "", "Cut to " & GetImpString(LenInt)))
                    Call FillSummaryGrid("421-0073", 1, "Sections", GetSubCategory("421-0073"), 0, "Cut to " & GetImpString(96 - (168 - LenExt)))
                Else
                    ClearConObj
                    aCon.Inch = LenInt
                    aCon.mmcalc
                    Call FillSummaryGrid("421-0071", 1, "Sections", GetSubCategory("421-0071"), 0, IIf(LenInt = 144, "", "Cut to " & Round(aCon.mm, 0) & "mm"))
                    ClearConObj
                    aCon.Inch = 96 - (168 - LenExt)
                    aCon.mmcalc
                    Call FillSummaryGrid("421-0073", 1, "Sections", GetSubCategory("421-0073"), 0, "Cut to " & Round(aCon.mm, 0) & "mm")
                End If
                Call FillSummaryGrid("421-0074", 1, "Sections", GetSubCategory("421-0074"), 0)
                MRSBWgt = MRSBWgt + ((W71 / 144) * LenInt) + ((W73 / 96) * 96 - (168 - LenExt)) + (W74)
'                W = ((W71 / 144) * LenInt) + ((W73 / 96) * 96 - (168 - LenExt)) + (W74)
            Else
                If MeasU = 1 Then
                    Call FillSummaryGrid("421-0073", 2, "Sections", GetSubCategory("421-0073"), 0, IIf(LenExt = 168, "Cut (1) to " & GetImpString(73 - (168 - LenInt)), "Cut (1) to " & GetImpString(73 - (168 - LenInt)) & _
                        " (1) to " & GetImpString(73 - (168 - LenExt))))
                Else
                    ClearConObj
                    aCon.Inch = 73 - (168 - LenExt)
                    aCon.mmcalc
                    LenExt = aCon.mm
                    aCon.Inch = 73 - (168 - LenInt)
                    aCon.mmcalc
                    Call FillSummaryGrid("421-0073", 2, "Sections", GetSubCategory("421-0073"), 0, IIf(LenExt = 168, ("Cut (1) to " & Round(aCon.mm, 0) & "mm"), ("Cut (1) to " & Round(aCon.mm, 0) & "mm" & _
                        " (1) to " & Round(LenExt, 0) & "mm")))
                End If
                Call FillSummaryGrid("421-0074", 2, "Sections", GetSubCategory("421-0074"), 0)
                MRSBWgt = MRSBWgt + ((W73 / 96) * 96 - (168 - LenInt)) + ((W73 / 96) * 96 - (168 - LenExt)) + (W74 * 2)
'                W = ((W73 / 96) * 96 - (168 - LenInt)) + ((W73 / 96) * 96 - (168 - LenExt)) + (W74 * 2)
            End If
        Case Is <= 192
            If LenInt <= 168 Then
                If MeasU = 1 Then
                    Call FillSummaryGrid("421-0073", 3, "Sections", GetSubCategory("421-0073"), 0, IIf(LenInt = 168, "Cut (1) to " & GetImpString(96 - (192 - LenInt)), "Cut (1) to " & GetImpString(96 - (192 - LenInt)) & _
                        " (1) to " & GetImpString(96 - (192 - LenExt))))
                Else
                    ClearConObj
                    aCon.Inch = 96 - (192 - LenInt)
                    aCon.mmcalc
                    LenInt = aCon.mm
                    ClearConObj
                    aCon.Inch = 96 - (192 - LenExt)
                    aCon.mmcalc
                    Call FillSummaryGrid("421-0073", 3, "Sections", GetSubCategory("421-0073"), 0, IIf(LenInt = 168, "Cut (1) to " & Round(LenInt, 0) & "mm", "Cut (1) to " & Round(LenInt, 0) & "mm" & _
                        " (1) to " & Round(aCon.mm, 0) & "mm"))
                End If
                Call FillSummaryGrid("421-0074", 1, "Sections", GetSubCategory("421-0074"), 0)
                MRSBWgt = MRSBWgt + ((W73 / 96) * 96 - (192 - LenInt)) + ((W73 / 96) * 96 - (192 - LenExt)) + W74 + W73
'                W = ((W73 / 96) * 96 - (192 - LenInt)) + ((W73 / 96) * 96 - (192 - LenExt)) + W74 + W73
            Else
                If MeasU = 1 Then
                    Call FillSummaryGrid("421-0073", 4, "Sections", GetSubCategory("421-0073"), 0, IIf(LenExt = 192, "Cut (1) to " & GetImpString(96 - (192 - LenInt)), "Cut (1) to " & GetImpString(96 - (192 - LenInt)) & _
                        " (1) to " & GetImpString(96 - (192 - LenExt))))
                Else
                    ClearConObj
                    aCon.Inch = 96 - (192 - LenExt)
                    aCon.mmcalc
                    LenExt = aCon.mm
                    aCon.Inch = 96 - (192 - LenInt)
                    aCon.mmcalc
                    Call FillSummaryGrid("421-0073", 4, "Sections", GetSubCategory("421-0073"), 0, IIf(LenExt = 192, ("Cut (1) to " & Round(aCon.mm, 0) & "mm"), ("Cut (1) to " & Round(aCon.mm, 0) & "mm" & _
                        " (1) to " & Round(LenExt, 0) & "mm")))
                End If
                MRSBWgt = MRSBWgt + ((W73 / 96) * 96 - (192 - LenInt)) + ((W73 / 96) * 96 - (192 - LenExt)) + (W73 * 2)
'                W = ((W73 / 96) * 96 - (192 - LenInt)) + ((W73 / 96) * 96 - (192 - LenExt)) + (W73 * 2)
            End If
        Case Is <= 216
            If LenInt <= 192 Then
                If MeasU = 1 Then
                    Call FillSummaryGrid("421-0073", 3, "Sections", GetSubCategory("421-0073"), 0, IIf(LenInt = 192, "", "Cut (1) to " & GetImpString(96 - (216 - LenInt))))
                    Call FillSummaryGrid("421-0072", 1, "Sections", GetSubCategory("421-0072"), 0, "Cut to " & GetImpString(120 - (216 - LenExt)))
                Else
                    ClearConObj
                    aCon.Inch = 96 - (216 - LenInt)
                    aCon.mmcalc
                    LenInt = aCon.mm
                    ClearConObj
                    aCon.Inch = 120 - (216 - LenExt)
                    aCon.mmcalc
                    Call FillSummaryGrid("421-0073", 3, "Sections", GetSubCategory("421-0073"), 0, IIf(LenInt = 192, "", "Cut (1) to " & Round(LenInt, 0) & "mm"))
                    Call FillSummaryGrid("421-0072", 1, "Sections", GetSubCategory("421-0072"), 0, "Cut to " & Round(aCon.mm, 0))
                End If
                MRSBWgt = MRSBWgt + ((W73 / 96) * 96 - (216 - LenInt)) + ((W72 / 120) * 120 - (216 - LenExt)) + (W73 * 2)
'                W = ((W73 / 96) * 96 - (216 - LenInt)) + ((W72 / 120) * 120 - (216 - LenExt)) + (W73 * 2)
            Else
                If MeasU = 1 Then
                    Call FillSummaryGrid("421-0072", 2, "Sections", GetSubCategory("421-0072"), 0, IIf(LenExt = 216, "Cut (1) to " & GetImpString(120 - (216 - LenInt)), "Cut (1) to " & GetImpString(120 - (216 - LenInt)) & _
                        " (1) to " & GetImpString(120 - (216 - LenExt))))
                Else
                    ClearConObj
                    aCon.Inch = 120 - (216 - LenExt)
                    aCon.mmcalc
                    LenExt = aCon.mm
                    aCon.Inch = 120 - (216 - LenInt)
                    aCon.mmcalc
                    Call FillSummaryGrid("421-0072", 2, "Sections", GetSubCategory("421-0072"), 0, IIf(LenExt = 216, ("Cut (1) to " & Round(aCon.mm, 0) & "mm"), ("Cut (1) to " & Round(aCon.mm, 0) & "mm" & _
                        " (1) to " & Round(LenExt, 0) & "mm")))

    Case 12 'T45: Aluminum retainer 26'-1"
            'T35: Btm Retainer (26'-1")

             W = (LenRet * 6 / 313) + (LenSeal * lsngSealWgt) 'calculate cut weight

    Case 13 'T45: Aluminum retainer cut length
            'T35 Alum. retainer cut length
  
        lsngTempW = DrWidth - 0.25

            Case Is <= 109.75

                End If
                
                varDoorData.varBtmRetainer(1).PN = lstrSealPN
                varDoorData.varBtmRetainer(1).RetLen(0) = LenSeal
                
            Case Is <= 121.75

                        Call FillSummaryGrid("328-713", 2, "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-713"), 0, IIf(LenExt = 337.75, "", "Cut to " & Round(aCon.mm, 0)))
                    End If

                End If

                Else

                End If

                varDoorData.varBtmRetainer(1).RetLen(0) = LenSeal

                Else

                End If

                varDoorData.varBtmRetainer(1).RetLen(0) = LenSeal
                
            Case Is > 433.75
                If DrType = "T35" Then
                    W = (LenRet * (6 / 313)) + (LenSeal * lsngSealWgt)
                    
                    'retainer: over 36'

                    Else

                    End If
                    varDoorData.varBtmRetainer(0).PN = "328-708"
                    varDoorData.varBtmRetainer(0).RetLen(0) = LenRet
                Else
                    W = (LenRet * (10.926 / 313)) + (LenSeal * lsngSealWgt)
                    
                    'retainer: over 36'
                    If MeasU = 1 Then
                        Call FillSummaryGrid("328-716", 1, "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-716"), LenRet, "Cut to " & GetImpString(LenRet))
                    Else
                        Call FillSummaryGrid("328-716", 1, "Sections", GetSubCategory("328-716"), LenRet, "Cut to " & Round(MetLenRet, 0) & " mm")
                    End If
                    varDoorData.varBtmRetainer(0).PN = "328-716"
                    varDoorData.varBtmRetainer(0).RetLen(0) = LenRet
                End If
                
                'seal: comes in 100' roll for now
                If MeasU = 1 Then
                    Call FillSummaryGrid(lstrSealPN, LenSeal, "Sections", GetSubCategory(lstrSealPN), LenSeal, "Cut to " & GetImpString(LenSeal))
                Else
                    Call FillSummaryGrid(lstrSealPN, MetLenSeal, "Sections", GetSubCategory(lstrSealPN), LenSeal, "Cut to " & Round(MetLenSeal, 0) & " mm")
                End If
                
                varDoorData.varBtmRetainer(1).PN = lstrSealPN
                varDoorData.varBtmRetainer(1).RetLen(0) = LenSeal
            Case Else
                MsgBox "Unable to find bottom retainer for that width." & vbCrLf & _
                    "Please contact product manufacturer.", vbExclamation + vbApplicationModal + vbOKOnly, "Bottom Retainer"
                Exit Function
            End Select
        
        Case 14 'pass door option A: T45
'            If DrWidth <= 122 Then
'                If MeasU = 1 Then
'                    Call FillSummaryGrid("FP0-0089", 1, "Sections", GetSubCategory("FP0-0089"), 0, "Cut to " & GetImpString(DrWidth - 2))

'                    aCon.Inch = DrWidth - 2
'                    aCon.mmcalc
'                    Call FillSummaryGrid("FP0-0089", 1, "Sections", GetSubCategory("FP0-0089"), 0, "Cut to " & Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm")
'                End If
'                varDoorData.varBtmRetainer(0).PN = lstrSealPN

'                W = (DrWidth - 2) * 5.14 / 122

'                If MeasU = 1 Then
'                    Call FillSummaryGrid("FP0-0090", DrWidth - 2, "Sections", GetSubCategory("FP0-0090"), DrWidth - 2, "Cut to " & GetImpString(DrWidth - 2))

'            Else

'            End If

    End Select
    
    If lbncheck = True Then 'cut and cap Galv angle along w/aluminum retainer and seal
                            'additional parts for option 10 (will run through case 11 plus add these parts)

Public Function GetLockWgt(ByVal LType As Integer, ByVal LQty As Integer) As Single
    
    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".GetLockWgt()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""

    'passed: Lock Type
    Dim PN(2) As String, W As Single 'Part Number array and weight of the lock itself
    Dim Q(2) As Integer 'qty array
    Dim SBar As Single  'length of Short Bar
    Dim LBar As Single  'length of Long Bar
    Dim SPN As String   'Part Number of Short Bar
    Dim LPN As String   'Part Number of Long Bar
    Dim SW As Single    'Weight of Short Bar
    Dim LW As Single    'Weight of Long Bar
    Dim NPanels As Integer
    
    NPanels = GetMaxLitesPerSect
    
    If ProdType = "Rolltite" Then
        SBar = ((NPanels \ 2) * (DrWidth / NPanels)) - 6
        LBar = ((NPanels - (NPanels \ 2)) * (DrWidth / NPanels)) - 6
    Else
        If DrWidth > 194 Then
            SBar = (DrWidth - 12) / 4
        Else
            SBar = (DrWidth - 12) / 2
        End If
        LBar = SBar
    End If
    
    SPN = BarLatchPN(SBar)
    If SBar <> LBar Then
        LPN = BarLatchPN(LBar)
    End If
    
    SW = BarLatchWgt(SPN)
    If LPN <> "" Then
        LW = BarLatchWgt(LPN)
    End If
    Q(0) = 1    'qty of the lock itselt will be one
    
    Select Case LType
    Case 0 'one point latch
        PN(0) = "114-0028"
        'PN(1) = "324-335"
        'Q(1) = LQty 'qty of the latch keeper is one per lock (one point latch)
        W = LQty * (0.79)
        Call FillSummaryGrid("324-335", LQty, "Sections", GetSubCategory("324-335"), 0)
    Case 1 'barlatch inside only
        PN(0) = "4-0062"
        PN(1) = SPN
        Q(1) = 2
        If LPN <> "" Then
            PN(2) = LPN
            Q(1) = 1
            Q(2) = 1
        End If
        W = 4.36 + LW + SW  'weight of the lock + bars
    Case 2 'complete package
'        If LiftType <> "Vertical Lift" Or LiftType <> "High Lift" Or LiftType <> "Low Headroom Vertical Lift" Then
            PN(0) = "4-0060"
            W = 4.14 + LW + SW  'weight of the lock + bars
'            Else 'discontinued
'                PN(0) = "4-0061"
'                W = 3.92 + LW + SW  'weight of the lock + bars
'            End If
        PN(1) = SPN
        Q(1) = 1
        If LPN <> "" Then
            PN(2) = LPN
            Q(2) = 1
        End If
    End Select
    
    If LType > 0 Then ' bars do not go with the one point latch.

        If Q(2) = 0 Then    'if long bar is 0 then use two short bars.
''''''''''Change in .NET''''''''''''''''
'            Q(1) = Q(1) + 1 'used to increment to 3 bars.
            Q(1) = 2
''''''''''END Change in .NET
        
        End If
    End If
    
    Dim i As Integer
    For i = 0 To 1
        If Q(i) > 0 Then
            Call FillSummaryGrid(PN(i), Q(i), "Sections", GetSubCategory(PN(i)), 0)
        End If
    Next
    
    GetLockWgt = W
    
    Exit Function

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Function

Public Function BarLatchPN(ByVal aLen As Single) As String
    
    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".BarLatchPN()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""

    Dim PN As String
    Dim i As Integer
    PN = ""
    i = CInt(aLen)
    If (i < aLen) Then
        i = i + 1
    End If
    If (i <= 43) Then
        PN = "425-9018"
    ElseIf (i <= 54) Then
        PN = "425-9017"
    ElseIf (i <= 55) Then
        PN = "425-9016"
    ElseIf (i <= 66) Then
        PN = "425-9015"
    ElseIf (i <= 67) Then
        PN = "425-9014"
    ElseIf (i <= 78) Then
        PN = "425-9013"
    ElseIf (i <= 79) Then
        PN = "425-9012"
    ElseIf (i <= 90) Then
        PN = "425-9011"
    ElseIf (i <= 91) Then
        PN = "425-9010"
    Else
        PN = "425-9010"
        i = 91
    End If
    BarLatchPN = PN

    Exit Function

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Function
Public Function BarLatchWgt(ByVal PN As String) As Single

    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".BarLatchWgt()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""

    Dim W As Single
    Select Case PN
    Case "425-9018"
        W = 2.26
    Case "425-9017"
        W = 2.85
    Case "425-9016"
        W = 2.88
    Case "425-9015"
        W = 3.35
    Case "425-9014"
        W = 3.53
    Case "425-9013"
        W = 3.96
    Case "425-9012"
        W = 3.99
    Case "425-9011"
        W = 4.6
    Case "425-9010"
        W = 4.64
    End Select
    
    BarLatchWgt = W

    Exit Function

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Function

Public Sub SelectRivets(pblnLock As Boolean, ByVal ECapType As Integer)
    
    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".SelectRivets()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""

    Dim SQty As Integer, RivQty As Integer
    
    RivQty = 0
    SQty = N21 + N24
    
    If pblnLock = True Then ' per each 426-0313/12
        RivQty = 16
    End If
    
    If varDoorData.OperatorType = 7 Then 'one trolley
        RivQty = RivQty + 16

    ElseIf varDoorData.OperatorType = 8 Then ' two trolley plates
        RivQty = RivQty + 32

    End If

    If varDoorData.LiteType > 8 Then ' full view
        If varDoorData.EndCapsOnFullView = False Then
            SQty = SQty - varDoorData.NLiteSec
        End If
    End If
    
    If ECapType > 0 Then
        If varDoorData.DblEndCaps = True Then  'double endcaps
            RivQty = RivQty + SQty * 8
        Else
            RivQty = RivQty + SQty * 4
        End If
    
    End If
    
    If varPassDoor.strPN <> "" Then
        Select Case varPassDoor.btBottom
            Case 1, 2: RivQty = RivQty + (((RoundUp(DrWidth / 12)) + 1) * 2)
            Case 3: RivQty = RivQty + (((RoundUp(DrWidth / 12)) + 1) * 4)
        End Select
        Select Case varPassDoor.btTop
            Case 1, 2: RivQty = RivQty + ((RoundUp(DrWidth / 12)) + 1)
            Case 3: RivQty = RivQty + (((RoundUp(DrWidth / 12)) + 1) * 4)
        End Select
    End If
    
    If RivQty > 0 Then
        aReg.OpenKey LocalMachine, "\Software\Richards-Wilcox Canada\BTMS", True 'opens or creates key
        Call FillSummaryGrid(IIf(aReg.ReadValue("Rivets") = "1", "233-064", "215-308"), RivQty, "Sections", GetSubCategory(IIf(aReg.ReadValue("Rivets") = "1", "233-064", "215-308")), 0)
        aReg.CloseKey
    End If
    Exit Sub

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Sub

Public Function Exhaust(ByVal IExhaust As Integer, ByVal QTY As Integer) As Single
    
    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".Exhaust()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""

    'send type of Exhaust and qty
    Dim PN As String
    Dim W As Single
    If IExhaust = 1 Then '3"
        If ProdType = "Rolltite" Or ProdType = "Alumatite" Then
            If Insulator > 0 Or (DrType <> "A175W" And DrType <> "A175W") Then
                PN = "324-990"  '3" rolltite & Alumatite bottom with thermatite section insulated
                W = 0.786
            Else
               PN = "324-991"   '3" alumatite (without thermatite bottom section) & Rolltite non-insulated
                W = 0.786
            End If
        Else
            PN = "324-990"      '3" all other door model insulated
            W = 0.966
        End If
    Else
        If ProdType = "Rolltite" Or ProdType = "Alumatite" Then
            If Insulator > 0 Or (DrType <> "A175A" And DrType <> "A175W") Then
                PN = "324-992"  '4" rolltite & Alumatite bottom with thermatite section insulated
                W = 1.434
            Else
                PN = "324-993"  '4" alumatite (without thermatite bottom section) & Rolltite non-insulated
                W = 1.434
            End If
        Else
            PN = "324-992"      '4" all other door model insulated
            W = 1.028
        End If
    End If
    Call FillSummaryGrid(PN, QTY, "Sections", GetSubCategory(PN), 0)
    Exhaust = W
    
    Exit Function

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Function

Public Function ToePlate(ByVal QTY As Integer) As Single
    
    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".ToePlate()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""

    Dim PN As String
    Dim W As Single
    Select Case ProdType
        Case "Rolltite"
            W = 0.43 + 0.17
            Call FillSummaryGrid("425-0222", QTY, "Sections", GetSubCategory("425-0222"), 0)
            Call FillSummaryGrid("324-202", QTY, "Sections", GetSubCategory("324-202"), 0)
        Case "Alumatite"
            W = 0.26
            Call FillSummaryGrid("111-1714", QTY, "Sections", GetSubCategory("111-1714"), 0)
        Case "Thermatite", "Thermalume", "Replacement"
            W = 0 'do not consider, weight = cut off section piece
            Call FillSummaryGrid("326-028", QTY, "Sections", GetSubCategory("326-028"), 0)
    End Select
    ToePlate = W * QTY

    Exit Function

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Function

Public Function GetTekScrewsLoose(pblnLock As Boolean) As Single
    
    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".GetTekScrewsLoose()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""
    
    Dim TS(2) As Integer
    Dim lintNofTrussSections As Integer
    Dim i As Integer
    
'    If pblnLock = True Then ' per each 426-0313/12
'        TS(0) = 16
'    End If
    
    If varDoorData.TrussSchedule > 0 Then
        If varDoorData.DblEndCaps = True Then
            TS(0) = TS(0) + GetTrussQtySelected * ((varDoorData.NPanels + 1) * 2) + 3 'for double endcaps and
        Else
            TS(0) = TS(0) + GetTrussQtySelected * ((varDoorData.NPanels + 1) * 2) + 1 'for single endcaps?
        End If
'        TS(0) = TS(0) + GetTrussQtySelected * ((varDoorData.NPanels + 1) * 2)
    End If
    
'    If varDoorData.OperatorType = 7 Then 'one trolley
'        TS(0) = TS(0) + 16
'    ElseIf varDoorData.OperatorType = 8 Then ' two trolley plates
'        TS(0) = TS(0) + 32
'    End If
    
    Select Case varDoorData.intBottomTreatment
        Case 1, 2, 3
'            TS(0) = TS(0) + (RoundUp(DrWidth / 12)) / 2 + 1
            TS(0) = TS(0) + (RoundUp(DrWidth / 12)) + 1
        Case 4
            If ProdType = "Rolltite" Then
                TS(1) = TS(1) + 4
            Else
'                TS(0) = TS(0) + (RoundUp(DrWidth / 12)) / 2 + 1
                TS(0) = TS(0) + (RoundUp(DrWidth / 12)) + 1
            End If
    End Select
    
    If varDoorData.intTopTreatment > 0 Then
'        TS(0) = TS(0) + (RoundUp(DrWidth / 12)) / 2 + 1
        TS(0) = TS(0) + (RoundUp(DrWidth / 12)) + 1
    End If
    
    If varPassDoor.strPN <> "" Then
        TS(0) = TS(0) + (RoundUp(DrWidth / 12) + 1)
        If varPassDoor.btTop > 0 Then
            TS(0) = TS(0) + (RoundUp(DrWidth / 12) + 1)
        End If
    End If
    
    If varDoorData.arrSections(0).intNTruss > 0 Then 'if truss on top section (ICB has to be used)
        TS(0) = TS(0) + (varDoorData.NPanels - 1) * 3 'pick 3 teks for each ICB
    End If
    
    For i = 1 To N21 + N24 - 1 'get number of truss sections omitting the top (need number of truss center hinges)
        If varDoorData.arrSections(i).intNTruss > 0 Then
            lintNofTrussSections = lintNofTrussSections + 1
        End If
    Next
    
    If lintNofTrussSections > 0 Then
        TS(0) = TS(0) + lintNofTrussSections * (varDoorData.NPanels - 1) '1 screw per each truss hinge (4 of the teks are picked up with the standard face hardware.  This is for the extra tek that attaches to the truss.)
    End If
    
    If TS(0) > 0 Then Call FillSummaryGrid("215-306", TS(0), "Sections", GetSubCategory("215-306"), 0)
    If TS(1) > 0 Then Call FillSummaryGrid("215-307", TS(1), "Sections", GetSubCategory("215-307"), 0)
    
    GetTekScrewsLoose = TS(0) * 0.012 + TS(1) * 0.012

    Exit Function

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Function

    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".BundleScrews()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String

'receive number of loose screws and bundle them in packages

    Dim i As Integer, PN(3) As String, Q(3) As Integer, Wgt(3) As Single, W As Single

'Alumatite face hardware packages = Thermatite.
    If (ProdType = "Rolltite") Then
        Exit Function
    End If
    PN(0) = "8-9028" '28
    Wgt(0) = 0.31
    PN(1) = "8-9034" '34

    PN(2) = "8-9040" '40

End Function

Public Sub FillSummaryGrid(ByVal PN As String, ByVal QTY As Single, ByVal Group As String, ByVal SubGroup As String, ByVal lLen As Single, Optional ByVal CutTo As String)
    
    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".FillSummaryGrid()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""
    'procedure will write the received parameters to the grid.
    Dim ID As Integer
    ID = CheckGroupID(PN)

    If varDoorData.Option = 1 And ID > 20 And Not (PN = "215-306") Then Exit Sub 'sections only
    If varDoorData.Option = 2 And ID > 25 Then Exit Sub 'retrofit
    If varDoorData.Option = 3 And ID < 20 Then Exit Sub 'HW only
    
    CRow = CRow + 1
    If CRow > 1 Then frmGrid.Grid1.Rows = CRow + 1

    frmGrid.Grid1.Row = CRow
    
    frmGrid.Grid1.Row = CRow
    frmGrid.Grid1.Col = 0
    frmGrid.Grid1.Text = PN
    frmGrid.Grid1.Col = 1
    frmGrid.Grid1.Text = QTY
    frmGrid.Grid1.Col = 2
    frmGrid.Grid1.Text = SubGroup
    frmGrid.Grid1.Col = 3
    frmGrid.Grid1.Text = Group
    If Not IsMissing(CutTo) Then
        frmGrid.Grid1.Col = 4
        frmGrid.Grid1.Text = CutTo
    End If
    frmGrid.Grid1.Col = 5
    frmGrid.Grid1.Text = lLen
    Exit Sub

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Sub

Public Function RollBack(ByVal Group As String, ByVal Total As Boolean, Optional ByVal Var As String = "") As Boolean
    
    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".RollBack()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""

    'passed: Group or Subgroup of the item. If Total is true the deletion is performed
    'based on the Group, otherwise, on the SubGroup. But if the optional Var is passed then
    'deletion is based on the specific part number and group and total are ignored.
    
    Dim T As String, i As Integer, X As Integer
    i = 0
    T = Group
    
    frmGrid.Grid1.Row = 1
    If Total = True Then
        frmGrid.Grid1.Col = 3
    Else
        frmGrid.Grid1.Col = 2
    End If
    
    If Var <> "" Then
        frmGrid.Grid1.Col = 0
        T = Var
    End If

''''''''''Change in .NET''''''''''''''''

    
'    Do While i = 0
'        If T = frmGrid.Grid1.Text Then
'            frmGrid.Grid1.RemoveItem (frmGrid.Grid1.Row)
'            CRow = CRow - 1
'            RollBack = True
'            If frmGrid.Grid1.Rows = 2 Then
'                For X = 0 To frmGrid.Grid1.Cols - 1
'                    frmGrid.Grid1.TextMatrix(1, X) = ""
'                Next X
'                CRow = CRow - 1
'                i = 1
'            End If
'        Else
'            If frmGrid.Grid1.Row < frmGrid.Grid1.Rows - 1 Then
'                frmGrid.Grid1.Row = frmGrid.Grid1.Row + 1
'            Else
'                i = 1
'            End If
'        End If
'    Loop

    Do While i = 0
        If T = frmGrid.Grid1.Text Then
            If frmGrid.Grid1.Rows = 2 Then
                For X = 0 To frmGrid.Grid1.Cols - 1
                    frmGrid.Grid1.TextMatrix(1, X) = ""
                Next X
                i = 1
            Else
                frmGrid.Grid1.RemoveItem (frmGrid.Grid1.Row)
            End If
            CRow = CRow - 1
            RollBack = True
        Else
            If frmGrid.Grid1.Row < frmGrid.Grid1.Rows - 1 Then
                frmGrid.Grid1.Row = frmGrid.Grid1.Row + 1
            Else
                i = 1
            End If
        End If
    Loop

''''''''''END Change in .NET

    Exit Function

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)
    
End Function

Public Function GetNumberOfSectionsPerHeight(ByVal pintH As Integer) As Integer 'not called
    
    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".GetNumberOfSectionsPerHeight()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""

    Dim i As Integer, lintCount As Integer
    For i = 0 To UBound(varDoorData.arrSections)
        If varDoorData.arrSections(i).intHeight = pintH Then
            lintCount = lintCount + 1
        End If
    Next i
    GetNumberOfSectionsPerHeight = lintCount
    Exit Function

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Function

Public Function GetNumberOfLiteSectionsPerHeight(ByVal pintH As Integer) As Integer
    
    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".GetNumberOfLiteSectionsPerHeight()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""

    Dim i As Integer, lintCount As Integer
    If Not isEmptySectionsArray Then
        For i = 0 To UBound(varDoorData.arrSections)
            If varDoorData.arrSections(i).intHeight = pintH Then
                If varDoorData.arrSections(i).intNLites > 0 Then
                    lintCount = lintCount + 1
                End If
            End If
        Next i
    End If
    GetNumberOfLiteSectionsPerHeight = lintCount
    Exit Function

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Function

Public Function isEmptySectionsArray() As Boolean

    On Local Error Resume Next
    
    Dim uLimit As Integer
    isEmptySectionsArray = False
    
    uLimit = UBound(varDoorData.arrSections)
    If Err.Number Then isEmptySectionsArray = True
    
    Err.Clear

End Function


Public Function GetOperatorType(ByVal OperatorType As Byte) As String

    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".GetOperatorType()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""

    Dim operatorDesc As String
    
    Select Case OperatorType
    Case 1
        operatorDesc = "Manual Operator"
    Case 2
        operatorDesc = "Chainhoist RW GHC"
    Case 3
        operatorDesc = "Chainhoist Model 2000"
    Case 4
        operatorDesc = "Chainhoist Model JR"
    Case 5
        operatorDesc = "Chainhoist RW direct drive"
    Case 6
        operatorDesc = "Dyna-Hoist Operator"
    Case 7
        operatorDesc = "Drawbar Operator - 1 trolley plate"
    Case 8

        operatorDesc = "Drawbar Operator - 2 trolley plates"
''''''''''Change in .NET''''''''''''''''
    Case 9
        operatorDesc = "Chainhoist 200D direct drive"
''''''''''END Change in .NET
    
    Case 10
        operatorDesc = "Jackshaft"
    Case 11
        operatorDesc = "Chainhoist Model JRG"
        
    End Select
        
    GetOperatorType = operatorDesc
    Exit Function

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Function

Public Function CalcTrussLen() As Single 'not called
    
    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".CalcTrussLen()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""
    
    Dim L As Single
    If DrType = "T175" Or DrType = "T150" Or DrType = "T175-20" Or DrType = "T140" Or DrType = "T165" Or DrType = "T175A" Or _
        DrType = "T150 Multi-Rib" Or DrType = "T175 Multi-Rib" Or DrType = "T138" Then
        L = DrWidth - 2   '3/8 of an inch is needed to put end caps
    ElseIf DrType = "T200" Or DrType = "T200 Multi-Rib" Or DrType = "ADV200" Or DrType = "T200A" Or DrType = "T200A Multi-Rib" Or DrType = "ADV200 Multi-Rib" Then
        L = DrWidth - 2   'only 1/8" for end caps
    Else
        L = DrWidth - 2          'no endcaps on other door types, subtract 2" only
    End If
    L = RoundFraction(L - Fix(L)) + Fix(L)
    CalcTrussLen = L
    Exit Function

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)
    
End Function

Public Function GetDrawBar(ByVal DBQty As Integer) As Single

    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".GetDrawBar()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""
