    If varDoorData.arrSections(0).intHeight = 21 Then
        PN = "426-0311"
        Wgt = 4.288
    Else
        PN = "426-0310"
        Wgt = 4.924
    End If
    
    GetDrawBar = DBQty * Wgt
        
    Call FillSummaryGrid(PN, DBQty, "Sections", GetSubCategory(PN), 0)

    Exit Function

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Function

Public Function GetOperator(ByVal pintType As Byte) As Single

    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".GetOperator()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""
    
    Dim PN As String
    Dim W As Single
    
    'Chain Hoist, Dyna-Hoist, Jackshaft operator comment line
    Dim PN_Notes As String
    PN_Notes = "1/4" & Chr(34) & " X 3/8" & Chr(34) & "  Key (436-0096) is required"
    
    Select Case pintType
        Case 1 'manual
            'no selection, pull cord is selected in track
        Case 2 'RW GHC
            Call FillSummaryGrid("131-109", IIf(MeasU = 1, 312, 7924.8), "Sections", GetSubCategory("131-109"), 312, IIf(varDoorData.KeyedTubeShaft = True, PN_Notes, "")) 'chain comes in 26' packages but they order by length
            
            Call FillSummaryGrid("7-3069", 1, "Sections", GetSubCategory("7-3069"), 0)
            Call FillSummaryGrid("7-2339", 1, "Sections", GetSubCategory("7-2339"), 0)

''''''''''Change in .NET''''''''''''''''
'            Call FillSummaryGrid("7-2340", 1, "Sections", GetSubCategory("7-2340"), 0)
            If varDoorData.ShaftDia = 1 Then
                Call FillSummaryGrid("7-2340", 1, "Sections", GetSubCategory("7-2340"), 0)
            Else
                Call FillSummaryGrid("7-2342", 1, "Sections", GetSubCategory("7-2342"), 0)
            End If
''''''''''END Change in .NET
            
            Call FillSummaryGrid("435-0107", 1, "Sections", GetSubCategory("435-0107"), 0)
            Call FillSummaryGrid("435-0108", 1, "Sections", GetSubCategory("435-0108"), 0)
        Case 3 '2000

''''''''''Change in .NET''''''''''''''''
'            Call FillSummaryGrid("950-105", 1, "Sections", GetSubCategory("950-105"), 0)
            If varDoorData.ShaftDia = 1 Then
                Call FillSummaryGrid("950-105", 1, "Sections", GetSubCategory("950-105"), 0, IIf(varDoorData.KeyedTubeShaft = True, PN_Notes, ""))
            Else
                Call FillSummaryGrid("950-106", 1, "Sections", GetSubCategory("950-106"), 0, IIf(varDoorData.KeyedTubeShaft = True, PN_Notes, ""))
            End If
''''''''''END Change in .NET
        Case 4 'JR
                If varDoorData.ShaftDia = 1 Then
                    Call FillSummaryGrid("950-110", 1, "Sections", GetSubCategory("950-110"), 0, IIf(varDoorData.KeyedTubeShaft = True, PN_Notes, ""))
                Else
                    Call FillSummaryGrid("950-112", 1, "Sections", GetSubCategory("950-112"), 0, IIf(varDoorData.KeyedTubeShaft = True, PN_Notes, ""))
                End If
                
        Case 5 'RW Direct
            Call FillSummaryGrid("7-3068", 1, "Sections", GetSubCategory("7-3068"), 0, IIf(varDoorData.KeyedTubeShaft = True, PN_Notes, ""))
        Case 6 'Dyna-Hoist
            'no selection (future zap)
        Case 7 'Draw bar 1 trolley
            If ProdType = "Thermatite" Or ProdType = "Thermalume" Then
                If varDoorData.arrSections(0).intHeight = 21 Then
                    Call FillSummaryGrid("426-0311", 1, "Sections", GetSubCategory("426-0311"), 0)
                Else
                    Call FillSummaryGrid("426-0310", 1, "Sections", GetSubCategory("426-0310"), 0)
                End If
            End If
        Case 8 'Draw bar 2 trolley
            If ProdType = "Thermatite" Or ProdType = "Thermalume" Then
                If varDoorData.arrSections(0).intHeight = 21 Then
                    Call FillSummaryGrid("426-0311", 2, "Sections", GetSubCategory("426-0311"), 0)
                Else
                    Call FillSummaryGrid("426-0310", 2, "Sections", GetSubCategory("426-0310"), 0)
                End If
            End If

''''''''''Change in .NET''''''''''''''''
        Case 9 '200 D Direct Drive
            Call FillSummaryGrid("950-104", 1, "Sections", GetSubCategory("950-104"), 0, IIf(varDoorData.KeyedTubeShaft = True, PN_Notes, ""))
''''''''''END Change in .NET
            
        Case 10 'jackshaft
            'no selection, shaft len changes.
            
        Case 11
            If varDoorData.ShaftDia = 1 Then
                Call FillSummaryGrid("950-111", 1, "Sections", GetSubCategory("950-110"), 0, IIf(varDoorData.KeyedTubeShaft = True, PN_Notes, ""))
            Else
                Call FillSummaryGrid("950-113", 1, "Sections", GetSubCategory("950-113"), 0, IIf(varDoorData.KeyedTubeShaft = True, PN_Notes, ""))
            End If
    End Select
    
    Exit Function

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Function

Public Sub SelectMiscFastenPkg()
    
    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".SelectMiscFastenPkg()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""

    Dim i As Integer, P As String
    P = ""
    i = 1
    If (ProdType = "RollTite") Then
        P = "4-0055"
        If (DrWidth > 242) Then
            i = 2
        End If
    Else
        P = "4-0032"
    End If
    Call FillSummaryGrid(P, i, "Sections", GetSubCategory(P), 0)
    
    Exit Sub

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, lstrErrDescr & vbCrLf & Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Sub

Public Sub CalcAlumatiteSections()
    
    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".CalcAlumatiteSections()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""

    Dim H As Integer, i As Integer, j As Integer
    Dim T As Integer, S As String
    frmSectionsEntry.Grd.ToolTipText = ""
    H = DrHgt
    T = H \ 24
    If ((H Mod 24) > 1) Then
        T = T + 1
    End If
    N24 = T
    N21 = 0
    
    If (T > 0) Then
        i = T
        With frmSectionsEntry.Grd
        .Row = 1
        'Set number of bottom sections
        .Col = 1
        .Text = "1"
        i = i - 1
        'Set number of top sections
        .Col = 4
        If (i > 0) Then
            .Text = 1
            i = i - 1
        Else
            .Text = "0"
        End If
        'set number of lock sections
        .Col = 2
        If (i > 0) Then
            .Text = "1"
            i = i - 1
        Else
            .Text = "0"
        End If
        'set number of intermediate sections
        .Col = 3
        If (i > 0) Then
            .Text = Format$(i, "##0")
        Else
            .Text = "0"
        End If
        'Set total number of sections
        .Col = 5
        .Text = Format$(T, "##0")
        End With
    Else
    End If
    
    Exit Sub

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, lstrErrDescr & vbCrLf & Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)
End Sub

Public Sub FillGrd()

    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".FillGrd()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""
    
    Dim Inter21 As Integer
    Dim Inter24 As Integer
    Inter24 = N24
    Inter21 = N21

    frmSectionsEntry.Grd.Clear
    Call SetGrdTitles
    
    frmSectionsEntry.Grd.ToolTipText = "Double click to reconfigure sections"
    
    frmSectionsEntry.Grd.Col = 5
    
    frmSectionsEntry.Grd.Row = 1
    frmSectionsEntry.Grd.Text = N24
    
    frmSectionsEntry.Grd.Row = 2
    frmSectionsEntry.Grd.Text = N21
    
    frmSectionsEntry.Grd.Col = 1
    
    frmSectionsEntry.Grd.Row = 1
    If varDoorData.arrSections(UBound(varDoorData.arrSections())).intHeight = 24 Then
        frmSectionsEntry.Grd.Text = "1"
        Inter24 = Inter24 - 1
    Else
        frmSectionsEntry.Grd.Text = "0"
    End If
    
    frmSectionsEntry.Grd.Col = 1
    
    frmSectionsEntry.Grd.Row = 2
    If varDoorData.arrSections(UBound(varDoorData.arrSections())).intHeight = 21 Then
        frmSectionsEntry.Grd.Text = "1"
        Inter21 = Inter21 - 1
    Else
        frmSectionsEntry.Grd.Text = "0"
    End If
    
    frmSectionsEntry.Grd.Col = 4
    
    frmSectionsEntry.Grd.Row = 2
    If UBound(varDoorData.arrSections()) > 0 Then
        If varDoorData.arrSections(0).intHeight = 21 Then
            frmSectionsEntry.Grd.Text = "1"
            Inter21 = Inter21 - 1
        Else
            frmSectionsEntry.Grd.Text = "0"
        End If
    Else
        frmSectionsEntry.Grd.Text = "0"
    End If
    
    frmSectionsEntry.Grd.Col = 4
    
    frmSectionsEntry.Grd.Row = 1
    If UBound(varDoorData.arrSections()) > 0 Then
        If varDoorData.arrSections(0).intHeight = 24 Then
            frmSectionsEntry.Grd.Text = "1"
            Inter24 = Inter24 - 1
        Else
            frmSectionsEntry.Grd.Text = "0"
        End If
    Else
        frmSectionsEntry.Grd.Text = "0"
    End If

    frmSectionsEntry.Grd.Col = 2
    
    frmSectionsEntry.Grd.Row = 1
    If UBound(varDoorData.arrSections()) > 0 Then
        If (N21 + N24) > 2 Then
            If varDoorData.arrSections(UBound(varDoorData.arrSections()) - 1).intHeight = 24 Then
                frmSectionsEntry.Grd.Text = "1"
                Inter24 = Inter24 - 1
            Else
                frmSectionsEntry.Grd.Text = "0"
            End If
        Else
            frmSectionsEntry.Grd.Text = "0"
        End If
    Else
        frmSectionsEntry.Grd.Text = "0"
    End If
    
    frmSectionsEntry.Grd.Col = 2
        
    frmSectionsEntry.Grd.Row = 2
    If UBound(varDoorData.arrSections()) > 0 Then
         If (N21 + N24) > 2 Then
            If varDoorData.arrSections(UBound(varDoorData.arrSections()) - 1).intHeight = 21 Then
                frmSectionsEntry.Grd.Text = "1"
                Inter21 = Inter21 - 1
            Else
                frmSectionsEntry.Grd.Text = "0"
            End If
        Else
            frmSectionsEntry.Grd.Text = "0"
        End If
    Else
        frmSectionsEntry.Grd.Text = "0"
    End If
    
    frmSectionsEntry.Grd.Col = 3
    
    frmSectionsEntry.Grd.Row = 1
    frmSectionsEntry.Grd.Text = Inter24
    
    frmSectionsEntry.Grd.Row = 2
    frmSectionsEntry.Grd.Text = Inter21
    
    Exit Sub

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Sub

Public Sub SetGrdTitles()

    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".SetGrdTitles()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""
    
    frmSectionsEntry.Grd.RowHeight(2) = frmSectionsEntry.Grd.RowHeight(1)
    frmSectionsEntry.Grd.Row = 0
    frmSectionsEntry.Grd.Col = 1
    frmSectionsEntry.Grd.Text = "Bottom"
    frmSectionsEntry.Grd.Col = 2
    frmSectionsEntry.Grd.Text = "Lock"
    frmSectionsEntry.Grd.Col = 3
    frmSectionsEntry.Grd.Text = "InterM"
    frmSectionsEntry.Grd.Col = 4
    frmSectionsEntry.Grd.Text = "Top"
    frmSectionsEntry.Grd.Col = 5
    frmSectionsEntry.Grd.Text = "Total"
    frmSectionsEntry.Grd.Col = 0
    frmSectionsEntry.Grd.Row = 1
    frmSectionsEntry.Grd.Text = "24"
    frmSectionsEntry.Grd.Row = 2
    frmSectionsEntry.Grd.Text = "21"

    Exit Sub

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Sub

Public Function GetSectionWeightPerHeight(ByVal pintH As Integer) As Single

    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".GetSectionWeightPerHeight()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""
    
    'returns average weight of one section in the door.
    
    Dim i As Integer, W As Single
    For i = 0 To UBound(varDoorData.arrSections())
        If varDoorData.arrSections(i).intHeight = pintH Then
            W = W + varDoorData.arrSections(i).sngWgt
        End If
    Next
    If W > 0 Then
        GetSectionWeightPerHeight = W / (IIf(pintH = 21, N21, N24))
    Else
        GetSectionWeightPerHeight = 0
    End If
    Exit Function

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, lstrErrDescr & vbCrLf & Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Function

Public Function CheckGroupID(ByVal PN As String) As Integer

    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".CheckGroupID()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""

    Dim lobjMainRS As ADODB.Recordset
    Dim lstrSQL As String
    Dim llngAffected As Long
    
    lstrSQL = "select PartNum, GroupID from Main"
    lstrSQL = lstrSQL & " where PartNum=" & SQLString(PN)
    Set lobjMainRS = CnnBTMS.Execute(lstrSQL, llngAffected, adCmdText)
    If Not lobjMainRS Is Nothing Then
        If Not (lobjMainRS.BOF And lobjMainRS.EOF) Then
            CheckGroupID = IIf(lobjMainRS.Fields("GroupID") > 0, lobjMainRS.Fields("GroupID"), 10)
        End If
    End If

    Exit Function

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, lstrErrDescr & vbCrLf & Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Function

Public Function GetPrepBarLatch() As Single

    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".GetPrepBarLatch()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""

    If varDoorData.arrSections(UBound(varDoorData.arrSections) - 1).intHeight = 24 Then
        Call FillSummaryGrid("426-0312", 1, "Sections", GetSubCategory("426-0312"), 0)
        GetPrepBarLatch = 4.846
    Else
        Call FillSummaryGrid("426-0313", 1, "Sections", GetSubCategory("426-0313"), 0)
        GetPrepBarLatch = 4.204
    End If
    
    Exit Function

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, lstrErrDescr & vbCrLf & Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Function

Public Function GetNumInsideHingesPerWidth() As Integer
    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".GetNumInsideHingesPerWidth()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""
    Dim H As Integer
    
    Select Case DrWidth 'get number of hinges per section
        Case Is <= 110
            H = 1
        Case Is <= 146
            H = 2
        Case Is <= 194
            H = 3
        Case Is <= 230
            H = 4
        Case Is <= 290
            H = 5
        Case Is <= 338
            H = 6
        Case Is <= 386
            H = 7
        Case Is <= 434
            H = 8
    End Select
    
    GetNumInsideHingesPerWidth = H
    
    Exit Function

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, lstrErrDescr & vbCrLf & Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Function

Public Function SelectPDKit() As String

    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".SelectPDKit()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""
    
    Dim lstrSect As String
    If UBound(varDoorData.arrSections) >= 3 Then lstrSect = varDoorData.arrSections(UBound(varDoorData.arrSections)).intHeight & _
        varDoorData.arrSections(UBound(varDoorData.arrSections) - 1).intHeight & _
        varDoorData.arrSections(UBound(varDoorData.arrSections) - 2).intHeight & _
        varDoorData.arrSections(UBound(varDoorData.arrSections) - 3).intHeight
    
''''''''''Change in .NET''''''''''''''''
'    Select Case lstrSect
'        Case "21212121"
'            SelectPDKit = "328-611"
'        Case "24212121"
'            SelectPDKit = "328-612"
'        Case "24242121"
'            SelectPDKit = "328-613"
'        Case "24242421"
'            SelectPDKit = "328-614"
'        Case "24242424"
'            SelectPDKit = "328-615"
'        Case Else
'            SelectPDKit = ""
'    End Select
    
'    Select Case lstrSect
'        Case "21212121"
'            SelectPDKit = IIf(DrType = "T150" Or DrType = "T150 Multi-Rib", "328-601", IIf(DrType = "T45", "FP0-0079", "328-611"))   'iif for false to add F/P
'        Case "24212121"
'            SelectPDKit = IIf(DrType = "T150" Or DrType = "T150 Multi-Rib", "328-602", IIf(DrType = "T45", "FP0-0080", "328-612"))
'        Case "24242121", "24212421"
'            SelectPDKit = IIf(DrType = "T150" Or DrType = "T150 Multi-Rib", "328-603", IIf(DrType = "T45", "FP0-0081", "328-613"))
'        Case "24242421"
'            SelectPDKit = IIf(DrType = "T150" Or DrType = "T150 Multi-Rib", "328-604", IIf(DrType = "T45", "FP0-0082", "328-614"))
'        Case "24242424"
'            SelectPDKit = IIf(DrType = "T150" Or DrType = "T150 Multi-Rib", "328-605", IIf(DrType = "T45", "FP0-0083", "328-615"))
'        Case Else
'            SelectPDKit = ""
'    End Select

'    Select Case lstrSect
'        Case "21212121"
'            SelectPDKit = IIf(DrType = "T150" Or DrType = "T150 Multi-Rib", "328-601", IIf(DrType = "T200" Or DrType = "T200A" Or DrType = "ADV200", "328-671", "328-611"))
'        Case "24212121"
'            SelectPDKit = IIf(DrType = "T150" Or DrType = "T150 Multi-Rib", "328-602", IIf(DrType = "T200" Or DrType = "T200A" Or DrType = "ADV200", "328-672", "328-612"))
'        Case "24242121", "24212421"
'            SelectPDKit = IIf(DrType = "T150" Or DrType = "T150 Multi-Rib", "328-603", IIf(DrType = "T200" Or DrType = "T200A" Or DrType = "ADV200", "328-673", "328-613"))
'        Case "24242421"
'            SelectPDKit = IIf(DrType = "T150" Or DrType = "T150 Multi-Rib", "328-604", IIf(DrType = "T200" Or DrType = "T200A" Or DrType = "ADV200", "328-674", "328-614"))
'        Case "24242424"
'            SelectPDKit = IIf(DrType = "T150" Or DrType = "T150 Multi-Rib", "328-605", IIf(DrType = "T200" Or DrType = "T200A" Or DrType = "ADV200", "328-675", "328-615"))
'        Case Else
'            SelectPDKit = ""
'    End Select
    
    Select Case lstrSect
        Case "21212121"
            If DrType = "T150" Or DrType = "T150 Multi-Rib" Then
                SelectPDKit = "328-601"
            ElseIf DrType = "T200" Or DrType = "T200 Multi-Rib" Or DrType = "T200A" Or DrType = "T200A Multi-Rib" Or DrType = "ADV200" Or DrType = "ADV200 Multi-Rib" Then
                SelectPDKit = "328-671"
            ElseIf DrType = "T35" Then
                SelectPDKit = "328-743"
            ElseIf DrType = "T45" Then
                SelectPDKit = "328-763"
            Else
                SelectPDKit = "328-611"
            End If
            
            'SelectPDKit = IIf(DrType = "T150" Or DrType = "T150 Multi-Rib", "328-601", IIf(DrType = "T200" Or DrType = "T200A" Or DrType = "ADV200", "328-671", "328-611"))
        Case "24212121"
            If DrType = "T150" Or DrType = "T150 Multi-Rib" Then
                SelectPDKit = "328-602"
            ElseIf DrType = "T200" Or DrType = "T200 Multi-Rib" Or DrType = "T200A" Or DrType = "T200A Multi-Rib" Or DrType = "ADV200" Or DrType = "ADV200 Multi-Rib" Then
                SelectPDKit = "328-672"
            ElseIf DrType = "T35" Then
                SelectPDKit = "328-744"
            ElseIf DrType = "T45" Then
                SelectPDKit = "328-764"
            Else
                SelectPDKit = "328-612"
            End If
            
            'SelectPDKit = IIf(DrType = "T150" Or DrType = "T150 Multi-Rib", "328-602", IIf(DrType = "T200" Or DrType = "T200A" Or DrType = "ADV200", "328-672", "328-612"))
        Case "24242121", "24212421"
            If DrType = "T150" Or DrType = "T150 Multi-Rib" Then
                SelectPDKit = "328-603"
            ElseIf DrType = "T200" Or DrType = "T200 Multi-Rib" Or DrType = "T200A" Or DrType = "T200A Multi-Rib" Or DrType = "ADV200" Or DrType = "ADV200 Multi-Rib" Then
                SelectPDKit = "328-673"
            ElseIf DrType = "T35" Then
                SelectPDKit = "328-745"
            ElseIf DrType = "T45" Then
                SelectPDKit = "328-765"
            Else
                SelectPDKit = "328-613"
            End If
        
            'SelectPDKit = IIf(DrType = "T150" Or DrType = "T150 Multi-Rib", "328-603", IIf(DrType = "T200" Or DrType = "T200A" Or DrType = "ADV200", "328-673", "328-613"))
        Case "24242421"
            If DrType = "T150" Or DrType = "T150 Multi-Rib" Then
                SelectPDKit = "328-604"
            ElseIf DrType = "T200" Or DrType = "T200 Multi-Rib" Or DrType = "T200A" Or DrType = "T200A Multi-Rib" Or DrType = "ADV200" Or DrType = "ADV200 Multi-Rib" Then
                SelectPDKit = "328-674"
            ElseIf DrType = "T35" Then
                SelectPDKit = "328-746"
            ElseIf DrType = "T45" Then
                SelectPDKit = "328-766"
            Else
                SelectPDKit = "328-614"
            End If
            
            'SelectPDKit = IIf(DrType = "T150" Or DrType = "T150 Multi-Rib", "328-604", IIf(DrType = "T200" Or DrType = "T200A" Or DrType = "ADV200", "328-674", "328-614"))
        Case "24242424"
            If DrType = "T150" Or DrType = "T150 Multi-Rib" Then
                SelectPDKit = "328-605"
            ElseIf DrType = "T200" Or DrType = "T200 Multi-Rib" Or DrType = "T200A" Or DrType = "T200A Multi-Rib" Or DrType = "ADV200" Or DrType = "ADV200 Multi-Rib" Then

        Case Else
            SelectPDKit = ""
    End Select
''''''''''END Change in .NET

    Exit Function

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, lstrErrDescr & vbCrLf & Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)


End Function

''''''''''Change in .NET''''''''''''''''


Public Function GetSectionWidth(ByVal psngW As Single) As Single

    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".GetSectionWidth()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""
    
    If DrType = "T175" Or DrType = "T150" Or DrType = "T175-20" Or DrType = "T165" Or DrType = "T140" Or DrType = "T175A" Or _
        DrType = "T150 Multi-Rib" Or DrType = "T175 Multi-Rib" Then
        
        GetSectionWidth = psngW - 0.375    '3/8 of an inch is needed to put end caps
    ElseIf DrType = "T45" Or DrType = "T35" Then
        GetSectionWidth = psngW - 0.125     'corrected in 2.0.14
    ElseIf DrType = "T200" Or DrType = "T200 Multi-Rib" Or DrType = "ADV200" Or DrType = "T200A" Or DrType = "T200A Multi-Rib" Or DrType = "ADV200 Multi-Rib" Then
        GetSectionWidth = psngW - 0.25     ' 1/4" for end caps
    ElseIf DrType = "T138" Then
        GetSectionWidth = psngW - 0.125
    ElseIf DrType = "T300" Or DrType = "T300 Multi-Rib" Then
        GetSectionWidth = psngW - 0.375    '3/8 of an inch is needed to put end caps
    Else
        GetSectionWidth = psngW            'no end caps on other door types
    End If

    Exit Function

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, lstrErrDescr & vbCrLf & Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Function

'
'    On Error GoTo Error_Handler

Public Function GetPhantomInfo(ByVal strBasePartNum As String, _
                               ByVal dblInches As Double, _
                               ByVal blnMetric As Boolean) As String
    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".GetPhantomInfo()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""

    Dim ldblFrac As Double
    Dim lstrDesc As String
    Dim lstrPart As String
    Dim lstrTemp As String
    Dim lstrPartDesc As String
    Dim llngFeet As Long
    Dim llngInch As Long
    
    lstrDesc = ""
    llngFeet = dblInches \ 12
    llngInch = dblInches - (llngFeet * 12)
    If ((llngFeet * 12) + llngInch) > dblInches Then llngInch = llngInch - 1
    ldblFrac = (dblInches - ((llngFeet * 12) + llngInch))
    
    If blnMetric = False Then
        
        lstrPartDesc = GetPartDesc(strBasePartNum, "I")
        lstrDesc = lstrDesc & Val(llngFeet) & "' "
        lstrDesc = lstrDesc & Val(llngInch) & """"
        If ldblFrac > 0 Then
            lstrDesc = lstrDesc & "-"
            lstrDesc = lstrDesc & GetFraction(ldblFrac)
        End If
        If Not lstrDesc = "" Then lstrDesc = " (" & lstrDesc & " wide)"
        lstrPartDesc = lstrPartDesc & lstrDesc
    Else
       
        lstrPartDesc = GetPartDesc(strBasePartNum, "M")
        lstrDesc = " (" & Format(dblInches, cstrQtyMask) & "mm wide)"
        lstrPartDesc = lstrPartDesc & lstrDesc
    End If
    
    'Calculate the part number
    lstrTemp = ""
    lstrPart = strBasePartNum 'Left(strBasePartNum, Len(strBasePartNum) - 3)
    lstrTemp = CStr(llngFeet)
    lstrPart = lstrPart & Left("00", 2 - Len(lstrTemp)) & lstrTemp
    lstrTemp = CStr(llngInch)
    If Len(lstrTemp) = 2 Then
        lstrPart = lstrPart & IIf((Left("00", 2 - Len(lstrTemp)) & lstrTemp) = "00", "0", (Left("00", 2 - Len(lstrTemp)) & lstrTemp))
    Else
        lstrPart = lstrPart & IIf((Left("0", 1 - Len(lstrTemp)) & lstrTemp) = "00", "0", (Left("0", 1 - Len(lstrTemp)) & lstrTemp))
    End If
    lstrTemp = ""
    If ldblFrac > 0 Then
        lstrTemp = CLng(ldblFrac * 10000)
        lstrPart = lstrPart & IIf(ldblFrac = 0, "", "-" & Left("0000", 4 - Len(lstrTemp)) & lstrTemp)
    End If
    
    
    GetPhantomInfo = lstrPart & "~" & lstrPartDesc

    
    
    Exit Function

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, lstrErrDescr & vbCrLf & Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)
End Function

Public Function FindNextNoCut() As Double
    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".FindNextNoCut()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""

    Dim lstrSQL As String
    Dim lobjSecConfRS As ADODB.Recordset
    Dim llngAffected As Long
    FindNextNoCut = 0
    lstrSQL = "SELECT * from SectionsConfig where CutTop+CutBtm=0 order by drHt"
    Set lobjSecConfRS = CnnBTMS.Execute(lstrSQL, llngAffected, adCmdText)
    If Not lobjSecConfRS Is Nothing Then
        lobjSecConfRS.ActiveConnection = Nothing
        If Not (lobjSecConfRS.BOF And lobjSecConfRS.EOF) Then
            lobjSecConfRS.MoveFirst
            Do While lobjSecConfRS.EOF = False
                If lobjSecConfRS.Fields("drht").Value > DrHgt Then
                    FindNextNoCut = DBLValue(lobjSecConfRS.Fields("drht").Value)
                    Exit Do
                End If
                lobjSecConfRS.MoveNext
            Loop
        End If
        Set lobjSecConfRS = Nothing
    End If
 
    Exit Function

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, lstrErrDescr & vbCrLf & Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Function
Public Function AddPartsForPassDoorWithSectionsOnly() As Single
    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".AddPartsForPassDoorWithSectionsOnly()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""

    'add 12 of the narrow rail hinges 950-081 and
    '431-??? 16Ga truss.
    Dim PN As String
    Dim W As Single
    Dim QTY As Integer
    
    'hinges''''''''''''''''''''
    CRow = CRow + 1
    If CRow > 1 Then frmGrid.Grid1.Rows = CRow + 1

    frmGrid.Grid1.Row = CRow
    
    frmGrid.Grid1.Row = CRow
    frmGrid.Grid1.Col = 0
    frmGrid.Grid1.Text = "9-2317"
    frmGrid.Grid1.Col = 1
    frmGrid.Grid1.Text = 12
    frmGrid.Grid1.Col = 2
    frmGrid.Grid1.Text = "Sections"
    frmGrid.Grid1.Col = 3
    frmGrid.Grid1.Text = GetSubCategory("9-2317")
    frmGrid.Grid1.Col = 4
    frmGrid.Grid1.Text = ""
    frmGrid.Grid1.Col = 5
    frmGrid.Grid1.Text = ""
    W = (0.23 * 12)
    
    'truss'''''''''''''''''''''''
    If varPassDoor.btBottom = 2 Or varPassDoor.btBottom = 3 Or varPassDoor.btTop = 3 Then
        Select Case DrWidth - 6
        Case Is <= 144
            If (varPassDoor.btBottom = 2 Or varPassDoor.btBottom = 3) And varPassDoor.btTop = 3 Then
                MRSBWgt = MRSBWgt + (2 * ((15.75 / 144) * (DrWidth - 6)))
            Else
                MRSBWgt = MRSBWgt + (15.75 / 144) * (DrWidth - 6)
            End If
            PN = "431-0112"
        Case Is <= 168
            If (varPassDoor.btBottom = 2 Or varPassDoor.btBottom = 3) And varPassDoor.btTop = 3 Then
                MRSBWgt = MRSBWgt + (2 * ((18.37 / 168) * (DrWidth - 6)))
            Else
                MRSBWgt = MRSBWgt + (18.37 / 168) * (DrWidth - 6)
            End If
            PN = "431-0114"
        Case Is <= 192
            If (varPassDoor.btBottom = 2 Or varPassDoor.btBottom = 3) And varPassDoor.btTop = 3 Then
                MRSBWgt = MRSBWgt + (2 * ((21 / 192) * (DrWidth - 6)))
            Else
                MRSBWgt = MRSBWgt + (21 / 192) * (DrWidth - 6)
            End If
            PN = "431-0116"
        Case Is <= 216
            If (varPassDoor.btBottom = 2 Or varPassDoor.btBottom = 3) And varPassDoor.btTop = 3 Then
                MRSBWgt = MRSBWgt + (2 * ((23.62 / 216) * (DrWidth - 6)))
            Else
                MRSBWgt = MRSBWgt + (23.62 / 216) * (DrWidth - 6)
            End If
            PN = "431-0118"
        Case Is <= 240
            If (varPassDoor.btBottom = 2 Or varPassDoor.btBottom = 3) And varPassDoor.btTop = 3 Then
                MRSBWgt = MRSBWgt + (2 * ((26.25 / 240) * (DrWidth - 6)))
            Else
                MRSBWgt = MRSBWgt + (26.25 / 240) * (DrWidth - 6)
            End If
            PN = "431-0120"
        Case Is <= 264
            If (varPassDoor.btBottom = 2 Or varPassDoor.btBottom = 3) And varPassDoor.btTop = 3 Then
                MRSBWgt = MRSBWgt + (2 * ((28.87 / 264) * (DrWidth - 6)))
            Else
                MRSBWgt = MRSBWgt + (28.87 / 264) * (DrWidth - 6)
            End If
            PN = "431-0122"
        Case Is <= 288
            If (varPassDoor.btBottom = 2 Or varPassDoor.btBottom = 3) And varPassDoor.btTop = 3 Then
                MRSBWgt = MRSBWgt + (2 * ((31.5 / 288) * (DrWidth - 6)))
            Else
                MRSBWgt = MRSBWgt + (31.5 / 288) * (DrWidth - 6)
            End If
            PN = "431-0124"
        Case Is <= 312
            If (varPassDoor.btBottom = 2 Or varPassDoor.btBottom = 3) And varPassDoor.btTop = 3 Then
                MRSBWgt = MRSBWgt + (2 * ((34.12 / 312) * (DrWidth - 6)))
            Else
                MRSBWgt = MRSBWgt + (34.12 / 312) * (DrWidth - 6)
            End If
            PN = "431-0126"
        Case Is <= 360
            If (varPassDoor.btBottom = 2 Or varPassDoor.btBottom = 3) And varPassDoor.btTop = 3 Then
                MRSBWgt = MRSBWgt + (2 * ((39.37 / 360) * (DrWidth - 6)))
            Else
                MRSBWgt = MRSBWgt + (39.37 / 360) * (DrWidth - 6)
            End If
            PN = "431-0130"
        End Select
        
        CRow = CRow + 1
        If CRow > 1 Then frmGrid.Grid1.Rows = CRow + 1
    
        frmGrid.Grid1.Row = CRow
        
        frmGrid.Grid1.Row = CRow
        frmGrid.Grid1.Col = 0
        frmGrid.Grid1.Text = PN
        frmGrid.Grid1.Col = 1
        frmGrid.Grid1.Text = IIf((varPassDoor.btBottom = 2 Or varPassDoor.btBottom = 3) And varPassDoor.btTop = 3, 2, 1) 'top and bottom truss or just one.
        frmGrid.Grid1.Col = 2
        frmGrid.Grid1.Text = "Sections"
        frmGrid.Grid1.Col = 3
        frmGrid.Grid1.Text = GetSubCategory(PN)
        frmGrid.Grid1.Col = 4
        frmGrid.Grid1.Text = ""
        frmGrid.Grid1.Col = 5
        ClearConObj
        aCon.Inch = DrWidth - 6
        aCon.mmcalc
        frmGrid.Grid1.Text = "Cut to " & IIf(MeasU = 1, GetImpString(DrWidth - 6), Round(aCon.mm, 0) & " mm")
        
    End If
    
    'tek screws loose''''''''''''''''''''''''
    QTY = (RoundUp(DrWidth / 12) + 1)
    If varPassDoor.btTop > 0 Then
        QTY = QTY + (RoundUp(DrWidth / 12) + 1)
    End If
    
    W = W + QTY * 0.012
    
    CRow = CRow + 1
    If CRow > 1 Then frmGrid.Grid1.Rows = CRow + 1

    frmGrid.Grid1.Row = CRow
    
    frmGrid.Grid1.Row = CRow
    frmGrid.Grid1.Col = 0
    frmGrid.Grid1.Text = "215-306"
    frmGrid.Grid1.Col = 1
    frmGrid.Grid1.Text = QTY
    frmGrid.Grid1.Col = 2
    frmGrid.Grid1.Text = "Sections"
    frmGrid.Grid1.Col = 3
    frmGrid.Grid1.Text = GetSubCategory("215-306")
    frmGrid.Grid1.Col = 4
    frmGrid.Grid1.Text = ""
    frmGrid.Grid1.Col = 5
    frmGrid.Grid1.Text = ""
    
    'tek screws pack of 28
    CRow = CRow + 1
    If CRow > 1 Then frmGrid.Grid1.Rows = CRow + 1

    frmGrid.Grid1.Row = CRow
    
    frmGrid.Grid1.Row = CRow
    frmGrid.Grid1.Col = 0
    frmGrid.Grid1.Text = "8-9028"
    frmGrid.Grid1.Col = 1
    frmGrid.Grid1.Text = 2
    frmGrid.Grid1.Col = 2
    frmGrid.Grid1.Text = "Sections"
    frmGrid.Grid1.Col = 3
    frmGrid.Grid1.Text = GetSubCategory("8-9028")
    frmGrid.Grid1.Col = 4
    frmGrid.Grid1.Text = ""
    frmGrid.Grid1.Col = 5
    frmGrid.Grid1.Text = ""
    W = W + 0.62
    
    AddPartsForPassDoorWithSectionsOnly = W
    
    Exit Function

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, lstrErrDescr & vbCrLf & Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Function

Public Function GetTopRollBracket_CE() As Single

    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".GetTopRollBracket_CE()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""
    
    Dim PN(3) As String
    Dim W As Single
    Dim Q(3) As Integer
    
    If Not (DrType = "Low Headroom Front Mount" Or DrType = "Low Headroom Rear Mount") Then
        'Engineering has just discovered that this will not work.  For all cases of SL, HL, VL and VL(LHR) instead of the parts selected above, we will instead select
        '(2) 322-225 (4 for double end)

'        PN(0) = "111-1474"
'        Q(0) = IIf(varDoorData.DblEndHinges = True, 4, 2)
'        PN(1) = "322-225"
'        Q(1) = 2
'        PN(2) = "425-0121"
'        Q(2) = IIf(varDoorData.DblEndHinges = True, 4, 2)
'        W = IIf(varDoorData.DblEndHinges = True, 4.24, 2.46)
        
        
        'commented for 2.0.19 instead introduce the door width and door height limitations for 322-224 (new part added in database) for T35/T45
        
'        PN(0) = "322-225"
'        Q(0) = IIf(varDoorData.DblEndHinges = True, 4, 2)
'        PN(1) = ""
'        Q(1) = 0
'        PN(2) = ""
'        Q(2) = 0

        If DrWidth <= 194 And DrHgt <= 120 Then
            PN(0) = "322-224"
            Q(0) = IIf(varDoorData.DblEndHinges = True, 4, 2)
            PN(1) = ""
            Q(1) = 0
            PN(2) = ""
            Q(2) = 0
        Else
            PN(0) = "322-225"
            Q(0) = IIf(varDoorData.DblEndHinges = True, 4, 2)
            PN(1) = ""
            Q(1) = 0
            PN(2) = ""
            Q(2) = 0
        End If
    Else
        PN(0) = "425-0103"
        Q(0) = IIf(varDoorData.DblEndHinges = True, 4, 2)
        PN(1) = ""
        Q(1) = 0
        PN(2) = ""
        Q(2) = 0
        W = IIf(varDoorData.DblEndHinges = True, 4, 2) * 0.74
    End If
    
    
    Dim i As Integer
    For i = 0 To 3
        If Q(i) <> 0 Then
            Call FillSummaryGrid(PN(i), Q(i), "Sections", GetSubCategory(PN(i)), 0)
        End If
    Next
    
    GetTopRollBracket_CE = W
    
    Exit Function

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, lstrErrDescr & vbCrLf & Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Function
Public Function GetBottomRollBrackets_CE(ByVal pblnDbl As Boolean) As Single
    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".GetBottomRollBrackets_CE()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""
    
    Dim PN(4) As String
    Dim W As Single
    Dim Q(4) As Integer
    
    If ProdType = "Thermatite" Or ProdType = "Thermalume" Or ProdType = "Alumatite" Or ProdType = "Replacement" Then
        If HWType = 2 Then
            PN(0) = "950-168"
            Q(0) = 1
            PN(1) = "950-169"
            Q(1) = 1
            W = 2.94
            If varDoorData.DblEndHinges = True Then
                PN(2) = "7-2387"
                Q(2) = 2
                W = W + (0.77 * 2)
            End If
        Else
            PN(0) = "950-174"
            Q(0) = 1
            PN(1) = "950-175"
            Q(1) = 1
            W = 9.88
            If varDoorData.DblEndHinges = True Then
                PN(2) = "7-2343"
                Q(2) = 2
                W = W + (0.76 * 2)
            End If
        End If
    End If
    
    W = W + 0.06 * IIf(DrWidth > 242, 2, 1)  ' add weight of pins

    Dim i As Integer
    For i = 0 To 3
        If Q(i) <> 0 Then
            Call FillSummaryGrid(PN(i), Q(i), "Sections", GetSubCategory(PN(i)), 0)
        End If
    Next
    
    GetBottomRollBrackets_CE = W

    Exit Function

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, lstrErrDescr & vbCrLf & Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Function

Public Function GetBottomRollBrackets_NonCE(ByVal pblnDbl As Boolean) As Single

    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".GetBottomRollBrackets_NonCE()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""
    
    Dim PN(4) As String
    Dim W As Single
    Dim Q(4) As Integer
    If ProdType = "Thermatite" Or ProdType = "Thermalume" Or ProdType = "Alumatite" Or ProdType = "Replacement" Then
        If LiftType = "Low Headroom Rear Mount" Then
            If HWType = 2 Then
                PN(0) = "950-151"
                Q(0) = 1
                PN(1) = "950-152"
                Q(1) = 1
                W = 2.94
                If varDoorData.DblEndHinges = True Then
                    PN(2) = "7-2387"
                    Q(2) = 2
                    W = W + (0.77 * 2)
                End If
            Else
                PN(0) = "950-090"
                Q(0) = 1
                PN(1) = "950-091"
                Q(1) = 1
                W = 9.88
                If varDoorData.DblEndHinges = True Then
                    PN(2) = "7-2343"
                    Q(2) = 2
                    W = W + (0.76 * 2)
                End If
            End If
        Else
            If varDoorData.DblEndHinges = False Then
                If HWType = 2 Then
                    PN(0) = "436-0049"
                    Q(0) = 1
                    PN(1) = "436-0048"
                    Q(1) = 1
                    W = 1.78
                Else
                    PN(0) = "7-2362"
                    Q(0) = 1
                    PN(1) = "7-2361"
                    Q(1) = 1
                    W = 1.66
                End If
            Else
                If HWType = 2 Then
                    If DrWgt < 1000 - 3.33 Then     'the weight of the btm brkt
                        PN(0) = "436-0049"          'is added to the weigth after
                        Q(0) = 1                    'this routine is called
                        PN(1) = "436-0048"          'yet if the drweight total is 1000
                        Q(1) = 1                    'we need to use the heavy brkts.
                        PN(2) = "7-2387"            'this way if drwgt at this moment is
                        Q(2) = 2                    '996.67 then after adding 3.33 for the brkts,
                        W = 3.32                    'the wgt will be 999.99, which if fine for lite brkts.
                    Else
                        'NOTE: 7-3304 / 7-3305 should be picked only if HWSize = 3"/3.25"
'                        PN(0) = "7-3304"
'                        Q(0) = 1
'                        PN(1) = "7-3305"
'                        Q(1) = 1
'                        W = 9.78
                    End If
                Else
                    If DrWgt < 1000 - 3.19 Then
                        PN(0) = "7-2362"
                        Q(0) = 1
                        PN(1) = "7-2361"
                        Q(1) = 1
                        PN(2) = "7-2343"
                        Q(2) = 2
                        W = 3.18
                    Else
                        PN(0) = "7-3304"
                        Q(0) = 1
                        PN(1) = "7-3305"
                        Q(1) = 1
                        W = 9.78
                    End If
                End If
            End If
        End If
    End If
    
    Call FillSummaryGrid("4-0032", 1, "Sections", GetSubCategory("4-0032"), 0)
    'pack of 2 clevis pins per any two bottom brackets
    W = W + 0.06 * IIf(DrWidth > 242, 2, 1)  ' add weight of pins

    Dim i As Integer
    For i = 0 To 3
        If Q(i) <> 0 Then
            Call FillSummaryGrid(PN(i), Q(i), "Sections", GetSubCategory(PN(i)), 0)
        End If
    Next
    
    GetBottomRollBrackets_NonCE = W
    
    Exit Function

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, lstrErrDescr & vbCrLf & Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)


End Function

Public Function GetLinHinge_T45(ByVal Ind As Integer, ByVal pblnHW As Boolean) As String

    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".GetLinHinge_T45()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""
    
    'T45, T35
    
    If HWType = 2 Then
        If pblnHW = False Then
            GetLinHinge_T45 = Choose(Ind, "9-2361", "9-2362", "9-2363", "9-2364", "9-2365", "9-2366", "9-2377", "9-2378", "9-2379", "9-2380", "9-2381", "9-2382")
        Else
            GetLinHinge_T45 = Choose(Ind, "9-2361", "9-2362", "9-2373", "9-2374", "9-2375", "9-2376", "9-2377", "9-2378", "9-2379", "9-2380", "9-2381", "9-2382")
        End If
    ElseIf HWType = 3 Then
            GetLinHinge_T45 = Choose(Ind, "9-2373", "9-2374", "9-2375", "9-2376", "9-2377", "9-2378", "9-2379", "9-2380", "9-2381", "9-2382")
    ElseIf HWType = 3.25 Then
        GetLinHinge_T45 = Choose(Ind, "9-2333", "9-2334", "9-2335", "9-2336", "9-2337", "9-2338", "9-2339", "9-2340", "9-2341", "7-3172", "7-3173", "7-3174", "7-3175", "7-3176")
    End If
    
    Exit Function

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, lstrErrDescr & vbCrLf & Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Function

Public Function GetInsideHinges_T45() As Single

    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".GetInsideHinges_T45()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""
    
    'separately get hinges for sections with trusses and without.

    Dim PN(1) As String, W(1) As Single
    Dim TotalW As Single
    Dim QTruss As Integer
    Dim HTruss As Integer       'number of truss hinges
    Dim HNoTruss As Integer     'number of no truss hinges
    Dim NoHPerSec As Integer    'number of hinges per section
    Dim NHPerDoor As Integer    'number of inside hinges per door
    Dim i As Integer
    Dim HLoc() As Single         'location of first roller bracket
    Dim HCtr As Single          'distance btwn inside hinge centers
    Dim lstrLocations As String 'position of inside hinges saved in the part notes, will display below the part in pick list.
    HTruss = 0
    HNoTruss = 0
    
    For i = 0 To UBound(varDoorData.arrSections) 'get number of sections with truss
        If varDoorData.arrSections(i).intNTruss > 0 Then QTruss = QTruss + 1
    Next
    
    NoHPerSec = GetNumInsideHingesPerWidth_T45 'number of rows - for 2.0.14 release
    ReDim HLoc(NoHPerSec)
    
    If QTruss > 0 Then
        HTruss = QTruss - 1 'QTruss includes top section and inside hinges don't go on top
    End If
    
    HNoTruss = (NoHPerSec * (N21 + N24 - 1 - HTruss))      'get number of hinges for all no truss sections
    If HNoTruss < 0 Then HNoTruss = 0
    HTruss = NoHPerSec * HTruss                          'number of hinges for all truss sections
    
    'for pass door add 12
    If varPassDoor.strPN <> "" Then
        HNoTruss = NoTruss + 12
    End If
    
    If (ProdType = "Thermatite") Or (ProdType = "Thermalume") Or (ProdType = "Replacement") Then
        If HTruss = 0 Then             'if no truss
            PN(0) = "9-2367"
            W(0) = 0.23
        Else        'with truss
            If HNoTruss = 0 Then
                PN(1) = "9-2368"
                W(1) = 0.32
            Else    'combination
                PN(0) = "9-2367"
                PN(1) = "9-2368"
                W(0) = 0.23
                W(1) = 0.32
            End If
        End If
    ElseIf (ProdType = "Alumatite") Then
        PN(0) = "9-2367"
        W(0) = 0.23
    End If
    
    'Bill leather confirmed for 2.0.14 release - Below lines of code are being replaced by the new schedule below
    
'    If varDoorData.DblEndHinges = False Then
'        HCtr = (DrWidth - 3.125) / (NoHPerSec + 1)
'        HLoc(0) = 1.5625
'    Else
'        HCtr = (DrWidth - 9.375) / (NoHPerSec + 1)
'        HLoc(0) = 4.6875
'    End If
    
'    If MeasU = 0 Then
'        ClearConObj
'        aCon.Inch = HCtr
'        aCon.mmcalc
'        HCtr = aCon.mm
'        ClearConObj
'        aCon.Inch = HLoc(0)
'        aCon.mmcalc
'        HLoc(0) = aCon.mm
'    End If
    
'    If ProdType = "Thermatite" Or ProdType = "Thermalume" Then
'        For i = 1 To NoHPerSec
'            HLoc(i) = HLoc(i - 1) + HCtr
'            If i > 1 Then lstrLocations = lstrLocations & ", "
'            If MeasU = 1 Then
'                If Fix(HLoc(i)) = HLoc(i) Then 'a whole number.
'                    lstrLocations = lstrLocations & HLoc(i) & Chr(34)
'                Else
'                    Dim Min As Single
'                    Dim Max As Single
'                    Dim Dec As Single
'                    'need to get the lower and higher fraction, compare which is closer to the decimal and round up or down accordingly.
'                    Max = RoundFractionUp(HLoc(i) - Fix(HLoc(i)))
'                    Min = RoundFractionDown(HLoc(i) - Fix(HLoc(i)))
'                    Dec = IIf(Max - (HLoc(i) - Fix(HLoc(i))) > (HLoc(i) - Fix(HLoc(i))) - Min, Min, Max)
'
'                    lstrLocations = lstrLocations & Fix(HLoc(i)) & "-" & GetFraction(Dec) & Chr(34)
'                End If
'            Else
'                lstrLocations = lstrLocations & Round(HLoc(i), 0)
'            End If
'        Next
'    End If
    
    'new schedule for T45 and for T35
    
    Dim Min As Single
    Dim Max As Single
    Dim Dec As Single
    Dim lsngFactor As Single
                    
    If ProdType = "Thermatite" Or ProdType = "Thermalume" Then
        If varDoorData.DblEndHinges = False Then
            For i = 1 To NoHPerSec
                If i = 1 Then
                    HLoc(i) = (DrWidth - 3) / (NoHPerSec + 1) + 1.5
                    If MeasU = 0 Then
                        Call ClearConObj
                        aCon.Inch = HLoc(i)
                        aCon.mmcalc
                        HLoc(i) = aCon.mm
                    End If
                ElseIf i > 1 Then
                    lstrLocations = lstrLocations & ", "
                    If MeasU = 0 Then
                        Call ClearConObj
                        aCon.Inch = (DrWidth - 3) / (NoHPerSec + 1) 'converting rest of factor in the eq. to metric
                        aCon.mmcalc
                        lsngFactor = aCon.mm
                        
                        HLoc(i) = HLoc(i - 1) + lsngFactor
                    Else
                        HLoc(i) = HLoc(i - 1) + (DrWidth - 3) / (NoHPerSec + 1)
                    End If
                End If
                
                If MeasU = 1 Then
                    If Fix(HLoc(i)) = HLoc(i) Then 'a whole number.
                        lstrLocations = lstrLocations & HLoc(i) & Chr(34)
                    Else
                        
                        'need to get the lower and higher fraction, compare which is closer to the decimal and round up or down accordingly.
                        Max = RoundFractionUp(HLoc(i) - Fix(HLoc(i)))
                        Min = RoundFractionDown(HLoc(i) - Fix(HLoc(i)))
                        Dec = IIf(Max - (HLoc(i) - Fix(HLoc(i))) > (HLoc(i) - Fix(HLoc(i))) - Min, Min, Max)
                        
                        lstrLocations = lstrLocations & Fix(HLoc(i)) & "-" & GetFraction(Dec) & Chr(34)
                    End If
                Else
                    lstrLocations = lstrLocations & Round(HLoc(i), 0)
                End If
            Next
        Else
            For i = 1 To NoHPerSec
                If i = 1 Then
                    HLoc(i) = (DrWidth - 9) / (NoHPerSec + 1) + 4.5
                    If MeasU = 0 Then
                        Call ClearConObj
                        aCon.Inch = HLoc(i)
                        aCon.mmcalc
                        HLoc(i) = aCon.mm
                    End If
                ElseIf i > 1 Then
                    lstrLocations = lstrLocations & ", "
                    If MeasU = 0 Then
                        Call ClearConObj
                        aCon.Inch = (DrWidth - 9) / (NoHPerSec + 1) 'converting rest of factor in the eq. to metric
                        aCon.mmcalc
                        lsngFactor = aCon.mm
                        
                        HLoc(i) = HLoc(i - 1) + lsngFactor
                    Else
                        HLoc(i) = HLoc(i - 1) + (DrWidth - 9) / (NoHPerSec + 1)
                    End If
                End If
                
                If MeasU = 1 Then
                    If Fix(HLoc(i)) = HLoc(i) Then 'a whole number.
                        lstrLocations = lstrLocations & HLoc(i) & Chr(34)
                    Else
                        
                        'need to get the lower and higher fraction, compare which is closer to the decimal and round up or down accordingly.
                        Max = RoundFractionUp(HLoc(i) - Fix(HLoc(i)))
                        Min = RoundFractionDown(HLoc(i) - Fix(HLoc(i)))
                        Dec = IIf(Max - (HLoc(i) - Fix(HLoc(i))) > (HLoc(i) - Fix(HLoc(i))) - Min, Min, Max)
                        
                        lstrLocations = lstrLocations & Fix(HLoc(i)) & "-" & GetFraction(Dec) & Chr(34)
                    End If
                Else
                    lstrLocations = lstrLocations & Round(HLoc(i), 0)
                End If
            Next
        End If
    End If
    
    If varDoorData.TrussSchedule > 0 And NewLinearHW = True Then
        lstrLocations = "ICB (425-0562) and center hinge centers located at: " & lstrLocations & " from section edge"
    Else
        lstrLocations = IIf(lstrLocations <> "", "Hinge centers located at: " & lstrLocations & " from section edge", "")
    End If
    
    If HNoTruss > 0 Then
        Call FillSummaryGrid(PN(0), HNoTruss, "Sections", GetSubCategory(PN(0)), 0, lstrLocations)
    End If
    If HTruss > 0 Then
        Call FillSummaryGrid(PN(1), HTruss, "Sections", GetSubCategory(PN(1)), 0, IIf(HNoTruss = 0, lstrLocations, ""))
    End If
    
    NHPerDoor = HNoTruss + HTruss
    TotalW = (HNoTruss * W(0) + HTruss * W(1))
    
    If varDoorData.TrussSchedule > 0 And NewLinearHW = True Then 'Intermediate Cross Brace
        Call FillSummaryGrid("425-0562", NoHPerSec, "Sections", GetSubCategory("425-0562"), 0)
        TotalW = TotalW + (0.15 * NoHPerSec)
    End If
    
    GetInsideHinges_T45 = TotalW

    Exit Function

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, lstrErrDescr & vbCrLf & Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Function
Function GetLinHingeWgt_T45(ByVal Ind As Integer)   'chose weight for linear HW.
    
    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".GetLinHingeWgt_T45()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""

    If HWType = 2 Then
        If varDoorData.gblnHingeUpgrade = False Then
            GetLinHingeWgt_T45 = Choose(Ind, 0.56, 0.56, 0.59, 0.59, 0.62, 0.66, 0.787, 0.787, 0.829, 0.829, 0.871, 0.871)
        Else
            GetLinHingeWgt_T45 = Choose(Ind, 0.56, 0.56, 0.703, 0.703, 0.745, 0.745, 0.787, 0.787, 0.829, 0.829, 0.871, 0.871)
        End If
    ElseIf HWType = 3 Then
            GetLinHingeWgt_T45 = Choose(Ind, 0.703, 0.703, 0.745, 0.745, 0.787, 0.787, 0.829, 0.829, 0.871, 0.871)
        
    'currently super 3" is not available for T45
'    ElseIf HWType = 3.25 Then
'        GetLinHingeWgt = Choose(Ind, 0.43, 0.47, 0.47, 0.5, 0.53, 0.56, 0.59, 0.62, 0.62, 0.82, 0.88, 0.9, 1.15, 1.15)
    End If

    Exit Function

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Function

Public Function GetTorxScrews_T45() As Single
    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".GetTorxScrews_T45()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""
    
    'At this time, the 215-314 will be used for T45 bottom retainers (1 on each end and 24" on centre).  When we receive the new T35 bottom retainer (for T35 and Pro-FP doors), we will change from the Torx screw (215-313) to the indented tek screw (3 screws per single car door; five screws per double car door) - this should be in about 1-1/2 months.

    Dim TS As Integer
    
    TS = (RoundUp(DrWidth - 4) / 24)
    If TS > 0 Then Call FillSummaryGrid("215-314", TS, "Sections", GetSubCategory("215-314"), 0)
    GetTorxScrews_T45 = TS * 0.019
    
    Exit Function

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, lstrErrDescr & vbCrLf & Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Function

Public Function GetSelfTappingScrew() As Single
    On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".GetSelfTappingScrew()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""
    
    'the fastener to attach the top seal (328-269) on T35 and T45 doors

    Dim STS As Integer
    
    STS = RoundUp(DrWidth / 24 + 1.375)
    If STS > 0 Then Call FillSummaryGrid("215-701", STS, "Sections", GetSubCategory("215-701"), 0)
    GetSelfTappingScrew = STS * 0.025
    
    Exit Function

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, lstrErrDescr & vbCrLf & Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)

End Function
Public Function GetNumInsideHingesPerWidth_T45()

On Error GoTo Error_Handler
    Const cstrErrorSource As String = cstrApplicationName & "." & cstrModuleName & ".GetNumInsideHingesPerWidth_T45()"
    Dim llngErrNo As Long
    Dim lstrErrDescr As String
    Dim lstrErrSource As String
    llngErrNo = 0
    lstrErrDescr = ""
    lstrErrSource = ""
    Dim H As Integer
    
    'Bill confirmed for 2.0.19:
    
'    If varDoorData.DblEndHinges = False Then
'        GetNumInsideHingesPerWidth_T45 = Int((DrWidth - 4) / 48)
'    Else
'        GetNumInsideHingesPerWidth_T45 = Int((DrWidth - 7) / 48)
'    End If


'What we should have for number of centre hinges with both single and double end hinges is
'
'    = INT ( ( DW – 4 ) / 36 )
'
'            Bill
'
'If we drill for centre hinges, we don’t know if the door will have single or double.  This way, we are covered.

    GetNumInsideHingesPerWidth_T45 = Int((DrWidth - 4) / 36)

    Exit Function

Error_Handler:
    llngErrNo = IIf(llngErrNo = 0, Err.Number, llngErrNo)
    lstrErrDescr = IIf(Len(lstrErrDescr) = 0, Err.Description, lstrErrDescr & vbCrLf & Err.Description)
    lstrErrSource = IIf(Len(Trim(Err.Source)) = 0, "", Err.Source & vbCrLf) & cstrErrorSource
    Call Err.Raise(llngErrNo, lstrErrSource, lstrErrDescr)
    
End Function
